Session order: `stop` (closing the tree moves the foreground), `focus-restore`, `own-window close`, `snapshot --compare`.
- **`resize` and `capture --splash` (M25 F5 L4).** `resize --width W --height H` sets the gate tree's main window's OUTER size with `SetWindowPos` (`SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE`) after the same gate, hands-off and identity checks as `show-window`; the pure `validate_resize` refuses anything outside 100..10000 (exit 2) and the result reports the requested size and the resulting rect. It exists because a drag cannot start on the invisible resize border, so the live size floor is proven through it (`min_size` makes WinForms lift a smaller request: a 400x250 request leaves 640x360). `capture --window --splash --series N` follows the splash window (title "GleepGlorp AI", an em dash, "starting", `SPLASH_WINDOW_TITLE`) instead of the main window, whose frames a plain series reports; it is series-only and refused otherwise. The splash covers frames until the main window shows (`tainted:covered_by_tree`), so two series can run at once (the splash's and a caption strip's). **Dry-run findings:** (a) WebView2 flushes `localStorage` about 5 seconds after a change but defers the last write by more than 20 seconds after a burst of changes (a rapid Light/Auto/Dark sequence), and a `stop` kill drops it, so a step that relies on a stored theme preference polls the newest `Local Storage\leveldb\*.log` mtime instead of sleeping. (b) Splash frames on a warm boot are the themed rectangle only (0.3 to 1 second, the logo has not painted).
- **The decided technique (premise 13).** `DECIDED_TECHNIQUE` is `own` (candidate 1): `front`/`focus-restore` click own window A through the gated click (so A's process holds the foreground right), then ask A's process to run `SetForegroundWindow` through the `own-window do` channel. It reached 5/5 from own window B in front, 5/5 from own window A in front, 2/2 from the operator's real foreground (each restored with `focus-restore`), and 5 more restores from the tree. The candidates after it (`switch` = `SwitchToThisWindow`, `topmost`, `minrestore`, `sfw-helper`) exist for `--technique` and `front-trial` but were not run, because the order stops at the first decided candidate. A plain `launch` opened in front in 4 of 4 trials (the operator's real foreground twice, own A, own B), so `launch --via-own` was not added. `AttachThreadInput`, `AllowSetForegroundWindow(ASFW_ANY)` and any system-setting change are never used.
- **Known limits.** `own` needs own window A shown and not covered by an operator window at the click point (otherwise the click is refused; `own-window show --topmost` for at most 60s is the escape). The key gate covers key-downs only; the one case it cannot see is an operator keypress that lands between the foreground check and the send (a few ms), which hands-off narrows by waiting for 5s of operator quiet first. A caption drag moves a real window with `SetCursorPos` steps (worked on this host). The helper trusts `GetLastInputInfo`, which does not distinguish an operator's mouse from a remote-desktop injection.
- **Findings (leg 1b, Windows 11 26200, WebView2 154, pywebview 5.4, 96 DPI, OS theme Light).** (1) `DwmSetWindowAttribute` 35/36/20 on the UI thread through `window.native.Invoke` returns HRESULT 0 and the active caption's empty middle samples EXACTLY the target in both themes (light 240,240,245 and dark 30,30,40); the splash's border (34) applied in `before_show` (which runs synchronously on the UI thread) is a 1px line on every edge of the frameless splash, exactly the target. (2) DWM picks the minimise/maximise/close glyph colour itself from the custom caption colour: white on the dark caption (contrast 16.5:1) and black on the light one (18.5:1) whether attribute 20 is 0 or 1, so the matched/mismatched distinction does not occur and no legibility fix is needed on this host. (3) Apply to first frame sampling the new caption: 8ms after the apply call started (the first frame after the 0.4ms apply), so no extra frame redraw is needed. (4) `hidden=True`, tint in `before_show`, then `show()`: 55 and 61 visible frames at 16ms (first at 16 and 9 ms after the `show()` call) were all the tint, none a default caption, and the window was in front 300ms after `show()`; `events.shown` fires at the hidden-creation flash (pywebview shows the form at opacity 0, then hides it), NOT at the later `show()`. (7) A custom text colour is not greyed when the window is inactive: the title text's lightest pixel is the text colour (240,240,240) both active and inactive. (11) A capture of an area covered by an own window is refused `covered_by_own`; with `--allow-own-cover` the covered point samples the own window's colour (240,184,112), not the test copy's, which is what a naive `CopyFromScreen` would have attributed to it; `PrintWindow` with `PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT` renders the DWM-tinted caption pixel-for-pixel like the screen capture. (9) A real Ctrl+K from `front`, a CDP-derived click on an empty page area and `keys ^k` opens `[data-testid="jump-to"]` with focus in `jump-to-input`; `keys {ESC}` closes it and focus returns to `main`.

#### UI capture tool (M31 F1 L3)

`scripts/ui_capture.py` takes before/after evidence PNGs of named UI surfaces. It starts its own scratch dev instance (a free port of 8490 or higher, a fresh data dir under `$HOME/.cache/the-one-ui-capture/<run-id>/`, a scratch cwd, `THE_ONE_DEV_MODE=true`, the Ollama library seam), seeds one provider per type plus an agent through the API with a credential it writes before start, and for each surface opens a fresh Chromium context (1440x900, `UTC`, `en-US`, `color_scheme` and `theme-preference` set, a per-context handoff mint, claude.ai aborted, the clock fixed to 2026-03-01) and writes `<surface>--light.png` / `--dark.png` plus `manifest.json` (git SHA, `git status --short`, captured/failed/skipped, pinned environment). Backends the host lacks are Playwright route stubs (Ollama models, Test results); the plan token's stamp and the sign-in URL's random state are pinned. `--list` prints the table (`SURFACES`; a `skipped` reason is printed at the end of a run), `--surfaces a,b` filters, `--keep` keeps the scratch tree, `--force` allows a non-empty `--out`.

`check_capture_safety(port, data_dir, out_dir, default_dir, env_data_dirs, repo_root)` refuses ports 8484, 8485 and below 8490, a data dir equal to, inside or containing the platformdirs default (derived by the probe's `default_data_dir()`, loaded with `importlib`) or any `THE_ONE_DATA_DIR` from the environment or the repo's `.env` (parsed as text, the app's settings are never imported), and an `--out` inside the repo. `THE_ONE_TEST_NETNS=1` re-runs the script under `unshare -rn` (with `lo` up; `THE_ONE_UI_CAPTURE_IN_NETNS` stops a loop; it fails loudly when unavailable). **Captures are never committed**: they are local flight-log evidence and the operator approves each flight's set at the HAT. Pinned by `tests/test_ui_capture_safety.py` (unit tier).

**Library surfaces (M31 F3 L01).** Eleven surfaces for the Library editors: `library-start`, `kb-new`, `kb-detail`, `kb-item-menu`, `kb-chunks`, `kb-source`, `kb-search`, `kb-status`, `kb-danger-armed`, `connector-new`, `connector-edit`. `seed_library()` writes four small text files under `<scratch>/home/library-docs` (beside the data dir, never inside it: a folder inside it is refused with 422), registers the folder on a "Capture library" knowledge base, polls `GET .../status` until four files are indexed (90 s, fails loudly with the status JSON), and creates a stub connector with a two-row channel map. The capture instance alone gets `THE_ONE_STUB_TEST_ENDPOINT=1`. Run-to-run text is masked by a MutationObserver (`MASK_JS`: indexed-at times, the scratch path's run id, search scores, the watch state, the pairing code and expiry). Search hits that tie on score can reorder between runs. `scripts/unused_selectors.py <stylesheet.css>...` is the companion developer report for CSS class tokens with no user (it matches runtime-built classes by prefix, listed in the script).

### Errors and late responses (M31 F4 L03a)

- **One reader.** `App.api.fetchJson(url, opts)` throws an `Error` whose `.message` is unchanged (string `detail`; array `detail` flattened to `loc: msg | …`; else "Server error N {statusText}: {first 500 chars}"; else "Request failed") and which also carries `.status`, `.detail` (the raw parsed `detail`) and `.body` (the raw text). New code reads errors through it; no raw `fetch` plus a per-file message extractor.
- **`App.api.fieldErrors(err, names)`** returns `{fields, rest, text}`. A leading `"body"` loc segment is dropped, then the first segment whose `String()` equals a name in `names` picks the field (exact equality, never a substring). Messages are humanised (`App.api.humanizeMessage`: "Input should be greater than or equal to N" becomes "Must be N or more", and so on; "Value error, " is stripped; unknown text passes through). Real shapes: tools `["timeout_seconds_max"]`, knowledge settings `["size_chars"]`, the overlap model validator `[]` (unmatched), string `detail` (unmatched); `tests/test_api_error_shapes.py` proves them against the handlers. A non-`fetchJson` error returns `{fields: {}, rest: [], text: err.message}`.
- **Placement rule (DD3).** A validation error that names a field shows on that field. Everything else (a request failure, an unmatched message) shows at editor level. When fields matched, the editor-level text is built from the unmatched rest only (`text`, sentences joined with a space, each ending in a period), never from `err.message`; when nothing matched it is `err.message`. The Add-folder request error stays on its path field.
- **`App.ui.util.latest()`** (`js/ui/core.js`): "latest request wins". `next()` returns an opaque token and makes earlier tokens stale; `isCurrent(token)`; `invalidate()` makes every token stale (teardown). `knowledge-search.js` keeps its own `generation` counter for now.
- **`App.ui.util.syncChildren(parent, nodes, {after})`** (`js/ui/core.js`, M31 F4 L03b): the one keyed-children reconcile. Element-only; the managed run is everything after the `after` anchor (or all children); leftovers are removed first, then a node is inserted only if it is not already at its position, so a node in place is never re-inserted (a re-insert blurs a focused descendant). Throws on a non-element, a duplicate, an anchor outside `parent` or a node before the anchor. The knowledge folders/rows modules and `provider-models.js` use it (the provider panel's `placeNodes` is gone: it moved every later row when an earlier one was removed, blurring a focused later row; the Ollama panel's `focusedRow`/`restoreFocus` workaround is KEPT because it also covers a focused row that is itself removed, which `syncChildren` cannot). `knowledge-shared.js` no longer has a copy.
- **`App.ui.section({layout: 'stack'})`** (M31 F4 L03b): opt-in flex-column rhythm (`editor-section--stack`, `row-gap: var(--space-3)`), neutralising `.section-title`/`.section-description`/`.field-group` vertical margins so the distances stay title-to-field 10px, title-to-description 6px, description-to-first-child 12px, field-to-field 12px; the default layout is unchanged. Opted in: the connector Diagnostics and Pairing sections and the knowledge Folders section, which retired three area rules (`.doctor-btn + :not([hidden])`, `.connector-pairing-result:has(...)`, `.kb-folders-list` margin). The pairing result wrapper hides itself while all its children are hidden (`syncPairingVisibility`). No opted-in section relies on inline flow (a flex column blockifies inline children). Note the knowledge Folders section also hosts the open Chunks/Source inspect sections as nested children, so an open one now sits one gap (12px) lower.

### Page header and banner (M31 F4 L04a)

`js/ui/page.js` holds `App.ui.pageHeader` and `App.ui.banner`; each owns a NEW marker class (`ui-page-header`, `ui-banner`) and keeps emitting its legacy classes (`page-header`/`page-title`, `banner`/`banner-*`), whose ownership hands over in leg 10 (DD12, no allowance row added). `pageHeader({title, headingId, intro, actions, testid, titleClassName})` renders `div.page-header.ui-page-header > div.ui-page-header-text (h1.page-title[tabindex=-1] + p.ui-page-header-intro?) + div.ui-page-header-actions?`; geometry stays the legacy `.page-header` rules (the `.area-admin` 16px 32px is an area rule). `editorShell({variant: 'page'})` composes it: the `h1` carries `editor-title` and `page-title`, the existing `.form-actions` cluster is the actions node, `subtitle` becomes the intro, and `breadcrumb` or `titleLevel: 2` throw; the default variant's DOM is pinned by a test. `inset: 'none'` on `editorShell` and `section` adds `editor-header--flush` / `editor-section--flush` (`!important`, so it beats area padding rules in the later `areas` layer). `banner({tone, text, children, role, live, className, actions, hidden, testid})`: `live` implies the role (a contradicting `role` throws) and keeps the node rendered while empty, collapsed by `[data-empty]`; the factory sets that attribute (no `:has`), and the banner never emits `action-blocked-message` (leg 04f passes it through `className`). Tests: `test_ui_page_header_contract.py`, `test_ui_banner_contract.py`.

### Code block, facts and input options (M31 F4 L04b)

`js/ui/page.js` also holds `App.ui.codeBlock({text, trim, maxHeight, label, className, testid})` -> `{el, set, refresh}` and `App.ui.facts({rows, testid})` -> `{el, set}`. `codeBlock` renders `pre.code-block.ui-code-block` (the legacy class keeps the look; DD12 handover later); `trim` defaults to false and strips blank lines only; `maxHeight` `'tall'` (24rem) / `'short'` (18rem) scrolls vertically; on overflow (measured at construction, one frame later, on `set` and on `refresh()`; no ResizeObserver) it gains `tabindex=0`, `role=region` and `aria-label` from `label`, so a scrollable block needs a `label`. `facts` renders `dl.ui-facts > div.ui-facts-row > dt + dd`, a value being text, a node or an array of nodes, with `mono`, a row `testid` and a `valueTestid`. `App.ui.section({info: {text, label}})` renders title, `infoButton`, then the eyebrow rule in `div.ui-section-title-row`. `App.ui.textInput` gained `min`, `max`, `step`, `inputMode` and `width: 'short'` (about 10ch, `ui-input--short`; ledger row 27). Tests: `test_ui_code_facts_contract.py`. No consumer moved.

**Library and Agents consumers (M31 F4 L04f).** The provider editor's plan sign-in error (`banner({tone:'error', role:'status', hidden:true})`, shown with `set`+`show`), the stale-revision warning (a `div` now, was a `p`), the external-token reveal (a banner built with `children`; never `set()`/`setChildren()` it afterwards, `hideTokenReveal` hides it and empties the field; the redundant `aria-live` on its `role=status` is dropped) and `dangerZone`'s notice (`live:'alert'`, class `action-blocked-message` passed through) are `App.ui.banner`; the seven self-configuration value blocks are `App.ui.codeBlock` (testids via `testid`, area classes via `className`). Squawk 0236: `performSave` in the provider editor calls `shell.setBusy(true)` after validation passes and restores it on an edit success and on failure; a create stays busy because the create-to-edit remount replaces the shell, and a local `saving` flag also stops the create-mode plan sign-in button (outside the shell's busy state) from posting twice.

### Control library (`App.ui`) (M25 F1)

**Control library history (moved from CLAUDE.md in M31 F5 L12; the operating rules stay in CLAUDE.md).** The editor family (`App.ui.editorShell`/`section`/`dangerZone`, M31 F1 L4) lives in `js/ui/editor.js`; `App.ui.confirm` in `js/ui/confirm.js`; the feedback and value controls (`status`, `flash`, `progress`, `chip` in `js/ui/feedback.js`, `copyButton`/`copyField` in `js/ui/copy.js`, and `App.ui.consentLink` in `js/ui/consent-link.js`, M31 F1 L5). Tool settings fields are one shared area module, `areas/shared/tool-config-fields.js` (`App.toolConfigFields`, M31 F4 L05b/c; admin and agent modes, replacing `studio/tool-override-fields.js`): the agent Tools tab reads through it, and a number override that is not whole blocks the agent Save. The provider models panel (`areas/studio/provider-models.js`, M31 F1 L6) is the first stateful pilot: `renderProviderModels` mounts once and returns a `{refresh, destroy}` controller, and refreshes reconcile rows by key instead of rebuilding, so a pull in progress, the pull select's choice and focus survive. The provider editor (`areas/studio/provider-editor.js`, M31 F1 L7) is built once per mount from the library (`editorShell`, `section`, `field`, `secretInput` in `js/ui/field.js`, `status`, `dangerZone`), with the key re-masked after a successful Save; its OAuth sign-in (three states over one replaced node, `App.ui.consentLink` with its `help` option, an `App.ui.confirm` Reset) and token-expiry line are library controls too (leg 8), so the editor and `providers-section.js` have no HTML-string sinks and no ownership-allowance rows. Disabled buttons use opaque `--btn-disabled-*` tokens (label >= 3:1, M31 F4 L04c), and `tests/test_ui_emitted_classes.py` fails on any class a `js/ui` factory emits that no catalogue row owns or `EMITTED_EXEMPT` lists. **Flight 3 (M31, the Library half)** added `App.ui.disclosure` (`js/ui/disclosure.js`: a native `<details>`, `bordered` or `plain`, with `meta` and `actions` beside the summary, used by the knowledge folder panels and the Self-configuration history), `App.ui.keyValueTable` (`js/ui/key-value-table.js`: a keyed row model; the connector channel map is its first consumer) and `App.ui.clearableInput` (`js/ui/field.js`; the knowledge search field, squawk 0100), plus options on existing controls (`chip` `warning` and `dataset`, `status` `ledTestid` and `textTone`, `editorShell` `save.enabledWhenClean` and `errorRole`, `copyField` `buttonLabel`, `field` `errorTestid`, `section` `descriptionTestid`, a `more` icon, `menu` now used for the knowledge item rows). The knowledge editor is seven modules (`areas/studio/knowledge-{shared,create,inspect,status,search,folders,detail}.js` plus `knowledge-glue.js`) and the connector editor (`connector-editor.js`) is built once per mount from the library; none of those files, nor `connector-glue.js`, `mcp-glue.js` or `library-pane.js`, has an HTML-string sink or an ownership-allowance row (historical, at the close of Flight 3: 135 rows / 315 uses and 37 sinks, from 187 / 409 and 46 at the start of that flight; current totals are in the Flight 4 sentence below). **Flight 4 (M31, the Settings half)** added `App.ui.pageHeader`, `App.ui.banner` (with a persistent `live` mode), `App.ui.codeBlock` (`trim`, `maxHeight`, a scrollable region named by `label`) and `App.ui.facts` (a subgrid so values share a left edge) in `js/ui/page.js`, `App.ui.toolTile` and `toolTile.grid()` in `js/ui/tile.js`, the `editorShell` `variant: 'page'` (composing `pageHeader`) with `inset: 'none'`, `testid`, `subtitleTestid`, `errorRole` and its controller's `errorEl`, `section` `layout: 'stack'`, `inset: 'none'`, `info` and `descriptionTestid`, `textInput` `width: 'short'`, `min`/`max`/`step`/`inputMode`, the inline `searchList` (`inline`, `keepScroll`, `describedBy`, `scrollActive`, item `selected`, `setActive(id, {scroll})`), `radioGroup` `slotIndent`, `chipList` middle ellipsis (`App.ui.util.middleEllipsis`, `data-value`), `App.ui.util.syncChildren` and `latest`, and opaque disabled-button tokens; `App.tabs` and `js/tabs.js` are deleted. Every Settings file is at zero HTML-string sinks and zero ownership rows; `tools-form-helpers.js` kept only the string-built chip-list rows (the MCP editor) until M31 F5 L06 deleted it with its `studio.css` chip-list rules, and `studio.css` holds the legacy switch rules moved from `admin.css`, now Settings-only (the page-layout classes it keeps are declared in the catalogue's "Declared, not controls" section). The tool editor shows one field per `config_fields` entry through the shared field module (booleans as checkboxes, plain preset labels, hidden settings that do not apply to the tool, info buttons as toggletips), and the Test route returns an additive `outcome`. Whole-app totals at the close of Flight 4: 66 ownership rows / 167 uses and 23 HTML sinks (from 135 / 315 and 37 at the close of Flight 3); at the close of Flight 5: 25 / 36 and 5; at the close of Flight 6 (Mission 31): 0 / 0 and 0. **Errors (M31 F4 L03a):** `App.api.fetchJson` errors also carry `status`, `detail` and `body`; `App.api.fieldErrors`/`humanizeMessage` map a 422 to fields (placement rule in `the_one/agents.md` "Errors and late responses") and `App.ui.util.latest()` is the "latest request wins" helper. See `the_one/agents.md` "Control library (`App.ui`)" and "UI test apparatus".

**Chat controls (M31 F6 L04, DD4).** Six factories, built and contract-tested in leg 04 and mounted by the chat area in legs 05a-05c (the composer in 05a, the transcript's message, thinking, trace, telemetry and notice in 05c; see "Chat transcript on the library"): `App.ui.composer` (`js/ui/composer.js`), `App.ui.message` with its `attribution` and `relabel` sub-entries (`message.js`), `App.ui.thinking` (`thinking.js`), `App.ui.trace` with its `find` and `thinking` sub-entries (`trace.js`), `App.ui.telemetry` (`telemetry.js`) and `App.ui.notice` (`notice.js`), loaded after `drawer.js`. All classes are `ui-composer*`, `ui-message*`, `ui-thinking*`, `ui-trace*`, `ui-telemetry*`, `ui-notice*` in `components.css`, the old look by default with options for the approved looks (`message` `visibilityStyle: 'chip'`, `trace` `chevron: 'icon'` and `countStyle: 'dot'`, `composer.setBlocked` dimming the box, the `thinking` label and the attribution time label from the caller). No control reaches an app global (`App.markdown`, `App.time`, `App.config`, `App.perf`): the area passes rendered Nodes, ready labels and thresholds. Test ids are caller-supplied only. The streaming bubble's contract is `appendText` / `commit(fragment, consumed)` / `finalize(fragment)` / `tail()` over an explicit empty tail text node; `thinking` keeps its interval only while the node is unattached or connected (it stops once the node has been connected and then disconnected); the composer never clears its input and Send is disabled while `blocked` or `busy`. Leg 04's old-renderer parity check (`test_flow_chat_controls_parity.py`) compared each old renderer's computed styles with its control's; it was deleted at the start of leg 05c with the renderers. Its one deliberate difference stays: a tool message inside a trace is `max-width: 100%`, not `none`, so an unbreakable word wraps (ledger row 39). Cascade notes for the consumer legs are in `components.css` above the chat-controls section.

**Disabled buttons and the emitted-class guard (M31 F4 L04c).** A disabled button (`.btn-*`, `.save-btn`, `.ui-progress-cancel`) takes `--btn-disabled-fg/-bg/-border` (opaque, light and dark, mirrored in `tests/browser/design_tokens.py`), never `opacity`; the label is >= 3:1 on its fill and on bg/surface/surface-elevated, `GrayText` under forced colours, and the 0.2s save-btn opacity transition is dropped. The checkbox and toggle track keep `opacity: 0.4`. `tests/test_ui_emitted_classes.py` is a heuristic ratchet: every class literal a `js/ui/*.js` factory emits is owned by a catalogue row or listed in `EMITTED_EXEMPT` (exact both ways; its docstring lists the blind spots). A new control adds its classes to the catalogue, or exempts them with a reason, in the same change.

The shared control library lives in `static/js/ui/` (`core.js`, `button.js`, `field.js`, `list-pane.js`, `choice.js`, `feedback.js`, `menu.js`, `modal.js`, `rail.js`, `nav-groups.js`, `search-list.js`, `drawer.js`; later flights add more). It is the single place a control's DOM, ARIA wiring and keyboard behaviour are written; area modules call factories instead of hand-rolling `_el`/`_btn`/`_fieldGroup` copies.

- **Mechanism (DD1, decided in M25 F1 L2): ES5 factory functions that return native elements.** Simple controls return the native element (`button` returns an `HTMLButtonElement`, `textInput` an `<input>`); stateful composites (`field`, `listPane`) return a controller `{el, ...methods, destroy}`. The L2 spike built the list pane both as a factory and as a throwaway light-DOM custom element and judged both on six criteria in Chromium and WebKit (test ids visible to the registry guard, Playwright locators and `fill`, the runtime label resolver, no HTML-string sinks, real WebKitGTK via the probe, listener cleanup). Factories passed all six, so custom elements are not adopted and the spike element was deleted. Cleanup was non-differentiating (the element passes through `disconnectedCallback`, the factory by installing no document-level listener); the evidence table is the flight log's "Control-library mechanism" decision.
- **Export idiom (DD5).** Each file is a `'use strict'` ES5 IIFE (no `class`, `let`/`const` or arrows). The only non-function assignments to `App.ui` are the namespace lines `App.ui = App.ui || {}` / `App.ui.util = App.ui.util || {}`; every factory is exactly one line `App.ui.<name> = function (opts) {`. `tests/test_ui_library_source.py` enforces ES5, no app globals (the disk harness loads only `icons.js` before `ui/`) and the idiom.
- **`util` vs factories.** `App.ui.util` holds helpers that return no control: `el(tag, attrs, children)` (DOM builder, no HTML strings), `uid(prefix)` (`ui-<prefix>-<n>`, page-unique), `icon(name)` (an `SVGElement` parsed with `DOMParser`, `aria-hidden`, throws on an unknown name or a parse failure, unlike the retired string `App.icon`, which only warned) and `rovingFocus(container, itemSelector, opts)` (Arrow/Home/End on the container only, items queried at event time, clamps by default, never takes Enter/Space). Factories are everything a caller mounts.
- **Cleanup rule.** Orchestrators wipe containers with the inner-HTML setter and never call `destroy()`, so `destroy()` is an optimisation, never a correctness requirement. A composite installs **no persistent document-level listener** (keyboard handling lives on its own root); a transient popup attaches document listeners only while open and each handler first checks `root.isConnected`; global shortcuts belong to the shell. `tests/browser/test_ui_listener_cleanup.py` proves it with an `addEventListener` wrapper (and a deliberately leaky negative control).
- **Test-id rule (DD2).** A factory takes a caller-supplied property named exactly `testid` (own options, a nested option such as `filter`, or a per-item object) and never derives an id. Inside `ui/` the value must be a forward (`opts.testid`, `item.testid`), set with `setAttribute('data-testid', ...)`; no comment in `ui/` may contain the key followed by a colon. Call sites outside `ui/` use a literal or a `'prefix-' +` concatenation, which the registry guard extracts. See "UI test apparatus".
- **No sinks, parsed icons (DD4).** `ui/` contains no inner-HTML sink token, comments included (`find_ui_dir_sink_tokens`); labels use `textContent`; icons come from `util.icon`.
- **Controls so far.** `button({variant: primary|secondary|ghost|danger, size, label, icon, iconOnly (requires ariaLabel; adds the btn-icon class), ariaLabel, disabled, onClick, className, testid})`; `textInput({id, name, type, value, placeholder, disabled, required, autocomplete, onInput, testid})`; `field({label, labelHidden, control, hint, error, testid})` returns `{el, control, setError(msg|null), setHint(msg|null)}` (label is a direct child of `.field-group`, hint then error joined into `aria-describedby`, the error is `role="alert"`, `setError(null)` removes the wiring); `listPane({title, actions, filter, items|groups, selectedId, density, emptyText, testid})` is a navigation list (DD14): rows are `<a href>` or `<button>`, the selected row has `aria-current="page"` plus a wash and inset ring (no colour-only information), Tab enters at the selected row else the first visible one, and the filter keeps the selection id so clearing it restores the tab stop. The `core-controls` leg (M25 F1 L4) added the factories below and hardened `field` and `listPane`.
- **Core controls (M25 F1 L4).** `testid` goes on the **native** control unless stated.
  - `textarea({id, name, value, placeholder, rows (default 4), disabled, required, onInput, testid})` returns `<textarea class="field-input field-textarea">`; `select({id, name, options, value, disabled, required, onChange, testid})` returns `<select class="field-input field-select">`, `options` being `{value, label, disabled}` or groups `{label, options}` (an `<optgroup>`), labels via `textContent`; it throws on empty `options` and on a `value` not among them. Both live in `field.js`; wrap them in `field` for label, hint and error wiring.
  - `checkbox({label, id, checked, indeterminate, disabled, hint, onChange(checked), testid})` returns `{el, control, setChecked(bool), setIndeterminate(bool)}`; `toggle({label, labelHidden, id, checked, disabled, hint, onChange(checked), testid})` returns `{el, control, setChecked(bool)}` (on/off only). Both are in `choice.js`: `el` is `div.ui-checkbox` / `div.ui-toggle` holding one `<label>` that wraps the native `<input type=checkbox>` (`.checkbox-primary`; the toggle's has `role=switch`, class `ui-toggle-input`, clip-hidden, never `display:none`) plus the text, so the label text is both click target and accessible name. A toggle also holds a decorative `span.ui-toggle-track[aria-hidden]` whose `::after` is the thumb. The optional `.field-hint` is a **sibling after the label**, wired by `aria-describedby`, so the name stays clean. `setChecked` clears indeterminate (indeterminate wins visually while set, even if checked). Keyboard: Tab reaches the input, Space toggles; the focus ring is the input's own outline for the checkbox and the track's outline for the toggle. Both inputs carry `data-ui-own-label="true"`, and `field` throws `field: checkbox/toggle carry their own label` for such a control (or a wrapper containing one); a bare checkbox without the marker is accepted. Colours: toggle off = `--control-border` track, on = `--accent-fill`, thumb `--control-thumb`; motion uses the motion tokens. The legacy iOS switch (`.tool-enable-switch`) is gone (M31 F5 L07, its last user was the MCP editor's Sensitive switch).
  - `badge({text, kind, title, testid})` (in `feedback.js`) returns `<span class="badge badge--<kind>">`; `kind` is one of `App.ui.badge.KINDS` (11), omit it for the neutral badge; it throws on an unknown kind or missing text. `components.css` defines `.badge` and every `.badge--<kind>` (these classes did not exist before L4, despite the leg text assuming them). All eleven kinds pair `--badge-<kind>` with `--badge-ink-<kind>` (gated by `test_badge_ink_passes_body_aa`). The seven orphaned kinds (`approved`, `connectors`, `dismissed`, `execution`, `filesystem`, `time`, `web`) were deleted in M31 F6 L09a (D6): a producer grep over `the_one/` and `tests/` found only test and fixture producers. `tests/test_ui_badge_kinds.py` pins `KINDS` against the `.badge--*` selectors in `static/css/*.css`.
  - `emptyState({title, body, action, icon, headingLevel (default 3), titleTag (`p`: a paragraph title, M31 F1 L6), variant (`'plain'`, inline size: the title inherits the container's size, weight and ink; M31 F2 L10c), size: inline|pane, role, testid})` returns the root element: inline is `div.empty-state`, pane is `div.empty-canvas > div.empty-canvas-inner`, with an optional `.ui-empty-icon`, an `<hN class="ui-empty-title">`, an optional `<p class="ui-empty-body">` and the action (which gains `.empty-canvas-cta` in pane size, its own classes kept). `title` is required; `role` is set only when given.
  - `listPane` changes: the empty element is `emptyState({title: emptyText})` (no `role`; a persistent `.ui-list-pane-status` `role="status"` live region announces) plus `ui-list-pane-empty` and its `hidden` toggling; a `badges[]` entry `{text, kind, title}` renders through `badge` (nodes and entries without a `kind` keep the neutral `.ui-list-pane-badge` span); a row `count` renders as trailing `.ui-list-pane-count` text; on `[aria-current="page"]` rows meta, neutral badges and counts use `--fg-secondary` (gated by `test_selected_row_meta_passes_body_aa`, since `--fg-muted` fails body AA on the selected wash in three of four theme/surface cases).
  - CSS: `.checkbox-primary:indeterminate` (a dash drawn with the same gated pair as the tick) and `:disabled` (opacity .4, not-allowed) plus the `.ui-checkbox*`, `.ui-toggle*`, `.ui-empty-*` and `.badge*` blocks, all in `components.css` `@layer components`. Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_choice_contract.py`.
- **Radio group (M28 F2 L2, DD3).** `radioGroup({legend, legendHidden, name, value, options: [{value, label, description, disabled, testid}], onChange(value), testid})` (in `choice.js`) returns `{el, value(), setValue(v), setDescription(value, text), optionEl(value), input(value), focus(), destroy()}`: a `fieldset.ui-radio-group` of option cards, each a native radio (the option's test id on the input) with an optional description wired by `aria-describedby` and an empty slot from `optionEl`; one delegated `change` listener, `setValue` is silent, `.is-checked` is re-synced on every wrapper (the `:has()` fallback). Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_radio_group_contract.py`.
- **Menu (M25 F1 L5).** `menu({trigger, items, align, testid})` (in `menu.js`) is the WAI-ARIA menu button; it returns `{el, trigger, popup, open(focus 'first'|'last'), close(restoreFocus), isOpen(), destroy()}`. `trigger` is `App.ui.button` options (iconOnly + ariaLabel allowed; its `testid` lands on the trigger), `items` are `{label, onSelect, icon, danger, disabled, testid}` (the item `testid` lands on the menuitem), `align` is `'start'` (default) or `'end'` (anything else throws), and `testid` goes on the popup root. It throws on empty `items` or an item missing `label`/`onSelect`. Markup: `el` is `div.ui-menu-wrap` (`position: relative`) holding the trigger (`aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`) and the popup `div[role=menu].ui-menu[hidden]` as a **DOM descendant** (no portal; it always opens downward, with no edge flipping - a known limitation, a later flight may add it). The popup is labelled by the trigger (`aria-labelledby`), or by `aria-label` for an icon-only trigger. Items are `button[type=button][role=menuitem][tabindex=-1]`; disabled items use `aria-disabled="true"` (never the `disabled` attribute, so they stay readable and focus-reachable programmatically); danger items use `--status-danger`.
  - **Keyboard contract.** Click, Enter, Space or ArrowDown on the trigger opens and focuses the first enabled item (mouse opens too, so focus is always inside the open popup); ArrowUp opens on the last. ArrowUp/Down wrap and Home/End jump, through `util.rovingFocus` on the popup with the selector `[role=menuitem]:not([aria-disabled="true"])` (the menu resets every item to `tabindex="-1"` afterwards). Enter/Space/click on an enabled item closes, restores focus to the trigger, **then** calls `onSelect(item)` (a throwing `onSelect` leaves the menu closed and focus restored, and the error propagates); on a disabled item it does nothing. With every item disabled the popup itself (`tabindex=-1`) takes focus so Escape still works; a disabled trigger never opens. Escape, Tab and `focusout` are handled on `el` (so they work wherever focus is inside the control): Escape while open does `preventDefault` + `stopPropagation`, closes and focuses the trigger (Escape while closed is not handled, so it reaches an enclosing dialog); Tab/Shift+Tab while open closes and focuses the trigger **without** `preventDefault`, so default Tab navigation continues from it; focus moving to a real element outside `el` closes without restoring (a `null` relatedTarget is left to the pointer handler, because WebKit does not focus a pressed button). A `mousedown` on the popup is `preventDefault`ed to keep focus put.
  - **Outside click and cleanup (DD1).** While open, exactly one capture-phase `pointerdown` listener sits on `document`; it is added on open and removed on close, so a closed menu has zero document listeners. Its handler first checks `el.isConnected`: a menu whose host was wiped removes the listener, clears its module-scope flags and touches nothing else. One menu is open at a time (a module-scope reference closes the previous one on open); `close` on a detached menu skips all DOM writes; `destroy()` while open closes it and clears the reference. Proven in `test_ui_listener_cleanup.py`.
  - **Dialog safety (DD8).** Inside a native `<dialog>` (the Leg 2 `_dialog_rule.js` harness) Escape closes only the menu, and the dialog's own Escape handler never sees it; a second Escape closes the dialog; an outside click inside the dialog closes the menu only. Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_menu_contract.py`. Typeahead, submenus and checkable items are out of scope.
- **Modal (M25 F2 L2, DD6).** `modal({title, body, actions, dismissible, cancelValue, onClose, id, testid})` (in `modal.js`) returns `{el, open(opener), close(result), isOpen()}`; `el` is a getter (the `<dialog class="modal-overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">` exists only while open, `null` otherwise). `title` is an `<h2 class="modal-dialog-title">`; `body` is text (a `<p>`) or a Node (wrapped in a `div`), both `.modal-dialog-message` and the `aria-describedby` target; `actions` are `{label, variant, value, testid, autofocus}` built with `App.ui.button` inside `.modal-actions.form-actions`; the card `.modal-dialog` has `tabindex="-1"` so a click on non-focusable text keeps focus inside and Escape still reaches the dialog. An action click, Escape, a dismissing backdrop click, an unrouted close and `close(result)` all end in one `finish` that reports `onClose(value)` once (actions report their `value`, the others `cancelValue`, default `null`). CSS: `.modal-overlay` is the full-viewport `<dialog>` (UA defaults reset; `display:flex` only on `[open]`), `.modal-dialog` the visible card, `::backdrop` coloured by `--scrim`. **`App.confirmDialog`** keeps its signature and test ids (`confirm-dialog-overlay` on the `<dialog>`, `modal-cancel-btn`, `modal-confirm-btn`), with Stay `autofocus`.
- **Rail (M25 F2 L6, DD7).** `rail({items, currentId, brand, label})` (in `rail.js`) returns `{el, setCurrent(id), setHref(id, href), destroy()}`. `items` are `{id, label, icon (an `App.icons` key), href, testid}`; `brand` is `{src, alt}` (a 24px `<img class="ui-rail-brand">` named by `alt`); `label` is the landmark name (default `"Main"`). `el` is `<nav class="ui-rail" aria-label="Main">`; each item is a plain `<a class="ui-rail-item" href>` holding an 18px `util.icon` and a `--font-size-tiny` label (the link's accessible name is the label alone). **No click handler, and the factory never reads `App.router`**: navigation, the leave guard, modifier-click passthrough and restore ride on `App.nav`'s link interceptor, and the shell feeds hrefs in through `setHref`. Keyboard: `util.rovingFocus` on the `<nav>` (ArrowUp/Down, Home/End, clamped) with a single tab stop; the factory initialises `tabindex` itself (current item 0, the rest -1, the first item when none is current) and `setCurrent` re-asserts it (`rovingFocus` only rewrites `tabindex` on a keydown). It may render with no `aria-current` before the router's first commit. `setCurrent(unknown|null)` clears the marker; `setHref(unknown)` and invalid `items` throw. Selected marker is DD14's wash plus an inset 1px `--accent` ring (`box-shadow`, so the 60 x 53 box never moves); hover is `--surface-elevated`; focus is the pinned `outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 2px`. The 1px right border is paid for by `padding-right: 1px` so the 60px items sit on whole pixels (x 5..65). Pairs gated by `test_rail_label_pairs_pass_body_aa`. **The `footer` slot (M25 F4 L7, DD9, DD14):** `footer` is an array of Nodes (it throws on a non-array or a non-Node item; an empty array is no footer), rendered last in the `<nav>` as `<div class="ui-rail-footer">` pinned to the bottom by `margin-top: auto` (the rail's 12px bottom padding below it). The caller builds the nodes, handlers included: the factory binds nothing on them (its only listener stays the roving `keydown` on the `<nav>`), and `setCurrent`/`setHref` never touch them. They are not `.ui-rail-item`, so each focusable one is its **own Tab stop**, outside the items' roving arrows (an arrow key on a footer button does nothing; the items' arrows clamp before it). An icon-only button there (`.btn-icon`) is the reference spec's 44px square with the 16px icon. The shell's footer is the Jump to control ("Jump-to palette" below). Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_rail_contract.py` (both engines, plus the global `.visually-hidden` utility in a harness that loads only `tokens.css` and `components.css`); the app-level tests are `tests/browser/test_shell_rail.py`.
- **List pane additions (M25 F3 L1, DD10).** All opt-in; existing callers are unchanged except the title row, filter, scroller and group label, which now follow reference spec sections 3 and 6.1 for every pane (title row `14px 16px 10px` with an 8px gap, the h2 at `--font-size-h2`/600/`--fg`, actions pushed right with `margin-left: auto`; filter `0 16px 8px`; scroller `0 8px 12px`; group label `12px 10px 4px`, 11px, 500, `--fg-muted`). New options: `count: true` (a `.ui-list-pane-total` span after the h2 reading "{shown} of {total}" across all groups, recomputed by every filter keystroke and `setItems`, empty while there are no items); `intro` (a `<p class="ui-list-pane-intro">` under the title row, 11px `--fg-muted`); per-group `emptyText` in `groups[{label, emptyText, items}]` (a group with **no items** keeps its caption and shows a `<p class="ui-list-pane-group-empty">` line, never a row, so it is outside the roving set; it hides while a filter query is active; a group whose rows are all filtered out hides as before; when every group is empty the lines show instead of the pane's empty state); `rowStyle: 'agents'|'library'` (A8: `7px 10px` rows with no list gap, about 48px, or `8px 10px` with a 2px gap, about 50px; 13px names, a 10px row gap, a 1px text gap, and the section 3 border selection model: `border: 1px solid transparent` at rest, `--accent-translucent` plus `border-color: var(--accent)` when selected, so selecting never shifts layout; Library rows ink `--fg-secondary` so the icon follows it while the name stays `--fg`; `rowStyle` padding wins over `density: 'compact'`; an unknown value throws; without it rows keep the inset-ring selection). Per-item `nameSuffix` appends to the row's accessible name through a `.visually-hidden` span after the label (never `aria-label`, which would replace the visible name for voice control); the span is blockified, so the accessible-name algorithm may put a space before it, and callers pass their own separators: a leading one, plus a trailing one when meta follows (`", Claude Max,"`, so the name reads "label, Claude Max, meta"; the flight-end review). Controller additions: `scrollToSelected()` (`scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest'})` on the selected row; a no-op with no selection or when the filter hides it; verified in WebKit too) and `getItems()` (the current items in display order, for tests). **`setItems` keeps state across its full rebuild**: the filter query (re-run, count updated), the body's `scrollTop`, and focus. Before clearing the body it looks up the entry whose row is or contains `document.activeElement` (through `entries[]`; no data attribute); after `applyView()` it sets the tab stop explicitly (the row with that id if it is still visible, else the selected row, else the first visible row, else the filter input gets focus) and focuses it **synchronously**, because removing the focused node fires `focusout` at once and any deferral would drop focus to `<body>`. Focus outside the body is never touched. This is what lets a store notification call `setItems` while the user roves. **Leg 3 additions:** per-item `lead` (a caller-built node placed directly before the text, after an icon, in a `.ui-list-pane-lead` wrapper with `flex: none`; it is not part of the name, so a decorative lead is `aria-hidden` by its caller; a non-node lead throws) and `emptyText` as a **function** of the trimmed filter query (`''` when no query is active), re-derived for the empty state's title and the filter status on every view change, so a pane can say `No agents match "{q}".`. The pane and every row are `position: relative`: the status region and the rows' `nameSuffix` spans are `.visually-hidden` (absolute), and without a positioned ancestor a long list's hidden spans escaped the scroller's clipping and stretched the DOCUMENT's scroll height (so `scrollIntoView` could scroll the viewport). `.badge.badge-dot` (with `.badge-dot--none`, a hollow neutral ring) is the text-less 8px disc of a badge kind, the provider dot an Agents row passes as its `lead`. **Leg 4a additions:** an optional `group.id` (`groups[{id, label, emptyText, items}]`; validated unique in `normalise`, a duplicate throws `listPane duplicate group id {id}` before any DOM change) and `scrollToGroup(id)`, which brings that group into view within the BODY scroller only (its top meets the body's top, by adjusting `body.scrollTop`, never `scrollIntoView`, so no ancestor scrolls; a no-op when the group is already wholly visible, for an unknown or missing id, or when the group is hidden by the filter). Contract-tested in both engines; the Library-style demo calls it. 11px muted pairs gated by `test_list_pane_caption_pairs_pass_body_aa`; forced colours give both selection models a `Highlight` border (a rowStyle row's rest border is forced to `Canvas` and its selected border to `Highlight` at the rowStyle rule's specificity: before the flight-end review the transparent rest border was forced to the row's text colour and the rowStyle selected rule outranked the forced one, so every Agents and Library row looked alike; `test_list_pane_forced_colors_cover_both_selection_models` now requires that no other row shows the selected border, falsified). **Selected-row `aria-current` follows the row kind** (the flight-end review): `"page"` on a link row (`href`: it opens a page), `"true"` on a button row (`onSelect`: it selects in place); every selected-row rule (the wash and ring, the rowStyle border, the `--fg-secondary` meta/badge/count inks and the forced-colours border) lists both values (`test_list_pane_aria_current_value_follows_the_row_kind`, both engines). Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_library_contract.py`.
- **Nav groups (M25 F3 L1, DD6/DD10).** `navGroups({label, heading, groups, selectedId, testid})` (in `nav-groups.js`) returns `{el, setSelected(id), destroy()}`: `<nav class="ui-nav-groups" aria-label="{label}">` holding an `<h2>` (`heading`, default `label`), then per group a caption `<div class="ui-nav-groups-caption">` and a `<ul aria-labelledby>` of `<li><a class="ui-nav-groups-item" href>`. `groups` are `[{label, items: [{id, label, href, testid}]}]`; a missing label, id or `href`, or a duplicate id (across groups), throws. `aria-current="page"` is on exactly the selected item (`setSelected(unknown|null)` clears it). **No click handler, no keyboard handling and no tabindex**: plain links in Tab order, so the router and `App.nav`'s interceptor own navigation; `destroy()` only drops the node map (nothing is bound). Geometry (reference spec section 8.1): items `display: block; padding: 6px 10px`, 12px type with a pinned 16px line-height (28px with any font), `--radius-sm`, `--fg-secondary` at rest, `--surface-elevated` plus `--fg` on hover, an inset `outline-offset: -2px` focus ring (never clipped by the aside); selected is the wash plus an inset 1px accent ring and `--fg`; captions share the section 3 group label with the list pane; the heading is 16px/600/`--fg` with `padding-left: 10px`, so its text lines up with the items' text (A3). The 2px flex gaps reproduce the board's pitch. The **host** supplies the column padding (`14px 10px`) and width (`--settings-nav-w`). Forced colours: a `Highlight` border on the selected item with 1px less padding (still 28px). Pairs gated by `test_nav_groups_pairs_pass_body_aa`. Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_nav_groups_contract.py`. Since leg 5a it is the Settings nav (see "Settings nav and pages (M25 F3 L5a)").
- **Search list (M25 F4 L1, DD14, A10).** `searchList({label (required; a visually hidden label, also the listbox's name), placeholder, source(query) (required), emptyText(query), onPick(item), testid, inputTestid, rowTestid})` (in `search-list.js`) returns `{el, input, refresh(), getActive(), setActive(id), destroy()}`: a combobox input over a grouped listbox, the agent picker's and the jump-to palette's list. `source` is called synchronously with the input's raw value on every `input` event and on `refresh()` and returns `[{id, label, items: [{id, label, description, meta, metaMono, lead, testid, dataset}]}]`; a group with no items is dropped, a group with no label renders its options with no caption; items are validated before the DOM changes. **ARIA:** the `<input>` is `role="combobox"` with `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-expanded="true"`, `aria-controls` (the `div role="listbox"`, named by `label`) and `aria-activedescendant` (removed when there is no option); a labelled group is `role="group"` `aria-labelledby` its visible `role="presentation"` caption; rows are `role="option"` with a generated id each (item ids may repeat across groups) and only the active one carries `aria-selected="true"`; rows are never focusable, and a `mousedown` on the listbox is prevented, so focus stays in the input. **Keys:** ArrowDown/ArrowUp move the active option across groups, clamped (no wrap); Enter picks it (`preventDefault`; nothing with no options); Home/End are NOT intercepted (they move the caret, per the APG combobox; a deliberate narrowing of the flight's DD10 wording); Escape is NOT handled (the containing modal owns it); keys during IME composition (`isComposing` or `keyCode` 229) are ignored. After every input event (a changed query) the first option is active; a `refresh()` with an unchanged query keeps the active option by `(group index, id)` while it exists (M31 F6 L03b, D4: scrolled into view with `nearest`; the first option when it is gone). A `pointermove` over a row activates it and a click picks it. Keyboard and source activation scroll the active option into view inside the listbox only (manual `scrollTop`, never `scrollIntoView`, which would scroll ancestors; a group's first option brings its caption along); pointer activation never scrolls. With no options the listbox is hidden, an `App.ui.emptyState` shows `emptyText(query)` (default "No matches"), and a persistent visually hidden `role="status"` region carries the same text. **Styling** (reference spec 5.6's picker row): `10px 12px`, a 12px gap, `--radius-sm`, a 1px transparent rest border; the `lead` in an `aria-hidden` wrapper, the 600-weight `--fg` label, the 11px `--fg-secondary` description (ellipsis), the right-aligned 11px meta (`--fg-muted`, mono with `metaMono`). The active row takes the `--accent-translucent-hover` wash and an `--accent` border; its meta takes `--fg-secondary`, because `--fg-muted` fails body AA on that wash (3.85:1 dark, 3.92:1 light over `--surface`; gated by `test_search_list_pairs_pass_body_aa`, with the forced-colors Canvas/Highlight border). The listbox is the scroller (`flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto`): the host bounds its height. Every listener sits on the input or the listbox, none on `document`/`window`; `destroy()` removes them and clears the options and `aria-activedescendant`. Contracts: `tests/browser/test_ui_search_list_contract.py` (both engines) and a case in `test_ui_listener_cleanup.py`.
- **List controls: inline search list, flush tabs, indented radio slot (M31 F4 L04d, DD4).** `searchList({inline: true, keepScroll, describedBy, scrollActive})` is the embedded variant (root also `.ui-search-list--inline`: listbox capped at 252px, mono description, a 16px accent lead, no active-row paint while the list is unfocused, with a forced-colours Canvas twin). The keep-by-identity rule is now every list's (M31 F6 L03b); `keepScroll` only says to restore the scroll offset as it was and not to scroll to the kept row (without it the kept row is scrolled into view with `nearest`); a changed query resets to the top and the first option. `scrollActive` (`'center'` default, `'nearest'`, `'none'`) governs programmatic activation; `setActive(id, {scroll})` overrides per call; arrow keys always use `nearest`, and the modal default (no `inline`) is unchanged. An inline item's `selected: true|false` is built inside the variant (the caller's `lead` is ignored for it): an aria-hidden lead with a check icon, an empty 16px spacer on the other rows, and a visually hidden " (selected)" in the option's label, so its accessible name carries it. `describedBy` (an id or a node) sets `aria-describedby` on the input for any list. `tabs({panelPadding: 'none'})` adds `.ui-tabs--flush` to the tablist and panels (no padding, visible overflow). `radioGroup({slotIndent: 'label'})` adds `.ui-radio-option--indent` (a 16px radio box, the slot indented by the box plus the 8px gap, so its left edge is the label text's). The `admin.css` overrides of these (Date and time, `.settings-list`) stay until the legs that consume them (06, 09), a recorded DD11 duplication. Contracts in `test_ui_search_list_contract.py`, `test_ui_tabs_contract.py`, `test_ui_radio_group_contract.py`.
- **Tool tile and long chips (M31 F4 L04e, DD4/DD12).** `App.ui.toolTile({key, name, href, selected, badge, description, toggle: {label, checked, disabled, onChange, testid, dataset}, testid, dataset})` (`js/ui/tile.js`) is the Built-in tools card: `div.ui-tool-tile[role=listitem]` holding a row (`a.ui-tool-tile-link`, `aria-current="page"` when selected, then an `App.ui.badge`), a two-line-clamped description and an in-flow `App.ui.toggle` (end-aligned; its accessible name is the caller's `toggle.label`, the testids are the caller's). It returns `{el, key, link, toggle, setSelected, setChecked, setDisabled, setDescription}` (setters silent). `toolTile.grid({label, testid})` is a `role=list` container whose `sync(tiles)` reconciles through `syncChildren` and refocuses a focused element if a move blurred it (neither Chromium nor WebKit blurs on a move; the refocus is a safety net). Until leg 06's handover the tile also emits the legacy `entity-tile`, `tool-tile`, `tile-title-row`, `entity-tile-name`, `tool-tile-link`, `entity-tile-description`, `entity-tile-grid` and `active` (the guard's `EMITTED_EXEMPT`), so the `admin.css` tile rules style it; the library CSS under the marker classes gives the same look standalone (the contract compares both, with and without the legacy names, against `LEGACY_ADMIN_TILE_CSS`; bottom padding and height are excluded, since the in-flow toggle replaces the corner switch). `chipList` chips now carry the full value in `data-value` and `title`, show `span.ui-chip-text` middle-ellipsized to `chipList.BUDGET` (40) characters by `App.ui.util.middleEllipsis` (a truncated text is `aria-hidden` with a `.visually-hidden` full twin), and read values from `data-value` (`chipList.valuesOf(el)` is the static reader). The string-built chip lists are untouched (Flight 5).
- **Modal chooser options (M25 F4 L1, DD14).** All optional; with none set a modal is built exactly as before (24px padding, `aria-describedby`, centred; pinned by `test_without_the_new_options_a_modal_is_unchanged`). `width` (px; an inline card width with `max-width: 100%` of the overlay's content box, i.e. the viewport minus 32px) and `top` (px; inline `align-self: flex-start` and `margin-top: top - 16` on the card, the overlay's 16px padding netted out, and `max-height: calc(100vh - top - 16px)`), so only the opted-in card changes. `initialFocus` (a Node inside the card, focused on `open()`). `closeButton: {label, ariaLabel, testid}` (an `App.ui.button` ghost/sm at the end of a `.modal-dialog-header` flex row; it closes with `cancelValue`, takes part in the Tab wrap, and focus returns to the opener like any close; the picker's is "Esc" named "Close (Esc)", A11). `footer` (a Node in `.modal-dialog-footer`, after the body; a DocumentFragment makes its items the row's flex children and is copied per `open()`; `footerEnd` (M31 F6 L03b: a Node or a string, placed last in the footer inside `span.modal-dialog-footer-end` and pushed to its end; the row is created when only `footerEnd` is given; any layout) replaces building that class by hand). `layout: 'chooser'` (`.modal-dialog-chooser`: zero card padding and gap, `overflow: hidden`; the header padded `16px 20px 12px`; the body, which must be a Node, full-bleed in a flexing `.modal-dialog-body`; the footer the 11px `--fg-muted` row over a hairline; **no `aria-describedby`**, so a screen reader does not read a whole list on open; actions throw; the title stays the `<h2>` the dialog's `aria-labelledby` references). **Focus on `open()`:** `initialFocus` when connected inside the card; else, when a close button or the chooser layout is in play, the first focusable in the BODY (never the header's close button); else the usual order (autofocus action, first action, first focusable other than the close button, the close button, the card). The backdrop keeps `--scrim` (no token change; A30 resolved in Flight 1). Contracts: the chooser tests at the end of `tests/browser/test_ui_modal_contract.py`.
- **Modal `ariaLabel` and `anyOpen()` (M25 F4 L7, DD14).** `ariaLabel` (a non-empty string) names a modal with **no visible title**, the jump-to palette's case: the `<dialog>` gets `aria-label` and **no** `aria-labelledby`, and the card has **no `<h2>` and no `.modal-dialog-header` row** at all (the body is its first child), in either layout; a non-chooser keeps its `aria-describedby`. It throws with a `title` ("pass one, not both"), with a `closeButton` (there is no header to hold one) and when it is not a non-empty string. The caller's BODY owns any close button and calls `controller.close()` (it reports `onClose(undefined)`); it is in the Tab wrap like any focusable in the card. **`App.ui.modal.anyOpen()`** returns `stack.length > 0` over the module-level stack every controller shares, so it is true while ANY modal is open, `App.confirmDialog`'s included (the adapter is built on this factory, so there is no second source), and false once the stack empties, including after a `finish` from a bypassed `close` event or a throwing `onClose` (`finish` leaves the stack before `onClose`). It is the shell's Ctrl K rule ("Jump-to palette" below). Not an `App.ui.<name> = function (opts)` export line, so the export-idiom guard is unchanged. Demo: the modal section's "Open labelled chooser" (an `ariaLabel` chooser with the palette's bar and body classes and its own Esc; a caption shows `anyOpen()`). Contracts: the `ariaLabel` and `anyOpen` tests at the end of `test_ui_modal_contract.py` (both engines).
- **List pane additions (M25 F4 L1, DD5, DD12, DD14).** All opt-in. `block`: a node inserted directly after the `<header>` (outside it, in `.ui-list-pane-block`, `padding: 0 16px 12px`, a flex column), so the header's children are unchanged. `rowStyle: 'chats'`: a two-line row (`flex-direction: column`, `8px 10px`, 2px between lines): line 1 is the 13px `--fg` label (ellipsis) with the item's `time` (11px `--fg-muted`, tabular, no shrink) pushed to the end; line 2 is the 11px `--fg-secondary` meta, then the badges; `icon`, `lead` and `count` are not drawn and `time` is drawn only in this style. On a selected row the time takes `--fg-secondary` (the A24 resolution: `--fg-muted` fails on the selected wash), gated by `test_chats_row_pairs_pass_body_aa`. `item.dataset` (a plain object; anything else throws) sets `data-*` on the row; the label span always carries `data-confirm-title`. **Row actions:** `item.action = {label, icon, testid, onClick(item, actionEl, li)}` (all but `testid` required) renders `<button type="button" class="ui-list-pane-action" aria-label="{label}">` (icon only, never a `.ui-list-pane-row`) after the row inside its `<li>` (`li.ui-list-pane-has-action`), absolutely placed over the row's right end; `display: none` until `li:hover` or `li:focus-within` (never visibility/opacity, which would keep a hidden action a Tab stop), the time or count under it fading to `opacity: 0` (still in the accessible name), and the row keeping its hover wash while the pointer is on the action. `onClick` reads the entry's current item at click time (the row's `onSelect` does too). **Tab policy:** only the roving stop row's action is `tabindex="0"` (every other `-1`); `setStop` re-derives the actions, and so does a body `focusin` (rovingFocus rewrites only the rows' tabindex), so Tab goes stop row, its action, out of the list. Shift+Tab from after the list lands on the stop ROW (its action is hidden until the li has focus; the legacy chat list behaved the same). On an action, ArrowUp/ArrowDown go to the previous/next visible row (clamped) and Home/End to the first/last; `rovingFocus` already ignores a non-row target. **Focus across `setItems`:** `focusedEntry` matches `entry.li.contains(active)` and records whether the action had focus; a focused action goes back to the same item's action (shown for the `focus()` call by a transient `.ui-list-pane-reveal` class, since `display: none` cannot take focus), else to the neighbouring row (the next old item still visible, else the previous) as the new stop; a focused ROW keeps Flight 3's rule (same id, else selected, else first). **Armed rows:** `App.ui.confirm(li)` arms with `.confirm-delete` on the `<li>` and swaps its `[data-confirm-title]` text; `setItems` reuses an armed `<li>` (row and action nodes, so the armed title and the revert timer survive) for the same item id, with `entry.item` replaced by the new item and the reused row still going through `applyView` (its label may be stale for the 3s window); an armed item absent from the new list has its `<li>` dropped, and `ui/confirm.js`'s lapse timer then runs harmlessly on the detached node (pinned); after it disarms, the next `setItems` rebuilds it. Armed CSS: the danger border/ring and title ink, the action shown in `--status-danger`. The body's `focusin` and `keydown` listeners are removed by `destroy()`. Contracts: `tests/browser/test_ui_list_pane_chats_contract.py` (both engines); every Flight 3 `listPane` contract stays green. `setQuery` came in Flight 4 leg 6 (below).
- **Drawer (M25 F4 L6, DD7, DD14).** `drawer({label (required; the `<aside>`'s `aria-label`), heading (text, set as an `h2.ui-drawer-title` with `textContent`, or a node placed as given), body, footer (nodes), el (an existing `<aside>` to fill: its children are replaced, its id kept; the chat area passes the static `#chat-drawer` shell slot), testid, onOpenChange(open)})` (in `drawer.js`) returns `{el, open(opener), close(), toggle(opener), isOpen(), setMode('push'|'overlay'), destroy()}`: a **non-modal** `<aside>` (a complementary landmark) of a 48px header (`.ui-drawer-header`, a hairline under it, A21), a body that alone scrolls (`.ui-drawer-body`, 16px padding) and an optional footer (`.ui-drawer-footer`, a hairline over it). Built closed; shown and hidden with `hidden`, never removed (a toggle's `aria-controls` must always resolve), and `.ui-drawer[hidden]` / `-header[hidden]` / `-footer[hidden]` are re-asserted in the components layer (a base-layer `[hidden]` rule loses to any components-layer `display`). `open()` never moves focus; `open(opener)` records the opener (no argument keeps the last one); `close()` returns focus to it only when focus is inside the drawer at that moment (a close from elsewhere never steals focus; a close from inside never drops it to `<body>`). **Escape** while focus is inside closes it (`preventDefault` and `stopPropagation`; ignored while an IME composes), so focus returns to the opener (Flight 1 DD8). `onOpenChange` fires after every change of the open state however it was made. **Modes:** `.ui-drawer--push` (and `data-mode`) is a `--drawer-w` (320px, border-box) flex sibling on `--surface` with a 1px `--border` left edge that never shrinks; `.ui-drawer--overlay` is `position: absolute; top: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; z-index: 10` over its nearest positioned ancestor with `--shadow-modal`, no scrim and no outside-click close. One `keydown` listener on the `<aside>`, none on the document or window (`test_ui_listener_cleanup.py`); `destroy()` unbinds it, empties and hides the drawer and leaves the element in place (the caller owns placement). Tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_drawer_contract.py` (contracts lane, both engines). Its consumer is the chat area's agent drawer ("Agent drawer" below).
- **List pane `setQuery` (M25 F4 L6, DD14).** `setQuery(q)` sets the filter's value and query and applies the view (rows shown or hidden, the selection, the tab stop and the count re-derived) **without rebuilding a row** (so focus survives) and **silently**: no live announcement (the polite status region is cleared, never written, so a stale "N results" does not linger). A no-op without a filter. `App.studio.clearAgentFilter()` calls it with `''` ("Agent drawer" below). Tests: `test_list_pane_set_query_*` in `test_ui_library_contract.py`.
- **Secret input and the editor handles (M31 F1 L7).** `secretInput({value, label, id, placeholder, autocomplete (default `new-password`), dataset, testid, revealTestid})` (in `field.js`) returns `{el, input, button, setRevealed(bool), setValue(text)}`: `el` is `div.api-key-wrap` holding the `input.field-input[type=password]` and an icon-only `button.api-key-eye` (`eye` / `eye-off`, names "Show password" / "Hide password", `aria-pressed`, focus stays on it). The value is only ever set through `.value`; `dataset` lands on the input, never the wrapper; reveal flips only `type`; `setValue` also hides. Put it in a `field` with `control: secret.input, host: secret.el`. `field` gained `host` (an element containing the control, placed in the control's slot while the label, `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid` stay on the control), `hintTestid` (kept across `setHint` calls) and `setLabel(text)`; `dangerZone`'s `action` gained `beforeArm(proceed)`, which gates only the first click (the armed second click commits). Contract tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_secret_input_contract.py`.
- **Catalogue and inventory (M25 F1 L6, DD10/DD11; reshaped by M31 F1 L1, DD7).** `docs/ui-control-inventory.md` classifies every control kind in the app as `library: App.ui.<factory>` (owner, known a11y/keyboard debt; a removed control moves to a "Retired controls" table, and `test_no_control_row_is_not_yet_defined` fails on any other status), and ends with a "Tokens and type" section (names and roles; the values live in `css/tokens.css` and `css/fonts.css`). It is the library's reference together with each factory's contract tests. The dev-mode `#settings/design-language` page that used to show every factory is deleted, with its `[data-ui-demo]` roots, the `devOnly` Settings-page mechanism and the `ui-demo-*` testids. Guards: `tests/test_ui_control_inventory.py` (unit: factory exports and inventory citations must match both ways; every table row's status cell is classified, reading only the control tables, up to the "Tokens and type" heading; every factory is called as `App.ui.<name>(` in at least one `tests/browser/test_ui_*.py`). The WebKit pass over a page is `scripts/webkitgtk_probe.py`.
- **Adding a factory (mandatory checklist).** (1) Create `static/js/ui/<name>.js` following the idiom (`App.ui.<name> = function (opts) {` on one line); add its `<script>` tag to `index.html` after `icons.js` and before `markdown.js` and bump the `/static` ref count in `tests/test_index_asset_cache_busting.py` in the same step (the disk harness loads only the `ui/` scripts `index.html` lists). (2) CSS goes in `components.css` inside `@layer components` using existing tokens. (3) Contract tests: engine-parametrized browser tests (Chromium and WebKit) with the shared resolver, as in `tests/browser/test_ui_*_contract.py`. (4) An inventory row in `docs/ui-control-inventory.md` citing `App.ui.<name>`. Steps 3-4 are enforced by the guards above (a contract test calling `App.ui.<name>(`, and the row); a factory without them fails `make test-unit`.
- **Four patterns from the Flight 5 HAT (squawk 0272).** (1) **Flash to the page, not the editor.** An action that closes or remounts the item (a delete, a create that opens the new item) flashes through `App.ui.flash`, whose toast always goes to the one page region; a toast appended to the editor's own container is torn down with it. (2) **`.ui-kv-center`.** The input-height strut that keeps a key/value row's switch and remove button aligned when a hint or an error makes the row taller than its input. (3) **Overlay and bubble controls reset inherited typography.** A control that can sit inside a host container (a toast, a tooltip, a menu, a bubble) sets `text-transform`, `letter-spacing`, `font-weight` and `font` itself, so a host's eyebrow or label styling cannot restyle it. (4) **A new badge kind updates the count pin too.** Adding a kind means `BADGE_KINDS`, the `.badge--<kind>` rule, both theme blocks of `tokens.css`, the contrast rows, and the `tests/test_ui_badge_kinds.py` count (and the `test_ui_choice_contract.py` count); the pin is currently 11.
- **Icon control (M31 F1 L2, DD6).** `App.ui.icon(name, opts)` (in `ui/core.js`) returns an `aria-hidden`, non-focusable SVG node with the owned classes `ui-icon` (16px) and, for `opts.size: 'sm'`, `ui-icon--sm` (14px); `opts.className` is appended; an unknown name throws. `App.ui.util.icon` shares its builder but adds no size classes, so hosts that size `svg` themselves are unchanged. The glyph table in `icons.js` stays data (`App.icons`); the string `App.icon` was deleted in M31 F6 L09a (`App.iconNode` in L03a).
- **Owned classes and the reuse check (M31 F1 L2, DD3/DD4).** Each `library:` row of `docs/ui-control-inventory.md` has an **Owned classes** column (six cells per control row). `tests/test_ui_ownership.py` counts those class tokens outside `js/ui/` (string-literal contents of `static/js/**/*.js`, found by its own tokenizer `scan_js`; selector text of `chat|studio|admin|base.css`) and the retired-API calls (`api:App.icon(`, `api:App.iconNode(`, `api:App.showFlash(`, `api:App.confirmDelete(`, `api:App.consentLink.`, `api:App.escapeHtml(`, `api:App.escapeAttr(`, in code only; since M31 F6 L09a these are a regression guard only, the aliases being deleted) per file, and compares with `OWNERSHIP_ALLOWANCE` in `tests/_ui_ownership_allowance.py`. Excluded: comments, testid values (`testid:`/`<word>Testid:` values, `'data-testid'` keys, `setAttribute('data-testid', ..)`, and `data-testid="..."` inside markup strings). Match is `(?<![\w-])name(?![\w-])`. **Ratchet:** the check fails on any difference, up or down, and prints the corrected dict; lower the allowance in the same change that removes uses, never raise it without a flight-log reason. `test_scan_ends_in_the_code_state` runs the tokenizer over every real JS file to catch desynchronisation.
- **Editor family and form extensions (M31 F1 L4, DD1/DD2/DD3/DD8).** `ui/editor.js` holds `App.ui.editorShell`, `App.ui.section` and `App.ui.dangerZone`; `ui/confirm.js` (moved from `static/js/confirm.js`, behaviour unchanged) holds `App.ui.confirm`, (its `App.confirmDelete` alias was deleted in M31 F6 L09a, with the sanctioned-alias exemption in `tests/test_ui_library_source.py`). **Shell boundary (DD1):** `editorShell` builds the header only. It reads no store or router, fetches nothing and attaches no handler to the breadcrumb link (`a.editor-breadcrumb-link[href]`, the `href` is the caller's; `trail`/`lead` are always text nodes, only `label` links). `setDirty` drives only the `span.editor-unsaved[role=status]` caption and button enablement (Save starts disabled in every mode; Cancel starts disabled unless `cancel.enabledWhenClean`, the create-form case); `setBusy`, `setError`, `setTitle` (textContent only, so focus stays), `setSubtitle`, `setSaveLabel` are the other setters; `titleEl`/`focusTitle()` are exposed and the shell never calls `App.focusAfter`. Classes are the legacy ones (`cancel-btn`, `save-btn`, `test-provider-btn`, overridable with `actions.test.className`) so editors' dirty code keeps selecting them; `afterTitle` and `actions.extra` are the extension points. `section` returns `{el, body, setTitle}`; `dangerZone` builds the trash-icon delete button (icon outside the `[data-confirm-title]` span) and an optional `.action-blocked-message` banner (a `role=alert` region that stays rendered while empty and takes no space via `:empty`, so setting its text is announced), and runs `App.ui.confirm` on click. **Armed style:** one rule for every button, `button.confirm-delete` plus `:hover` and `:focus-visible` at (0,2,1) in the `App.ui.confirm` banner, so no variant hover rule undoes it (list-row and KB-summary armed rules stay with their owners); `.confirm-lapsed` stays element-agnostic. **Form extensions:** `App.ui.infoButton({description, label, testid})` (in `ui/field.js`; its `aria-label` is "More information about {label}", or "More information" without one, while `title` keeps the full description and the hidden span plus `aria-describedby` carry it once) returns `{el: span.ui-info, button: button.field-info-icon, descriptionId}`; `field`, `toggle` and `checkbox` take `info` (field: label and button as siblings in `div.field-group-label`; choices: `div.ui-choice-head`), the description id joins the control's `aria-describedby`; every native control takes `dataset` (a `testid` key is ignored, the test id option wins) and the choices take `name` and gain `setDisabled(bool)` (also toggles the root's `is-disabled`). Contracts: `test_ui_editor_contract.py`, `test_ui_info_contract.py`, `test_ui_confirm_contract.py` (both engines, `contracts` lane).
- **Tab and tile controls (M31 F2 L4, flight DD3/DD4/DD11).** Six additions, none of them used by a surface yet (legs 05-08 migrate the consumers; the hand-built copies stay as recorded debt in the ownership allowance and are deleted with them). **`App.ui.tabs`** (`ui/tabs.js`): `{label, items:[{id,label,testid}], initial, onBeforeChange(id, commit), onChange(id), idPrefix, panelClass, split}` -> `{el, tablist, panels, select, selected, panel, destroy}`. It reproduced `js/tabs.js` (`App.tabs`, deleted in M31 F4 L06 when the Built-in tools list was its last caller; `api:App.tabs(` stays a counted retired API) exactly: manual activation (arrows move focus only, wrapping; Home/End; Enter, Space and click commit; the roving `tabindex` and `aria-selected` change on a commit only; panels `hidden` unless active), `data-tab-id` on tab and panel, `onBeforeChange` proceeding only if it calls `commit()` (doing nothing is the veto; arrows never go through it). Ids are `<idPrefix>-tab-<id>` / `<idPrefix>-panel-<id>`. The `.tablist`/`.tab`/`.tabpanel` rules sit under the tabs banner in `components.css`, same layer and declarations as before; `el` is `div.ui-tabs` (flex column, `flex:1; min-height:0`), `split: true` returns the tablist and panels unwrapped (`el` is `null`). `tests/browser/test_ui_tabs_contract.py` states the keyboard contract explicitly (the old parity tests against `App.tabs` went with it) and compares computed styles to hand-written legacy markup in the `area-studio` and `area-admin` wrappers. `tab` itself is not an owned class (the reuse check reads string literals and "tab" is prose there); `tablist`, `tabpanel`, `tabpanels`, `ui-tabs` are. **`App.ui.groupTile`** (`ui/tile.js`): the tile the Tools and MCPs tabs share (master tristate switch, name, count and extra chips, header `actions`, `subtitle`, expand button "**N** tools" / "1 tool" / `toolLabel`, collapsible `body`; `onFirstExpand(body)` once). It never changes its children; the consumer reacts to `master.onChange(nextChecked)` and calls `setMaster('on'|'off'|'mixed')` / `setCount` back. The MCP Refresh in `actions` is `App.ui.button({size:'sm'})`: 24px, 10px text and, since this leg, a 14px icon (`.btn-sm svg`). **`App.ui.toolRow`** (`ui/tile.js`): `label.ui-tool-row` (checkbox + mono name + " \u2014 " + description cut to 100 characters, full text in its `title`), or with `disclosure:{label,onFirstOpen(body),open}` a `details.ui-tool-row` whose `summary` holds the label face and the "Overrides" affordance. A click or Space on the checkbox toggles only the checkbox because an interactive control inside a `summary` does not toggle the `<details>` (HTML, no `stopPropagation`; verified in Chromium and WebKit, `test_tool_row_four_interactions_toggle_only_their_own_target`); `disabled` has no disclosure, "Disabled by operator" and the strike-through; `note` is the extra `current_time` line (`time-tool-note`). **`App.ui.chipList`** (`ui/chip-list.js`): a copy of the (since deleted, M31 F5 L06) `tools-form-helpers.js`' chip list (Enter, comma and Tab commit, Tab on empty moves on, Backspace removes the nearest editable chip before the input, pasted comma lists split, duplicates ignored, click on a chip body repositions the input, an empty commit clears without `onChange`; locked chips have no remove button; `validate(v)` mirrors `onChipAttempt`). The remove "\u00d7" is `button.ui-chip-remove` inside the chip (so `readChipListValue` still strips the trailing x) and is `--fg-secondary`, `--status-danger` on hover and focus-visible (canvas, approved). **`App.ui.toggle({tristate:true})`**: keeps native checkbox semantics (no `role=switch`; mixed is `indeterminate`, `checked=mixed` in Chromium's tree), adds `setIndeterminate`, `setChecked` clears the mixed state, all setters are silent, a click on a mixed toggle gives `checked` and `onChange(true)`; the mixed look is the accent-wash track with the accent thumb at `translateX(8px)` (plus a forced-colors rule). **`App.ui.dangerZone({actions:[...]})`**: one delete-style button per `{label, testid, confirmLabel, beforeArm, onConfirm, icon}` (each has its own `beforeArm`; `icon` defaults to `trash`), `action` is the one-item form, returns `buttons` as well as `button`, and `setDisabled(bool, index?)`. **Intentional duplicates until their consumers migrate:** the `.tools-category-tile*`/`.mcp-source-tile*`/`.tool-tile*`/`.mcp-tool-toggle-label` rules in `studio.css` (legs 07-08 delete them) and the `.chip-list-*`/`.chip-remove` copies in `admin.css` (Flight 3). Tests: `test_ui_tabs_contract.py`, `test_ui_tile_contract.py`, `test_ui_chip_list_contract.py`, and the tristate and `actions` cases in `test_ui_choice_contract.py` / `test_ui_editor_contract.py` (all both engines, `contracts` lane).
- **Shared editor changes (M31 F5 L05, flight DD4/DD7).** `editorShell`'s error is no longer in `.form-actions`: `errorEl` (`span.editor-error.ui-editor-error-row`) is the LAST child of the shell's header element (`div.editor-header`, or the `pageHeader` element of the page variant), which carries `ui-editor-shell-header` (`flex-wrap: wrap; row-gap: 0`). The row is `flex: 0 0 100%`, `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, takes the top margin only while it has text, and carries `data-empty` while empty (set by `setError`; never `hidden`, so a `role=alert` region stays in the tree), so a long message wraps under the title and cluster and never narrows them.
