The cluster keeps the empty span's old 8px trailing gap as `margin-right: 8px` under `.ui-editor-shell-header`, so the no-error layout is pixel-identical. The colour/size is one shared declaration (`.form-actions .editor-error, .ui-editor-error-row`; the legacy selector stays for the MCP editor's hand-built header until leg 10). `setTestState({disabled, label})` holds Test disabled across `setDirty`/`setBusy` and relabels the text node only (`null` restores; a no-op with no `actions.test`). B7: the provider, connector and knowledge delete buttons read "Delete provider", "Delete connector", "Delete knowledge base" (the agent editor's button reads "Delete agent" too, since M31 F5 L11, ledger row 28), and `dangerZone`'s default title is "Danger zone" (CSS still shows capitals).
- **Disclosure, key-value table and clearable input (M31 F3 L03-L04, flight DD4).** `disclosure({variant: 'bordered'|'plain' (required), summary, meta, actions, open, onToggle, onFirstOpen, chevron, testid, summaryTestid, summaryTextTestid, dataset, className})` in `disclosure.js` is a native `<details>`; it returns `{el, summary, summaryText, body, actions, setOpen, isOpen, setSummary, destroy}`. The summary's accessible name comes from `aria-labelledby` over the text and each `meta` node, so an `actions` button keeps its own name and a click on it never toggles; `summaryText` carries `data-confirm-title` so `App.ui.confirm` armed on `ctl.summary` swaps and restores it, and `setSummary` is a no-op while armed. `keyValueTable({columns, rows, rowDataset, addLabel, removeLabel, onChange, ...})` in `key-value-table.js` keeps a keyed row model (a remove never re-indexes the survivors) and returns `{el, values, setValues, setError, focusRow, destroy}`; the connector channel map is its first consumer, and Flight 5 adds a sensitive column type for the MCP editor. `clearableInput({...})` in `field.js` is a text input with an in-field clear button (squawk 0100). Options added to existing controls in the same flight: `chip` `warning` tone and `dataset`, `status` `ledTestid` and `textTone`, `editorShell` `save.enabledWhenClean` and `errorRole`, `copyField` `buttonLabel`, `field` `errorTestid`, `section` `descriptionTestid`, and the `more` icon. **Single-consumer options at the close of the flight (L09, all kept):** `button.compactIcon` (MCP Refresh), `groupTile` `master.describedBy`, `master.tristate: false` and `master.dataset` (`mcps-tab.js`; the Tools tab uses the tristate default), `dangerZone.beforeArm` (provider editor), `status.ledTestid` and `copyField.buttonLabel` (connector editor), `disclosure.summaryTextTestid` (knowledge folder panel), `clearableInput` and `keyValueTable` (one consumer each until Flight 5 and the Settings flight). `emptyState` keeps its `plain` and `flush` variants (no third variant appeared).
- **Feedback and value controls (M31 F1 L5, DD3/DD8/DD9).** (`App.ui.status` also takes `led: false`, M31 F2 L10c: no LED node at all, the words alone, `data-state` still set.) `ui/feedback.js` gained `App.ui.status` (`p.ui-status` with the 10px `indicator-led`, `pending` shows the idle LED, LED `aria-hidden` with text, and `role=img` named by `ledLabel` (option, or `set(state, text, {ledLabel})`) without text; with no label it stays `aria-hidden`; no glyphs), `App.ui.flash(message, {error, duration})` (the `App.showFlash` delegate was deleted in M31 F6 L09a; since M31 F6 L03a a toast always goes to one page region, `div.app-flash-region`, exported as `App.ui.flash.region()`, or inside the topmost open `App.ui.modal` dialog (`App.ui.modal.top()`), which moves it to the next dialog down or the region when it closes; the newest toast replaces the one showing and clears its timers; a `container` option is ignored), `App.ui.progress` (returns `{el, bar, cancelButton, set, setCancelDisabled}`; `role=progressbar` is on the inner `ui-progress-track`, with the text and cancel button as its siblings so the button stays accessible; fill by `style.width`, indeterminate is the `ui-progress--indeterminate` class, optional icon-only cancel) and the display-only `App.ui.chip` (`neutral`/`danger`, `size: 'sm'`). `ui/copy.js` holds `App.ui.copyButton` (visible text "Copied" for 2s, "Copy failed" on failure; the accessible name follows the visible text; `doneText`/`failText` shape only the announcement, the visible text is always "Copied"/"Copy failed"; `liveEl` reuses a caller's `role=status` node) and `App.ui.copyField` (read-only input, optional `extra` nodes, then the button; `inputClass`/`rowClass`/`copyClass` keep a caller's classes). `ui/consent-link.js` (moved from `static/js/consent-link.js`) is `App.ui.consentLink`, with `build`, `isSafeUrl` and `focusTarget` as properties; (the `window.App.consentLink` alias was deleted in M31 F6 L09a.) Its default test ids stay literal per surface; `urlTestid`/`openTestid`/`copyTestid` override them. The `.test-result-*` rules were dead CSS and are deleted (M31 F6 L09a). **M31 F6 L03a additions:** `App.ui.badge.live({kind, text})` returns `{el, set({kind, text})}` (empty text hides; unknown kind throws) for badges that change in place; `App.ui.button.setIcon(btn, name)` swaps a button's first `svg`; an icon-only `copyButton` now shows the check icon and `title="Copied"` for 2 s ("Copy failed" keeps the copy icon) and reverts both; `App.ui.noteFlag({sources})` is the untrusted-note flag; and `components.css` holds no unlayered rule (`tests/test_css_layers.py`).
- **Sink count (M31 F1 L2, DD5).** `HTML_SINK_ALLOWANCE` in `tests/test_ui_guards.py` is the exact per-file count of every `innerHTML`/`outerHTML`/`insertAdjacentHTML` site `classify_sinks` finds (dynamic and static, clears included); `test_html_sink_count_matches_allowance` fails both ways. `DYNAMIC_SINK_BASELINE` stays as the text freeze.
- **Library behaviour fixes (M31 F2 L5, squawks 0220 and 0221, DD2/DD5).** (1) **`App.ui.confirm` announces.** One shared polite live region (`div.visually-hidden[role=status][aria-live=polite]`, `data-testid="ui-confirm-live"`) is appended to `document.body` on the first arm, cached with an `isConnected` check, and written through a single pending-timeout handle (clear, then write on the next tick; each write cancels the last, so a lapse cannot race a following arm). Arm: "Press {subject} again to {confirmLabel lower-cased without '?'}."; lapse: "{subject}: not confirmed"; commit clears. The subject is `opts.announceName`, else the control's `aria-label`, else the original title. The rules, notes and self-configuration local arm announcements are gone (their `rules-status`/`notes-status`/`externalStatus` regions stay for limit and error messages). (2) **`App.ui.infoButton` is a press-only toggletip** (the operator chose press-only on 2026-10-21, so the hover `title` tooltip is removed; release-note line for the next release): `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls` on the button, a `div.ui-info-bubble[hidden]` inside `span.ui-info` holding a polite live region filled one tick after the bubble shows. Escape (focus in the wrapper), an outside press, focus leaving or a re-press closes it, focus stays on the button; the Escape handler stops propagation only when it closes a bubble, so a modal around it stays open. The capture-phase document `pointerdown`/`keydown` listeners are added on open and removed on close and `destroy`; a module-level reference keeps one bubble open at a time; the bubble flips above / to the end edge when it would overflow the viewport. `tests/test_ui_guards.py::test_ui_dir_document_listeners_are_paired` requires every `document.addEventListener` in `js/ui/*.js` to have a matching `removeEventListener` of the same handler in the same file. The string-path icons (then `tools-form-helpers.js`, `tools-editor.js`, `mcp-env-headers.js`) kept `title` until Flights 3-5; all three now use `App.ui.infoButton`. (3) **`App.ui.util.mountSlot()`** (see the catalogue) replaced the provider editor's two module-level singletons (`activeController` in `provider-models.js`, `latestMount` in `provider-editor.js`); destroy order is previous mount, then `container.textContent = ''`, then the new mount.
- **Probing the real engine.** `scripts/webkitgtk_probe.py --setup-script PATH` (repeatable; run after load and the hash route, before the root-selector poll; each file wrapped to end in `true`) mounts test scripts such as `tests/browser/_probe_mount_ui.js` into a `#ui-probe-root` outside the app shell; a script may leave a JSON-serialisable value on `window.__probeReport`, echoed as `report`. Both probe scripts are also evaluated by Playwright contract tests so they cannot rot.

### Theme switching contract

Tri-state preference (`'dark'` | `'light'` | `'auto'`) persisted in `localStorage` under `theme-preference`. `<html data-theme="dark|light">` is applied synchronously by `static/js/theme.js` BEFORE any stylesheet load — the `<script>` is in `<head>` ahead of `<link rel="stylesheet">` to avoid FOUC.

API on `App.theme`:
- `get()` → preference or `'auto'` if unset
- `resolved()` → currently-applied theme (reads `data-theme`)
- `set(pref)` — throws on values other than `'dark'`/`'light'`/`'auto'`. `'auto'` REMOVES the localStorage key (page falls back to OS).

Auto-mode resolution: `darkMedia.matches` → dark, else `lightMedia.matches` → light, else **dark** (canonical default — the design language is dark-first). Reads/writes are try/catch-wrapped so private-browsing degrades to in-memory non-persistent.

**Cross-tab sync (M17 F2 L3)**: theme.js listens for `window` `storage` events on the `theme-preference` key, so a preference set in one tab applies live in every other open tab — including key REMOVAL (`newValue === null` → auto) and garbage values (degrade to auto via `resolve`). The listener reads the event's `newValue` (never re-reads localStorage — a re-read races a subsequent write) and re-dispatches `theme-preference-changed` so a mounted toggle in the receiving tab re-checks. `storage` events never fire in the originating tab, so there is no echo loop. Regression: `tests/browser/test_theme_cross_tab_sync.py` (two pages, SAME BrowserContext — storage events don't cross contexts).

Test environment: Playwright pins `color_scheme="no-preference"` so tests don't inherit host OS preference. Measured ground truth (M17 F2 L3): Chromium under that emulation MATCHES `(prefers-color-scheme: light)` — the no-preference media state was dropped from the spec — so **auto resolves light in the harness** (the "neither matches → dark" default is unreachable there). To capture a specific theme: `page.evaluate("App.theme.set('light')")` + wait for `App.theme.resolved() === 'light'`.

### Typography & icons

Sizes / weights / families / icons are defined in `static/css/fonts.css` and `static/js/icons.js` — read the source rather than mirror the catalog here. Icons pinned to `lucide@v0.265.0`; the pin matters for snapshot-test stability. Add icons by appending to `Icons` in `icons.js` (preserve Lucide's `width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"`).

### App shell — invariants

- **Global `[hidden]` reset (squawk 0178).** `base.css` has ONE `[hidden]:not([hidden="until-found"]) { display: none !important; }`; `!important` reverses layer order, so no author `display` in any layer can defeat `hidden`. Do not add per-component `[hidden] { display: none }` copies.
- The `<a class="skip-link" href="#main-content">` is the FIRST element in `<body>` (before the rail). Activating it sets `location.hash = '#main-content'`; the router ignores it because `main-content` is an element id, not a route (an element-id fragment is never routed, and while it is present `sync` treats the URL as unchanged). `<main tabindex="-1">` receives programmatic focus.
- **Four destinations over three area modules** (`App.router.destinations`): Chats (`chat`), Agents (`studio`), Library (`studio`), Settings (`admin`). The rail (`mountRail` in `app.js` mounts `App.ui.rail` into `<div id="app-rail">`) renders one `a[data-testid=rail-<id>]` per destination (`rail-chats|agents|library|settings`); there is no top bar (`#app-topbar`, `.app-brand*`, `.app-picker-button`, `nav-chat-btn` and `--topbar-h` are gone, and the "GleepGlorp AI" wordmark was dropped: the brand mark's `alt` is the only name). `mountRail` subscribes with `App.router.onCommit(fn(destId))` (called from `applyShell`, commit step 5, on every commit including boot; returns an unsubscribe; a throwing subscriber never breaks a commit) and on each commit calls `setCurrent(destId)` and `setHref` for every destination: the current one to `#` + its root, the others to `#` + `lastRoute(dest)` (which falls back to the root, so first boot needs no special case). A click is therefore an ordinary in-app link handled by the interceptor. **The rail footer and Ctrl K (M25 F4 L7):** `mountRail` builds the Jump to control (`rail-jump-to`) BEFORE `App.ui.rail()` and passes it as `footer`; `onCommit` never touches it. The ONE shell-level `keydown` listener for Ctrl K is on `document`, installed once per boot by `installJumpToKey()` right after `mountRail()` in `app.js`'s `DOMContentLoaded` handler (library factories may not install document listeners). See "Jump-to palette (client, M25 F4 L7)". `app.js` keeps the area registry, `App.areas` and a private `mountArea(areaId, destinationId)` (the panel lifecycle: it deactivates/activates the area module ONLY when the module changes, so Agents <-> Library never re-activates studio).
- `.content-panel.active` carries `data-destination="<id>"` (inactive panels have it removed); it is the browser tests' readiness probe (`_nav.destination_panel`). Panels keep their `.area-*` classes (about 600 area-scoped CSS rules).
- **Layout (M25 F2 L6).** `.app-shell-row` is a flex ROW, not a grid (in a grid a `hidden` aside would let `<main>` auto-place into its track): `#app-rail` (flex-shrink 0), `<aside id="app-sidebar">`, `<main id="main-content" tabindex="-1">`; `body` keeps `overflow: hidden` and the `min-width: 0` chain. Rail 72px; the aside's width is per destination (below); 1024px is the supported minimum and below it is unsupported (no horizontal scroll). Reference-spec section 2 deviations: destinations are `<a>`, not `<button>`; the active marker is the DD14 wash plus inset ring rather than `border-color: var(--accent)`; hover is `--surface-elevated` (section 2 defines none); the brand mark is 24px, not 28px (non-integer scale of pixel art); the Jump-to control (44px ghost, Ctrl K) is Flight 4. `.visually-hidden` is a global utility defined once in `components.css` (removed from `admin.css`/`chat.css`), so library `labelHidden` fields work with only `tokens.css` + `components.css` loaded.
- `<aside id="app-sidebar">`: visible on every destination since M25 F3 L3 gave Agents its list column (`DESTINATIONS.agents.sidebar` is `true`; `hidden` would still apply to a `sidebar: false` destination, and `#app-sidebar[hidden] { display: none }` in `base.css` beats the author `display:flex`), and `aria-label` = the destination name ("Chats", "Agents", "Library", "Settings"). **`data-destination` and the widths (M25 F3 L1, DD1).** `applyShell` (`router.js`, commit step 5) also stamps `aside.dataset.destination = destId` on every commit (boot, rail clicks, Back/Forward), and that is all the router does for widths; before the first commit the aside has no attribute and the default applies. `base.css` sizes the aside from it: `#app-sidebar[data-destination="chats"|"agents"|"library"]` is `width: var(--list-w)` (300px; Chats since M25 F4 L4a, which retired the `--sidebar-w` 240px aside and its 200px-under-1280px media rule; the default before the first commit is `--list-w` too) and `[data-destination="settings"]` is `width: var(--settings-nav-w)` (260px, A2's approved 261px render within 1px), both border-box and **fixed at every supported width** (the detail pane gives up the width: 1024 - 72 - 300 = 652px). The two tokens are theme-agnostic, in `tokens.css` `:root`. Specificity: the destination rules are (1,1,0), tying `#app-sidebar[hidden]`, so they must **never set `display`**: `[hidden]` still wins over them (pinned by `test_aside_hidden_wins_over_the_destination_width_rule`, which hides the Agents aside by hand now that no destination lacks a sidebar). The aside's `padding-top: 10px` is zeroed on every destination: `#app-sidebar[data-destination="chats"|"agents"|"library"|"settings"] { padding-top: 0 }` (M25 F3 legs 3, 4a and 5a; Chats in M25 F4 L4a) lets the list column and the Settings nav set their own section 3 and 8.1 padding. Tests: `test_shell_rail.py` (widths per destination at 1024/1279/1280/1440, all fixed since M25 F4 L4a; the attribute across Back/Forward) and `test_flow_chat_list.py` (the Chats column at 1024 and 1440). Selectors that mean the active panel must keep the `.content-panel` qualifier, since the aside now carries `data-destination` too. The Library aside holds the grouped Library list (a `<section>` labelled by its "Library" h2) since M25 F3 L4a retired the "Library sections" nav. The Settings aside holds the Settings nav, `<nav aria-label="Settings">` (reference spec section 8.1, M25 F3 L5a; Flight 2's section nav was "Settings sections"): the complementary landmark and the navigation landmark share the name "Settings" across two roles, which assistive tech announces apart. No two landmarks of the same ROLE share a name (`test_landmarks_and_aside_labels`, narrowed from "no two landmarks" to per-role in L5a). `descriptor.sidebarLabel` is gone.
- **The agent drawer slot (M25 F4 L6, DD7).** `index.html` holds a STATIC `<aside id="chat-drawer" aria-label="This chat's agent" data-testid="chat-drawer" hidden>` in `.app-shell-row`, after `<main>` (the skip link and `<main>` are unchanged; `test_shell_rail.py` pins the row's children as `app-rail`, `app-sidebar`, `main-content`, `chat-drawer`). The chat area fills it through `App.ui.drawer({el: slot})` and toggles its `hidden`; nothing else creates or removes it, and it is `hidden` on Agents, Library and Settings (the chat area's `deactivate`), so `<main>` keeps its full width there. `.app-shell-row` is `position: relative` (the overlay mode's containing block), and `#chat-drawer[hidden] { display: none }` re-asserts `hidden` for the slot before the chat area fills it (once filled, the components-layer `.ui-drawer[hidden]` is the rule that wins). Landmarks with a chat open: `main`, the drawer's complementary "This chat's agent", the rail's `nav` "Main" and the sidebar's complementary "Chats", no name repeated within a role (`test_landmarks_and_aside_labels`).

### Studio area modules (M31 F2 L02a)

`js/areas/studio.js` is being split into modules, each a factory on the `providers-section.js` precedent. Leg 02a moved two, leg 02b moved the Library list and the three section-glue modules, and leg 03 (M31 F2 L03) moved the Agents list and the agent editor (below). `studio.js` is now the shell (~740 lines): the area object, `init` (`state`, layout, module creation), `closeOpenItem` / `discardOpen_` / the breadcrumb handler / `setActiveSection` / `updateContentVisibility` / `writeUrl_`, the store subscriptions, the `_refresh` and `_dirty` test seams, the nav source and boot:

- **`studio/studio-route.js`** (`App.studio.route.serialize(self)` / `.hydrate(self, sub, opts)`): the hash serialize/hydrate bodies. Stateless and NOT a `create(ctx)` module, because the router may call `hydrateState` before `init` and every hook it calls is guarded (`self._closeOpenItem || function () {}`). `serializeState` / `hydrateState` on the area are one-line delegations.
- **`studio/agent-data.js`** (`App.studio.agentData.create(ctx)`): the network wrappers (agents, tools, MCP, rules, notes, self-configuration, credentials, knowledge bases, providers), the operator-state / MCP / knowledge caches, `fetchToolSchema`, `fetchMcpSourceTools`, `loadAll`, `showLoadAllError`. ctx: `self`, `content`, `sectionContentEl`, `renderAgentList(scroll)`, `onSelfConfigChanged(updated)` (the self-configuration funnel's call; since M31 F2 L10a it goes to the editor controller's `onSelfConfigChanged`, not `doSelect`). The handle exposes EVERY wrapper under its existing name (the agent editor takes about 30 by reference) plus `loadAllDepth()`. The `App.store.subscribe` calls (agents, providers and the five Library ones) stay in `studio.js`, the coordinator, and call module handles.

- **`studio/library-pane.js`** (`App.studio.libraryPane.create(ctx)`, created FIRST): the Library list column (`LIBRARY_GROUPS`, the gated first fill, the selection rule, the Add menu, the start page, the row-focus helpers). Handle: `syncCache`, `reload` (`reloadLibrary`), `render` (`renderLibraryList`), `sync` (`syncLibrarySelection`), `rowIndex`, `focusNear`, `focusGroupStart`, `renderEmpty`, `load` (`loadLibrary`), `create` / `destroy` (the pane's lifetime), `reset` (= destroy), `isOpen`, plus `selection`, `atRoot`, `goCreate`, `focusCreate`, `isSection`. ctx: `self`, `state`, `listHost`, `sectionContent()`, `buildRootHeading` and `providers()`, a call-time getter, the one back-reference (the providers section is created after the pane and takes its handle).
- **`studio/knowledge-glue.js`**, **`studio/mcp-glue.js`**, **`studio/connector-glue.js`** (`App.studio.knowledgeGlue` / `mcpGlue` / `connectorGlue` `.create(ctx)`): the Knowledge, MCP and Connectors section dispatch and editor mounts. One shared ctx built after the providers section: `self`, `state`, `pane`, `providers` (the handle), `setActiveSection`, `discardOpen`, `writeUrl`, `sectionContent()`, `fetchAgents`, `buildRootHeading`, `agentData` (the connector module also takes `mcp`). Each handle has `render`, `reset()` (what `closeOpenItem` / the outgoing-section cleanup call) and `isOpen()` (what `editorOpen` calls); the MCP handle adds `prefetchTypes` and `tryConsumePending`, the connector one `tryConsumePending` and `resolvePendingLibraryItem`, and the Knowledge one `clearPoll` and `itemsSignature`. The pending-item rule (`PENDING_KINDS`, `resolvePendingLibraryItem`) lives in `connector-glue.js`, the last glue module created, so it takes the MCP handle with no lazy reference. The 5 s knowledge poll timer stays on `self._knowledgePollTimer` because `deactivate` reads it. The five Library store subscriptions stay in `studio.js` and call the handles.

- **The agent editor modules** (M31 F2 L03), created in this order in `init` (each only needs handles created before it):
  1. **`studio/agent-form-data.js`** (`App.studio.agentFormData.create(ctx)`): `collectFormData`, `getFormSnapshot`, `checkDirty`, `_storedUngatedDisabledNames` and the `App.toolCategories.ungatedPrefixes` read. ctx: `self`, `state`, `editorEl`. It holds `checkDirty` so the model picker and the controller both take it from here (no picker <-> controller cycle). **`tool_overrides` rule (squawk 0223, ported in M31 F2 L08a):** `collectFormData` omits the key when no `[data-tool-override-tool][data-tool-override-field]` element is mounted (the server keeps the stored map); otherwise (sortie 07: the own-only walk) it reads the field HANDLES, stores the agent's OWN fields only (an inherited field stores nothing; a denied list stores only the entries added beyond the locked app-wide ones), carries `self._selectedAgent.tool_overrides` entries of tools whose panel was not opened, and omits `tool_overrides` entirely when the result equals the stored map (`{}` is stored as `{}`, `null` clears).
  2. **`studio/agent-model-picker.js`** (`App.studio.agentModelPicker.create(ctx)`): `fetchModelsForProvider` (abort + live-provider race guards, the `(custom)` option), the capability and context chips, `wireEditorEvents` (dirty listeners and provider-change effects), the Ollama VRAM check. Local `formatBytes` / `vramCheckTimer`. ctx: `self`, `state`, `editorEl`, `formData`.
  3. **`studio/agent-editor-controller.js`** (`App.studio.agentEditorController.create(ctx)`, created after `agentData`): `renderEditor` (the callback bag handed to `App.studio.renderAgentEditor`, with `App.toolCategories.byCategory`), `setActiveTab`, Cancel, Delete, Purge, Test provider, `save` and `extractApiError`. ctx: `self`, `state`, `editorEl`, `agentData`, `formData`, `picker`, `discardOpen`, `writeUrl`, and `list()`. Its self-configuration refresh (patch the open editor in place, rebase a dirty baseline, the stored-value note) is `studio/agent-editor-selfconfig.js` (`App.studio.agentEditorSelfConfig.create(ctx)`, M33 F3 L1), built by the controller.
  4. **`studio/agents-list.js`** (`App.studio.agentsList.create(ctx)`, created last): the Agents `listPane`, the empty detail state (`AGENTS_INTRO`), row-focus helpers, selection (`doSelect`, `replaceWithAgent_`, `replaceWithAgentsRoot_`, `doEnterCreateMode`), `renderEmptyEditor` and the pending-agent rule (`resolvePendingAgent`, `tryConsumePendingSelect`, which waits on `state.bootLoad`). ctx: `self`, `state`, `listHost`, `editorEl`, `sectionContent()`, `agentData`, `controller`, `updateContentVisibility`, `setActiveSection`, `writeUrl`, `closeOpenItem`. Handle: `isOpen`, `create`, `destroy`, `resetEmpty`, `render` (`renderAgentList`), `selection`, `focusNear`, `indexOf`, `renderEmptyEditor`, `doSelect`, `enterCreate`, `syncSelection`, `clearFilter`, `replaceWithAgent`, `replaceWithAgentsRoot`, `resolvePending`, `tryConsumePending`.

  **Agent editor frame (M31 F2 L06a).** `studio/agent-editor.js` (`App.studio.renderAgentEditor(container, agent, providers, callbacks)`) builds the frame on the library and returns a handle `{key, shell, tabs, loadFailed, patch, rebuildPanels, setLoadError, destroy}`: the header is `App.ui.editorShell` (breadcrumb "Agents", title "New Agent" or the name, the description as subtitle, `extra: [agent-chat-btn]` on a saved agent, Cancel with `enabledWhenClean` in create mode, Save/Create; no shell Test button, Test stays in General), then `App.ui.tabs` (`split: true`, so the tablist and `.tabpanels` stay direct children of `.studio-editor`; ids `agent-tab-{id}` / `agent-tab-panel-{id}` through the new `panelIdPrefix` option; testids `tab-{id}`). It has no HTML-string sinks and no ownership rows. **Identity and the slot.** The controller mounts through `App.ui.util.mountSlot()`; the slot data is `{key, token, handle, wireGen}` and the key is `create` or `edit:{saved name}`. A different key (another agent, create -> edit, a **rename**, a delete via `destroyEditor`, which `renderEmptyEditor` calls) remounts the frame and restores `self._activeTab`; the same key calls `patch`. **`patch(agent, providers, cb, opts)` (interim list, in order):** (1) the shell title, subtitle and Chat target; (2) the load-failure line (squawk 0090: `setLoadError` acts only on a change of `h.loadFailed`, writing the header's error slot through `shell.setError` and putting the `agent-load-error` testid on it while the load has failed; `doSelect` raises a one-time error toast for the failed load); (3) General patched through `handle.general.setAgent` (06b), Knowledge patched through `handle.knowledge.setAgent` (07a), MCPs patched through `setSources` then `setAgent` (07b), Tools patched through `setAgent` with `{revert}` for Cancel (08b) Rules and notes left alone (09) and Self-configuration handed its record through `handle.selfConfig.setAgent` (10a): every panel is patched, none is rebuilt, so the interim rebuild list is empty; then in the controller (4) `wireEditorEvents` re-run, the requested tab selected if it differs (a hydrate's pending tab), and (5) `state.snapshot = getFormSnapshot(); checkDirty()`. Cancel is the same refresh from the stored agent (no frame remount). **Dirty/save block.** `collectFormData` and `getFormSnapshot` stay DOM-based; `checkDirty` calls `shell.setDirty(dirty)` through a call-time `ctx.shell()` getter and then re-applies squawk 0090 with `shell.saveButton.disabled = true` (never `setBusy`, which would disable Cancel). **Async guards.** `liveGuard()` (controller) and the `isCurrent` argument of `wireEditorEvents` (picker: model fetch, VRAM check) are true only while the mount token is current and no panel rebuild has bumped `wireGen`; Test provider and the model/VRAM callbacks drop their result otherwise, and a Save whose mount is gone skips the re-render. Focus targets (`editorTitleEl_`, `saveBtnEl_`, `cancelBtnEl_`) resolve from the current slot data's shell. **Self-configuration refresh (M31 F2 L10a, DD9-3).** A self-configuration write (enable, disable, approval mode, approve, override, dismiss, restore) no longer re-selects the agent. `agent-data.js::_refreshAgentAfterSelfConfigurationChange` still invalidates `agents`, refetches the MCP sources cache and the agent and replaces the list row, then calls `ctx.onSelfConfigChanged`, which `studio.js` wires to the controller's `onSelfConfigChanged(updated)`. That acts on the open editor only when `self._activeSection === 'agents'`, the slot is mounted with key `'edit:' + updated.name` and no Save is in flight (`saveInFlight`, set from the start of a PUT/POST to the end of its handling); otherwise it only replaces `self._selectedAgent` (name match) and re-renders the list row. When it acts it never touches `selectSeq`, `state.dirty` or `_selectedAgentLoaded` and never calls `doSelect`, `patch`, `General.setAgent` or `MCPs.setAgent`. Per tab: `general.applyStored({description, system_prompt}, baseline)` writes a changed stored value into a CLEAN field only (the DOM value equals the snapshot baseline's) and returns the keys of changed fields the operator edited (they keep their text); `general.setStoredChangeNote(keys | null)` shows the `agent-stored-value-changed` status ("The stored description changed. Save will replace it with your text.", or "system prompt"); the note is cleared by General's `setAgent` (Save, Cancel), by a new identity (a remount) and, through the dirty callback (`recheckStoredNote_`), once the field equals the rebased baseline again; `shell.setSubtitle` follows a changed description; `mcps.setSources(self._allMcpSources, name)` adds or removes the surface tile and keeps the other tiles' unsaved state (07b); `selfConfig.setAgent(updated)`. **Snapshot rebase:** a form that was clean is re-baselined (`state.snapshot = getFormSnapshot()`); a dirty one has its baseline parsed and changed key by key (`description`/`system_prompt` to the new stored values, `mcp_source_ids` gaining or losing the surface id in the form's DOM order, `mcp_overrides` following the surface source's entry), stringified back, then `checkDirty()`. **The tab handle** `{setAgent, destroy}` (`renderSelfConfigurationTab`, kept as `h.selfConfig`, skipped by Cancel and called by `patch`): `setAgent` stores the record and follows `self_revision_requires_approval` (no fetch, no rebuild; it leaves an in-flight toggle alone); `destroy` bumps the tab's counters and hides the token reveal. The tab refetches its own data after a successful action behind per-kind sequence counters (late responses drop): enable/disable refetch status (with External access) and revisions, approval mode refetches the status, approve/override/dismiss/restore refetch the revisions. On success the action's message is written to `self-configuration-action-status` and focus moves there BEFORE the refetch (removing the focused card cannot drop focus to `body`); a stale or failed action moves no focus. **The token reveal's lifetime changed:** a same-identity Save no longer clears it (the panel is no longer rebuilt); disable, a new identity and `destroy` clear it (leg 10c adds tab deactivation). The security behaviour is unchanged: approve stays fail-closed against staleness, `?override=true` is sent only from the override button, restore targets only `applied` revisions, and credential mint, rotate and revoke still skip `onSelfConfigChanged`. The never-used `fetchPendingCount` callback is gone. **Module split (M31 F2 L10b).** `self-configuration-tab.js` is now the entry only (`App.studio.renderSelfConfigurationTab` and its `{setAgent, destroy}` handle, unchanged for `agent-editor.js`); the tab's logic lives in four modules loaded before it (`index.html`): `self-config-status.js` (`App.studio.selfConfigStatus`: the action-status focus target and `announce`, the enable and approval-mode switches and status lines, `refetchStatus` / `refetchRevisions`, `setAgent` / `destroy`), `self-config-revisions.js` (`selfConfigRevisions`: the pending queue, cards, approve, dismiss, the stale panel and the override, and the pure diff helpers), `self-config-history.js` (`selfConfigHistory`: the history list, restore nodes and LEDs, `fieldName`, the timestamp formatters) and `self-config-external.js` (`selfConfigExternal`: the credentials and the token reveal). Each exports `build(ctx)`; the entry builds one per-mount context (`panel`, `agent`, `callbacks`, `destroyed`, `statusSeq`, `revisionsSeq`, `approvalReady`) and calls them in panel order (status, revisions, history, external), each appending its sections and publishing its renderers back on `ctx` (`announce`, `refetchStatus`, `refetchRevisions`, `renderQueue`, `renderHistory`, `renderExternalState`, `hideTokenReveal`, `fieldName`, the list and count nodes), which the others read at call time; `setAgent` updates `ctx.agent`, and handlers read `ctx.agent` when they run. No module keeps per-mount state at module level and no `window.App` state was added beyond the four namespaces. The security branches moved with identifier rewrites only (closure variables to `ctx.` fields).

  **Knowledge tab (M31 F2 L07a).** `studio/knowledge-tab.js` (`App.studio.renderKnowledgeTab(panel, agent, kbs, {onChange, knowledgeBasesLoaded})`) returns `{setAgent(agent, opts), destroy}` and has no HTML-string sinks and no ownership rows. Each knowledge base is a row `div.agent-knowledge-row[data-kb-id]` holding an `App.ui.toggle` (label = the name; the `data-kb-toggle`/`data-kb-id` datasets and testid `agent-knowledge-toggle-{id}` sit on the native `input[role=switch]`, which is what `agent-form-data.js` reads) and a sibling tool-name `span` (testid `agent-knowledge-tool-name-{id}`, wired to the input by `aria-describedby`). The load-error state is an `App.ui.status` (`agent-knowledge-load-error`, no `data-kb-list-loaded` marker) and the empty state an `App.ui.emptyState` (`titleTag: 'p'`, the Library link as its `action`, `agent-knowledge-empty-state`). **Dirty tracking:** the editor's `wireEditorEvents` sweep only sees `.field-*` and `.tool-toggle`, so each switch's own `onChange` calls the editor's `checkDirty` directly (passed in as `callbacks.onChange`). **`setAgent`** sets each switch from `kb_ids` and each tool-name node from `kb_tool_names` (placeholder "attach to see the tool name" otherwise) and never rebuilds, unless the inventory it is given (`allKnowledgeBases`, `knowledgeBasesLoaded`) differs from what was built (the id set or the loaded flag), in which case it rebuilds its own content. The inventory is otherwise refreshed only when the editor remounts or the controller's same-identity refresh passes a new list. Squawk 0094 is closed by this (the old `.tool-toggle` input was `display: none`).

  **MCPs tab (M31 F2 L07b), now the Tools tab's "MCP servers" section (M33 F1 L06).** The editor has six tabs (M33 F1 L08 added Routines): General, Rules and notes, Knowledge, Tools, Routines, Self-configuration. `buildPanel('tools')` builds two `App.ui.section`s (`agent-tools-mcp-section` "MCP servers" first, then `agent-tools-builtin-section` "Built-in tools"; the order was reversed at the M33 F4 HAT), each holding a plain host div that its module fills (both `tools-tab.js` and `mcps-tab.js` wipe their host, so neither gets the tabpanel); it returns a composite `{builtin, mcps, setAgent, destroy}` and `h.tools`/`h.mcps` are its children. Deep link `#agents/{name}/tools/{sourceId}` expands and scrolls that tile into view. `studio/mcps-tab.js` (`App.studio.renderMcpsTab(panel, agent, sources, {fetchMcpSourceTools, onChange, onSelectedSourceChange, pendingSourceId})`) returns `{setAgent(agent, opts), setSources(sources, agentName), destroy}`; no HTML-string sinks and no ownership rows. One `App.ui.groupTile` per source (testids `mcp-source-row-{id}` on the tile, `mcp-source-expand-{id}`, `mcp-source-details-body-{id}`; `aria-expanded` is on the expand button, not the tile). **The master is the attach switch**, `master.tristate: false` (a `role=switch`, never mixed; tool rows never change it), with `master.dataset` `{mcpSourceToggle, mcpSourceId}` on its input and testid `mcp-source-toggle-{id}`, so `agent-form-data.js` reads the same `[data-mcp-source-toggle][data-mcp-source-id]` DOM and the payload is unchanged. A self-configuration source's master is locked on and disabled (`locked || attached`) with the hint (`mcp-source-self-configuration-hint-{id}`) in the tile's actions. The body (lazy, on the first expand through `onFirstExpand`, guarded by a `loadStarted` flag so a Refresh on a collapsed tile fetches once) holds a note host (loading, the auth-required `App.ui.status` with its link, `App.buildMcpError`, or the no-tools `emptyState`) above `div[data-mcp-tool-group][data-mcp-tool-mode]` of `App.ui.toolRow` rows (`inputDataset` `{mcpToolToggle, mcpSource}`, testid `mcp-tool-toggle-{id}-{tool}`). **Refresh** is `App.ui.button({size:'sm', icon:'refresh-cw', label:'Refresh'})` named "Refresh tools for {name}"; it keys rows by tool name: a tool still present keeps the operator's unsaved check, a new one takes the stored default for the group's CURRENT mode (a Refresh never recomputes the mode), a removed one's row goes; on error the rows stay and the error node shows above them (replaced by the next error, removed by a success). **One delegated `change` handler per group** recounts the chip and calls the dirty callback (the editor's `wireEditorEvents` does not see library inputs; the masters call it from their own `onChange`). **`setAgent`** resets the masters from `mcp_source_ids`, recomputes each group's mode from `mcp_overrides` and re-derives every loaded row's checked state and the count chips on the same nodes; `opts.pendingSourceId` (a later deep link) expands that tile and calls the selected-source callback explicitly (`setExpanded` fires no `onExpand`). **DD9-1:** the collapse path is `onExpand(false)`, which calls `onSelectedSourceChange(null)` when the tab last reported that source, so the route drops the segment; the tab no longer parses the URL (`_parseSourceIdFromHash` and the dead `_populateDetailsBody` are gone). **`setSources`** reconciles tiles by id (adds the agent's own new self-configuration surface, removes a deleted one, swaps the empty state and the list) and leaves every other tile untouched; `agent-editor.js`'s `patch` calls `mcps.setSources(cb.allMcpSources, name)` before `mcps.setAgent(...)`, because enabling self-configuration creates the source row server-side and `update_agent` never re-adds the loopback id (a mount-fixed list would drop the surface on the next Save). The source list otherwise refreshes on remount. Library additions: `groupTile` `master.tristate`/`master.dataset`, `toolRow` `inputDataset` (contract cases in `test_ui_tile_contract.py`). The legacy `.mcp-source-tile*`, `.mcp-refresh-tools-btn`, `.mcp-tools-checkbox-group`, `.mcp-tool-toggle-label`/`-hint` rules left `studio.css` (the tile contract test inlines the legacy tool-row rules as a fixture); `.mcp-source-tile-toggle`, `.tool-toggle`/`.tool-switch`, `.mcp-tool-text`/`-toggle-name`/`-description` stay for `tools-tab.js` (leg 08) and `.mcp-source-tile-subtitle` for `admin.css`. Tests: `test_studio_mcps_tab.py` (DD9-1, real-click dirty tracking, patch-in-place, mode, payload round trip, keyed Refresh per mode, error/success, collapsed Refresh once, self-configuration enable/disable then Save, `setSources` alone).

  **Built-in tools tab (M31 F2 L08b; the tab label is sentence case since M31 F5 L11, A21).** `studio/tools-tab.js` (`App.studio.renderToolsTab(panel, agent, {toolCategories, allTools, onChange, disabledTools, operatorConfigs, fetchToolSchema})`) returns `{setAgent(agent, opts), setNotices(notices), fieldHandles(), openTool(name), destroy}`; no HTML-string sinks and no ownership rows (`tools-form-helpers.js` was deleted in M31 F5 L06). One `App.ui.groupTile` per category (root dataset `{category}`; the master is a tristate switch with dataset `{categoryMaster}`, accessible name "Enable all {Category} tools" from its visually hidden label; count chip "N / M enabled" through `setCount`, never hidden; `aria-expanded` is on the expand button) holding one `App.ui.toolRow` per tool (row root dataset `{tool}`, checkbox `inputDataset` `{tool}`, which `collectFormData` reads as `input[type=checkbox][data-tool]`). An operator-enabled tool has an "Overrides" disclosure; a disabled one has the strike, the `Disabled by operator` title and none. `current_time` carries the `time-tool-note`. **Overrides** are built lazily by `App.toolConfigFields.render(name, prop, value, {mode: 'agent', toolName, operatorValue, onChange})` (`areas/shared/tool-config-fields.js`, M31 F4 L05b/L05c; see "Tool config fields" below), plus `seedValue` and `allowed`. The `data-tool-override-tool` / `data-tool-override-field` / `data-field-type` (array: `data-marker`) markers sit on the control element itself, never the row: `agent-form-data.js` walks them, `App.toolConfigFields.readValue` reads them, and the squawk-0223 `loadedTools` rule derives "which tools were opened" from them. **Locked chips**: a NarrowSuperset field's operator items are locked (no remove button) and are part of the union sent (`chipList` reads locked chips too). **Chip rules**, as the string path had them: Narrow (subset) and NarrowPrefix are checked by the server (422 on Save), so the client accepts any non-empty, non-duplicate entry; NarrowSuperset only locks the operator's items. Un-marked non-boolean fields are hidden. Since sortie 07 the agent PUT DOES validate `tool_overrides` (a new or changed field that widens or is invalid is a 422 on `loc ["tool_overrides", tool, field]`, shown on its field), so the browser's own checks only guard the form (a whole number below); see "Agent override validation and clipping". Each field is the agent's own or inherited (Reset returns it), and a tool row carries a "{n} notice(s)" flag chip while the tool has notices. **Patch and Cancel**: built once per identity (06a); the editor's `patch` calls `tools.setAgent(agent, {revert, disabledTools, operatorConfigs, fetchToolSchema})`. After a Save (same identity) the rows' checked states, masters and counts are updated and LOADED override bodies are kept (their values equal the stored ones; an open disclosure with focus inside survives). Cancel (the controller's `cancelEdit` calls `renderEditor(agent, {revert: true})`) drops every loaded body, closes the disclosure and re-arms the lazy load, so the next open renders the stored values. A changed operator-disabled set rebuilds the tab's own content. A failed schema fetch shows a danger status and retries on the next open. **Dirty tracking**: the editor's `wireEditorEvents` only sees `.field-input`/`.field-select`, so each master's and row's own `onChange`, and every override control, calls the dirty callback (real-click tests in `test_flow_studio_tools_tab_patch.py`). The legacy `.tool-toggle`/`.tool-switch` pill, `.tools-category-tile*`, `.tool-tile*`, `.tool-row-overrides*`, `.tool-actions`, `.mcp-source-tile-toggle` and `.mcp-tool-text`/`-toggle-name`/`-description` rules left `studio.css` (`tests/browser/_legacy_tool_css.py` freezes them for the tile and chip-list contract comparisons; the a11y pins moved to the library toggle's rules). **Hidden settings and a Save (M31 F4 L05c).** A tool's `config_fields` (leg 05a) keeps a setting out of its served schema, so the Tools tab never renders it. `collectFormData`'s `tool_overrides` walk rebuilds a LOADED tool's entry from the fields it rendered, so it carries every stored override key that has no rendered field over unchanged (it stays stored and still applies at resolve time); only a key whose field the schema shows but no longer marks overridable is dropped on a Save, as it always was (the panel flags it in `data-tool-override-dead` on its body, next to `data-tool-override-panel`, and the console warning stays for it). **Number overrides**: a number field is a text input with `inputmode="numeric"` (a `type=number` input reads "12abc" as blank); "1.5" or "12abc" shows "Enter a whole number." on the field as you type (`aria-invalid`), blank is skipped, "12" sends 12; min and max are attributes only for a value the operator did not change (an own number the app-wide maximum has since undercut is exempt from the range check, so it never blocks a Save; its clipped line covers it), and the server checks the rest. `formData.blockingError()` (kept apart from the pure `collectFormData`, which also feeds `checkDirty` and the snapshot) returns the first invalid override control; the controller's `save()` calls it right after `collectFormData()`, for create and edit, and refuses before any request: the editor error names the tool and the field ("Can’t save: http_request, Maximum timeout. Enter a whole number."), the tool's Overrides `<details>` is opened and the field focused. Tests: `test_flow_studio_tool_override_fields.py` (every kind and rule, both engines), `test_flow_studio_tools_fields.py` (the carry-over, number validation, squawk 0223, A8 and A10 on the tab), `test_flow_studio_tools_tab_patch.py`, `test_flow_studio_tools_overrides.py`, `test_flow_studio_time_tool.py`, `test_flow_studio_tool_overrides_preserved.py`.

**Tool config fields (M31 F4 L05b, DD13).** `areas/shared/tool-config-fields.js` (`App.toolConfigFields`; `areas/shared/` is the first folder for area code shared across areas, loaded as the first `areas/` script) renders one Config field for the Settings tool editor (`mode: 'admin'`, the default) and the agent Tools tab (`mode: 'agent'`). `render(name, prop, value, {mode, toolName, operatorValue, onChange})` returns `{el, control, field, value(), setValue(v), setError(msg|null), setVisible(bool), validate(), chips?}` (null when agent mode hides the field); `showIf(properties, handles)` wires `show_if` to `setVisible` (a controlling field with no handle is treated as absent: the row stays shown) and returns `{apply, destroy}`; `readValue(el)` dispatches on `data-field-type` (arrays through `chipList.valuesOf`, checkbox kept); `invalidMessage(el)` is the number check. DOM hooks kept: admin `data-field` on the control, `tef-{name}` ids (a chip list: `data-field` on its root, the id on its inner input), `data-field-name` and `.tools-editor-field` on the row; agent `data-tool-override-tool`/`-field` on the control (the row has `.tool-override-field` and `data-field-name`, never the override attribute). Booleans are `App.ui.checkbox` with the label inline in both modes (A10). Numbers are text inputs with `inputmode="numeric"`; admin adds `width: 'short'`. Number parsing: blank is `''`, an integer string is the number, anything else is the raw string (admin sends it and the tools PUT's 422 lands on the field, leg 07a; agent mode blocks the Save). A custom preset value is humanised from the field-name unit token (A8): a `_bytes` token gives MB/KB (÷1024) when it divides evenly, else "{n} B"; a `_seconds` token gives h/m when it divides evenly, else "{n}s" ("4MB (custom)", "45s (custom)"); `tests/test_tool_config_text.py` fails on an integer preset field with no unit token that is not listed unitless. The admin editor moved onto this module in leg 07a (`tools-form-helpers.js`'s `renderField` and `readFieldValue` went first; the file itself was deleted in M31 F5 L06); `studio/tool-override-fields.js` and `App.studio.toolOverrideFields` are gone. Tests: `test_flow_admin_tool_config_fields.py` (both modes, every kind, both engines, `settings` lane).
  **Flight 2 close (M31 F2 L11).** Every agent-editor file is at zero rows in `HTML_SINK_ALLOWANCE`, `DYNAMIC_SINK_BASELINE` and `OWNERSHIP_ALLOWANCE` and no module in the agent path exceeds 800 lines (`studio.js` 746, the controller 748; `rules-notes-tab.js` was split into `rules-notes-shared.js`, `notes-section.js` and the entry). No consumer CSS styles a library node or a testid (`[data-testid` is banned in `static/css`): the Rules and notes list notices use `App.ui.emptyState({variant: 'flush'})` (plain title plus a flush, left-aligned body-sm root). Library additions this leg: `App.ui.badge.dot(kind)` (the provider dot, used by the Agents list and the chat picker), `listPane`'s `rows(visibleOnly)` (focus helpers no longer query the row class), `App.ui.button`'s `compactIcon` (the MCP Refresh's 14px icon). `studio.css` has no dead class selectors (token grep over `static/js`, `index.html` and the other CSS); the remaining legacy rules are named Flight 3/4 debt: the admin `.tool-tile*`, the MCP editor's `.mcp-tool-item*` and the chip-list copies the admin string path uses. The toggletip bubble is not clipped by the editor pane's scroll container (`tests/browser/test_flow_toggletip_clipping.py`: the bubble rect lies inside every clipping ancestor, for the provider editor and an agent Tools override field). Lanes: `agents` was split into `agents` and `agent-tabs` (the self-configuration, Rules and notes and Tools-override files) when it ran 1.3x over budget (squawk 0217).
  **General tab (M31 F2 L06b).** `studio/general-tab.js` (`App.studio.renderGeneralTab(panel, agent, providers, callbacks)`) builds General ONCE per editor identity from `App.ui.section`/`field`/`textInput`/`textarea`/`select`/`button`/`status`/`dangerZone` and returns `{providerSelect, modelSelect, testButton, dangerZone, setAgent(agent, providers), setTestResult(state, text), clearTestResult()}`; the frame handle keeps it as `handle.general`. Field ids (`studio-name` ... `studio-max-iterations`), `data-field` values and the `field-*` classes are unchanged (the DOM-based `collectFormData` and the tests read them); the two number inputs get `min`/`max`/`field-input-short` after construction. **`setAgent`** is the patch path (same-identity Save, Cancel, refresh): it sets the name, description, prompt and numbers, rebuilds the provider options (placeholder, providers, "Unknown provider"), resets the model select to its placeholder, drops `dataset.pendingValue`, clears the loading text, capability line and VRAM warning, recomputes Test's disabled state and hides the Test result; the controller then re-runs `wireEditorEvents`, whose per-call tail sets `pendingValue` to the stored model and refetches the list (so a provider change followed by Cancel restores both selects). **Dirty:** each General control's own `onInput`/`change` calls `callbacks.onChange` (the editor's `checkDirty`) directly and carries `_dirtyWired`, which the picker's listener sweep skips; the sweep (now tag-based, `input:not([type=checkbox]...)`, `textarea`, `select`, `.tool-toggle`, each element once) still serves the panels that are rebuilt, and the picker's provider/model listeners are attached once per control (`_pickerWired`). **Picker without sinks:** `agent-model-picker.js` builds `<option>`s with DOM (`replaceChildren`, keeping the "Choose a model" disabled first option, the `selected` rules, the "(custom)" fallback and `pendingValue`), and the VRAM warning is the same `.studio-vram-warning` element filled with `textContent`. **Test** is an `App.ui.button` (secondary, play icon, "Test", testid `test-provider-btn`, default size) in `.test-provider-row` beside an `App.ui.status` (testid `test-provider-result`, hidden until a test starts, `data-state` `pending` -> `success`/`danger`; no glyphs). The controller's `testProvider` writes it through `handle.general.setTestResult` and drops a result whose mount or panel wiring is gone (`liveGuard`). **Danger zone:** `App.ui.dangerZone({actions: [Purge now, Delete agent]})` (M33 F4 HAT: two stacked rows, Purge first, each with an opt-in `heading` and `description`: "Purge chats and history" / "Permanently delete agent"), armed labels "Confirm purge?" / "Confirm delete?", a completed purge calls `revert` itself so the button returns to "Purge now" and flashes "Deleted {n} chats and their history.", `onConfirm(revert)` calls the controller's `handleDeleteEditor(revert)`/`handlePurgeHistory(revert)` (no second `confirmDelete`); no `beforeArm`; create mode has none. `general-tab.js` and `agent-model-picker.js` have no sinks and no ownership rows; the `.test-provider-btn` rules left `components.css`.

  The only call-time (not creation-time) references are two getters into the list, named in `studio.js`: the controller's `ctx.list()` (its Cancel, Delete and Save call the list's `replaceWith*`, `focusNear`, `indexOf`) and `agentData`'s `renderAgentList` / `onSelfConfigChanged` callbacks. Everything else resolves at creation. `bootLoad` lives in `state.bootLoad` (the shell writes it, the list reads it). The shell keeps assigning the public surface from the handles: `self._syncAgentSelection`, `self._enterCreate`, `self._setActiveTab`, `self._tryConsumePendingSelect`, `App.studio.clearAgentFilter` (with its early no-op stub) and the `_refresh` / `_dirty` seams. `studio-sections.js` (the fallback for the shell's section teardown functions) was not needed.

**Knowledge modules (M31 F3 L02).** `knowledge-section.js` (1493 lines) is split with no change in behaviour or DOM (verified by seven before/after DOM dumps against a pre-edit worktree). Each module is an ES5 IIFE attached under `window.App.studio`; load order in `index.html` is shared, create, inspect, status, search, folders, detail, all before `knowledge-glue.js`:

- **`studio/knowledge-shared.js`** (`App.studio.knowledgeShared`): `STATUS_LABELS` and the pure helpers (`el`, `appendIcon`, `btn`, `input`, `textarea`, `fieldGroup`, `section`, `containingFolder`, `relativeItemPath`, `baseName`, `formatStamp` (delegates to `App.time.formatDateTime`), and, since M31 F4 L02b, the keyed-reconcile helpers `syncChildren`, `setTextIfChanged`, `setAttrIfChanged`). The one `el` and the one `baseName` for every knowledge module. No per-mount state.
- **`studio/knowledge-create.js`**: `App.studio.renderKnowledgeCreate(container, callbacks)` (the `~new` form).
- **`studio/knowledge-detail.js`**: `App.studio.renderKnowledgeDetail(container, kb, state, callbacks)`, same signature and `{updateStatus, updateItems, destroy}` handle (M31 F3 L05a). It owns the frame, Name and Description with their dirty tracking, the danger zone and the composition, plus `refreshStatusNow`/`refreshItemsListNow` (they need both status and folders). `state` is the glue's `{status, items}` snapshot, read only at construction.
- **`studio/knowledge-rows.js`** (`App.studio.knowledgeRows.create(ctx)`, M31 F4 L02b, split out of `-folders.js`, loaded before it): the file rows (keyed row groups, `buildFileItemRow`/`patchFileItemRow`/`renderRows`), the remove-an-individual-file flow with its focus chain, and the "Individual files" panel. It owns the groups' `order` arrays, every row menu and the Individual files panel and attached flag; `-folders.js` owns the `removedIds` tombstones and `lastItems` and reaches rows through the handle `{newGroup, releaseGroup, closeGroupMenus, renderRows, removeFile, focusAfterRemove, buildIndividualFilesPanel, renderIndividualFiles, individualSummary, destroy}`; rows reaches folders only through its ctx (`isCurrent`, `folders()` getter, `markRemoved`, `renderAll`, `attachIndividual`, `focusAddFolder`, plus the detail's setError/refresh/open callbacks). Rows is created before folders' first `renderFolders`.
- **`studio/knowledge-inspect.js`**, **`-folders.js`**, **`-status.js`**, **`-search.js`** (`App.studio.knowledgeInspect` / `knowledgeFolders` / `knowledgeStatus` / `knowledgeSearch` `.create(ctx)`): the chunks and source panels; the Folders section (Add folder, the folder and Individual files panels ("Other items" until the M31 F3 HAT), file rows, the item menu, `renderFolders`, `renderAllItems`); the Status section with re-index; Search. Each section appends to the `root` in ctx when created, so the detail creates them in today's DOM order: inspect first (appends nothing), folders, then `renderAllItems(items)` and the inspect panels appended to the Folders section, then status, search, the danger zone. Cross-module calls (`openChunks`, `openSource`, `refreshStatus`, `refreshItems`) are lazy wrappers in ctx. All per-mount state (`folderPanels`, `currentFolders`, `individualPanel`, `removedIds`, the panel nodes, `summaryEl`) lives in each `create` closure; nothing is cached across mounts. The armed-confirm invariant (squawk 0088) stays in folders: `renderFolders` and `renderAllItems` never rebuild an armed folder `<summary>`.

**Knowledge frame on the library (M31 F3 L05a).** The create form and the detail frame are built from `App.ui.editorShell` (breadcrumb "Library / Knowledge bases", title, subtitle, Cancel, Save/Create, `errorRole: 'alert'`), `App.ui.field` with `textInput`/`textarea`, and `App.ui.section`; `kb-create-error` is an `App.ui.status` (danger, `led: false`, `textTone`, `role: alert`) and carries only server and network failures; the empty-name "Name is required." is Name's own `App.ui.field` error (`kb-create-name-error`: `aria-invalid` on the input, which is focused; cleared on edit and on the next submit), an operator-approved exception to DD6 made at the M31 F3 HAT. Dirty comes from a value model (`readValues()`, a `snapshot` at mount and after Save, `stored` = the last saved record that Cancel restores from), never DOM queries. **Save patches in place**: the inputs are disabled while the PUT is in flight (`shell.setBusy`), then `stored = updated`, the title/subtitle/fields (the trimmed name) and the 43-code-point hint are written from the saved record, the snapshot is retaken and dirty recomputed; the glue only calls `reloadLibrary('knowledge-bases')`. Nothing remounts, so an expanded folder panel, an open chunks/source panel, search results and scroll survive a Save, and a Save fetches neither `/status` nor `/items`. **The glue** (`knowledge-glue.js`) holds ONE `App.ui.util.mountSlot()` for both the create and detail handles, keyed `'create'` / `'kb:' + id` / `null` (the root); `renderKnowledgeSection` remounts only on a key change (`teardown()`: poll cleared, `slot.destroy()` runs `handle.destroy()`, key nulled; then `sectionContentEl.replaceChildren()`), a same-key render only re-syncs the list selection. A failed load or a load that finds the knowledge base gone releases the key so a same-route render retries. `reset()` (closing the open item, leaving the Library group, and `deactivate` leaving the Studio area) destroys the handle, so Library -> Chats -> Library is a fresh mount and the poll restarts. `isCurrent(token)` gates every late callback (dirty mirror, poll responses, load resolution); each handle's own `alive` flag gates its Save/Create/search/re-index/refresh writes after `destroy()`, and `destroy` calls `App.focusAfter.cancel()`. Every sub-module (`inspect`, `folders`, `status`, `search`) returns an object with `destroy` (no-ops in L05a; the folders and inspect legs fill them). The loading and failure lines are `App.ui.status` lines placed with `replaceChildren()`; `App.focusAfter.flush()` is no longer called from the knowledge mount.

**Knowledge folder panels on the library (M31 F3 L06a).** `knowledge-folders.js` builds no `<details>`, `<summary>`, chip, button or field by hand. Add folder is `App.ui.field` (error through `setError`, `errorTestid: 'kb-folder-add-error'`) + `textInput` + `button`; the cascade sentence is the Folders `section`'s description (`descriptionTestid: 'kb-folders-cascade-hint'`). Each folder is a bordered `App.ui.disclosure` (`testid kb-folder-panel`, `summaryTestid kb-folder-summary`, `summaryTextTestid kb-folder-path`, `meta` = the `kb-folder-status` and `kb-folder-watch` chips, `actions` = the ghost icon-only `kb-folder-delete-btn`, so the summary's accessible name is the path plus the two chips and never "Delete folder"); the delete arms `App.ui.confirm(ctl.summary, ...)`. **0088 still holds**: nothing recreates an armed summary and nothing writes its text while armed (`updateFolderMeta` never calls `setSummary`; the library's is a no-op while armed). **Moving is the other hazard**: `appendChild`/`insertBefore` of an attached node blurs a focused descendant, so every reconcile goes through `syncChildren(parent, nodes, offset)`, which removes children not wanted first and inserts a node only when it is not already at its position (panels, the trailing "Individual files" panel, file rows, the meta lines, a row's own children). **File rows are keyed** by item id in a per-panel `group.rows` map and patched in place (path, status, error line, indexed-at, standalone `warning` chip; each only on a text change). Since the M31 F3 HAT a row is one compact line: the name at body size, then the status word and "Indexed {date}, {time}" at the small size in the secondary tone, the chip, the menu slot at the end, and a failed item's error line wrapped below it (`.kb-item-error-line`, `flex-basis: 100%`); the folder body's meta line is "Last checked …" plus an optional " · Skipped …"; `renderAllItems` no longer calls `closeAnyOpenItemMenu()` (DD8-4: a poll refresh neither closes an open menu nor steals focus), and a row's menu is closed only when its own row is removed (`entry.menu.close`). A row's item menu is an `App.ui.menu` (leg 06b replaced the interim `buildItemMenu`). Row and meta error lines are `App.ui.status` danger lines (`led: false`, `live: off`, `textTone`); the empty states are `App.ui.emptyState` (`titleTag: 'p'`). The detail passes the folders ctx `isCurrent` and `setError`: a folder-delete failure ("Couldn’t remove the folder: ...") goes to the editor header, cleared when the next add or delete starts; every late write is gated by `isCurrent`. After a successful folder delete focus goes to the Add-folder input. A stale panel removed by `renderFolders` calls `ctl.destroy()` and releases its rows' menus; `destroy()` does the same for every panel. A removed armed summary's pending confirm timer still fires once on the detached node, and since squawk 0237 (M31 F4 L03b) only reverts it: no lapse cue and no "not confirmed" announcement. Tests drive the 5 s poll with `page.clock` and stub `/status` and `/items` with `page.route` (`_KbStub` in `test_flow_studio_knowledge_inventory.py`).

**Individual files, the row rule and the inspect panels (M31 F3 HAT).** *Grouping (amends L6-D2):* `renderAllItems` puts an item with no `parent_id` in the trailing **"Individual files ({n})"** panel (`kb-individual-files-panel`, replacing `kb-other-items-panel`), an item whose `parent_id` is a known folder in that folder's panel, and HOLDS OUT an item whose `parent_id` names a folder the client does not know (`kb_items.parent_id` is ON DELETE CASCADE, so that is client lag, never real state) until its folder appears or the item vanishes; `renderFolders` re-runs `renderAllItems(lastItems)` only when it added or removed a panel, so a held-out item lands as soon as its folder does (keyed rows: a patch, not a rebuild). The panel's body opens with a description (`kb-individual-files-hint`: "Added one at a time through the API. These files aren’t watched for changes; use Re-index all files to pick up edits.": parentless items are never watched and `scope=changed` skips them) and its summary carries no chips. An individual row is `span.kb-item-file` (the basename in `.kb-item-path`, over `kb-item-full-path`, small, ellipsised, the path as `title`), then the status word, the stamp, the DD-9 chip ("Also in folder {folder basename}", the folder path as `title`, `.kb-item-badge` so it wraps inside the row), and the slot. *Remove:* parentless individual rows (eligibility decided at row build) get a ghost icon-only trash `kb-item-remove-btn` ("Remove file {path} (press twice to confirm)", title "Remove file") before the menu, `App.ui.confirm` armed ON THE BUTTON ("Confirm remove?", announced as "Remove file {basename}"); walked files never get one (the route deletes them, but the next walk re-adds them). The commit is guarded by `entry.removing` and the disabled button (one DELETE); a 2xx or a 404 tombstones the id in `removedIds` (the mount's lifetime; filtered by every `renderAllItems`, so a stale `/items` can't resurrect the row), re-renders from the last items, then refreshes status and items. Focus moves only if it was on the row or `body`: to the remove button of the row now at the removed index, else the previous row's, else the Add-folder input. A failure writes "Couldn’t remove the file: {detail}" to the editor header, reverts the arm, re-enables and refocuses the button. *Status rule (every panel):* indexed with an `indexed_at` shows only "Indexed {date}, {time}"; indexed without shows "indexed"; pending, indexing and failed show the word plus "Last indexed {date}, {time}" when set (the failed error line stays below). *Inspect panels (`knowledge-inspect.js`):* the `h3` (`kb-panel-title`, `tabindex=-1`) is the static "Chunks"/"Source", named by `aria-labelledby` over itself and the name line (focus announces "Chunks runbook-01.md"); under it `div.kb-inspect-head` holds the name (`kb-panel-file-name`) and path (`kb-panel-file-path`, ellipsised, `title`) lines and a ghost icon-only x (`kb-panel-close-btn`, "Close chunks"/"Close source", title "Close"). The openers take `(item, opener)` (the row's menu trigger); the one mount slot holds `{item, opener, panel}`, so opening the other panel destroys the first. `close()` hides the panel and clears its content (a late response fails `isCurrent`), then `ctx.onClose(item, opener)`, wired by the detail to `folders.focusAfterInspect`: the opener if it is connected, has client rects and the focus actually lands (an element inside a collapsed `<details>` can still report rects but cannot take focus), else the summary of the panel holding the item (its folder by `parent_id`, or Individual files), else the Add-folder input. After every `renderAllItems` the folders module calls `ctx.onItemsRendered(liveIds)` and `inspect.closeUnless(liveIds)` closes a panel whose item is gone, moving focus (by the same chain) only if it was inside the panel. Escape does not close a panel: it is an inline, non-modal region.

**Knowledge Status, Search and Danger zone on the library (M31 F3 L05b).** Status (`knowledge-status.js`) builds its four `App.ui.chip`s (testid `kb-status-count`, `dataset.status`) and two `App.ui.status` lines (`kb-chunk-count`, `kb-indexer-state`; `led: false`, `live: 'off'`) once; `update(status)` caches the last strings and writes only text that changed, so an unchanged poll tick performs no DOM mutation in `kb-status-summary` (pinned by a MutationObserver test). The re-index scope hint is the section's description (`kb-reindex-scope-hint`); re-index failures stay silent. Search (`knowledge-search.js`) uses `App.ui.clearableInput` (`kb-search-input`, `kb-search-clear-btn`; squawk 0100): x or Escape-with-text empties the query, results and degraded line. A `generation` token is bumped by every `runSearch` (before the empty-query return), by a clear and by `destroy`; a response or failure for an older generation is dropped, so a slow earlier search never overwrites a later one. The degraded line and a failure are danger-tone `status` lines (`textTone`); "No results." is `App.ui.emptyState`. Since the M31 F3 HAT the hits are a `ul.kb-search-hit-list` of `li.kb-search-hit` rows (hairline separators): the file's basename (`.kb-search-hit-title`, body, medium), one small meta line (the full path ellipsised on one line with the path as its `title`, then the muted "score 0.016"), and the snippet (`.kb-search-hit-text`, small, secondary, `white-space: pre-line`, whitespace-only lines emptied and runs of blank lines collapsed to one in JS, `textContent` only, clamped to three lines). The Danger zone (`knowledge-detail.js`) is `App.ui.dangerZone` with a notice (`kb-delete-error`): a `deleting` flag plus `setDisabled(true)` guarantee one DELETE; a failure reverts the arm and shows `fetchJson`'s message in the notice (flight DD6, amended: the notice, not the header).

**Connector frame on the library (M31 F3 L07a).** `connector-editor.js` builds the frame and fields once per mount from `App.ui.editorShell` (breadcrumb "Library / Connectors", title, `errorRole: 'alert'`, the header status line through `afterTitle`), `section`, `field` with `textInput`/`select`/`textarea`/`secretInput`, `toggle` (Enabled, label beside it), `status` and `button`; it returns `{destroy, setBusy, setError}` and holds its handle in a module `mountSlot`. **The value model:** `readValues()` returns `{name, type, token, enabled, default_agent, allowlist_peers, allowlist_channels, always_listen, channel_map_rows}` from the control handles (the channel map from `kv.values()`, rows `{channel, agent}`, since leg 07b); dirty is `JSON.stringify(readValues()) !== snapshot`, recomputed from each control's own input/change callback and from the channel-map table's `onChange` (typing and reverting is clean); every transition calls `shell.setDirty` and `callbacks.onDirtyChange`, back to clean included. **Save** disables Save and Cancel (`setBusy(true)`) for the request, sends through `App.api.fetchJson`, and on an edit success patches in place (`cfg = config = stored = updated`, `applyRecord(stored)`, the snapshot read back from the controls, title, `setBusy(false)`, `setDirty(false)`, `resetDoctorResult()` (clears the doctor line), `runHeaderProbe()` (re-runs the automatic connection probe for the header status line), focus to the title). A **create stays busy** until the glue remounts it in edit mode; if that remount's loads fail, the glue's `.catch` un-busies the create handle and writes "Couldn't reopen the connector: ...". **Cancel** (edit) restores every control from `stored` (the last saved record), including the channel-map table (`kv.setValues`), synchronously with no remount. **The token (security surface):** the input is written only through `.value` (`secretInput.setValue`); a saved or cancelled form shows the server's MASKED value with the reveal off; an untouched Save sends the mask and the server (`_looks_masked`) keeps the stored token; a POST with a masked-looking token is a 422 shown in the header error. No variable holds a typed token after its request settles (the payload is serialised in the click handler). A masked-looking string TYPED on edit is still "preserve" (known, unchanged). **Late writes:** the Save `.then`, the doctor responses and the probe check `slot.isCurrent(token)` before any DOM, flash or focus write (`App.store.invalidate('connectors')` still runs). **Diagnostics:** the doctor result is a `status` line (non-OK response: danger with the error text; network failure: "Doctor request failed: ..."); the automatic probe's non-OK response sets the header line to danger, "Connection check failed". **The glue** (`connector-glue.js`) keeps `handle` and `handleKey` (`'add'` | `'edit:' + id`), compares the key inside the load's `then`, and remounts only on an identity change; a same-key render keeps the editor and its unsaved edits and does NOT refresh the agents and types lists (accepted); `reset()`/`_closeConnectorForm` destroy the handle and null the key, so re-entering the section remounts. A stale `default_agent` falls back to the select's first option (known). 

**Connector sections on the library (M31 F3 L07b).** *Pairing* (edit only) is a `section` with a `Generate pairing code` button (`connector-pairing-btn`) and a plain `connector-pairing-result` container: one `App.ui.status` ("Generating…", or the danger text "pairing-code request failed: HTTP n"), and on success an `App.ui.copyField` (`connector-pairing-code` is its READ-ONLY INPUT named "Pairing code"; the copy button `connector-pairing-copy-btn` is named "Copy pairing code" and shows "Copied") then the `connector-pairing-expiry` line; a sequence counter and `slot.isCurrent` drop a stale response. The *Channel to agent map* is `App.ui.keyValueTable` (inputs `connector-channel-map-channel`/`-agent`, named "Channel id, row n"/"Agent, row n"; per-row remove named "Remove row n"); its model is the source of truth, so adding or removing a row never loses typed values (DD8-1). After an edit Save the table is re-seeded from the response, and Cancel re-seeds it from `stored`. A row with an empty channel is dirty but dropped from the payload (and from the table after Save); duplicate channel ids: the last row wins and the re-seed shows one row. The *Danger zone* is `App.ui.dangerZone` (`delete-connector-btn`, arms through `App.ui.confirm`); a commit sends one DELETE (a `deleting` flag plus disabling the button until it settles); a failed DELETE reverts the arm and shows `fetchJson`'s text in the zone's own alert notice `connector-delete-error`. `connector-editor.js` has no hand-built section, button, clipboard or confirm markup and no allowance rows.

**The `state` rule.** `init` declares one `var state = {...}` holding every variable that more than one section reads or writes: the controller state (`dirty`, `snapshot`, `tabHandle`, `selectSeq`, `editorGen`), the MCP / connector / knowledge-base form state, `kbFocusFolderFor`, `knowledgeDetailHandle`, and the Library section caches (`knowledgeBases`, `mcpSources`, `connectors`, `providersCache`). The `Object.defineProperty` getters on the area (`_mcpSourceFormMode`, `_pendingConnectorId`, `_kbCreateMode`, ...) read and write `state`, keeping their init seeds. A module reads and writes `state.x` at use time and never caches a mutable flag or object across an async boundary; generation tokens (`selectSeq`, `editorGen`) are the exception and are captured by value (`var gen = state.editorGen`) and compared with the live value when they resolve. `self._*` fields that tests and `deactivate` read (`_activeSection`, `_selectedKbId`, `_agents`, `_dirty`, ...) stay on `self`. Static pins that read studio text use `_studio_src()` (`tests/test_store_static.py`), which concatenates `studio.js` and every studio module; the `invalidate('agents')` count is pinned per file.

### Editor contracts (M31 F3 L01)

Written down for Flights 3 and 4, which reuse the mount-once, patch-through-handles pattern for the Library editors (squawk 0235). Pinned by `tests/test_studio_editor_contracts_static.py`.

**Tab-handle contract** (what a `renderXxxTab` builder returns to `agent-editor.js`'s `rebuildPanels`, stored as `h.general`, `h.knowledge`, `h.mcps`, `h.tools`, `h.selfConfig`):
- `destroy()` is required of every tab that holds listeners, timers or child controllers: Knowledge, MCPs, Tools and Self-configuration. It is idempotent teardown of the tab's own content.
- **Known exemptions**, listed in the static test's allow-list: General (`renderGeneralTab` returns its controls plus `setAgent`, `applyStored`, `setStoredChangeNote`, `setTestResult`, `clearTestResult`; its controls live in the panel and die with `h.tabs.destroy()`, and it holds no timers) and Rules and notes (`renderRulesNotesTab` returns nothing; it saves immediately, is built once per identity and keys only on the agent name).
- As found, `agent-editor.js`'s `h.destroy` calls only `selfConfig.destroy()` and `tabs.destroy()`. It should also call `knowledge.destroy()`, `mcps.destroy()` and `tools.destroy()` (MCPs and Tools hold per-row controllers and in-flight fetch generations); today they are reclaimed with the panel DOM only.
- Optional members and their callers: `setAgent(agent, opts)` (called by `h.patch`; Rules and notes deliberately has none), `setSources(sources, name)` (MCPs; called by `h.patch` and by the controller's `onSelfConfigChanged`), `deactivate()` (Self-configuration; the controller's `deactivateSelfConfig` on leaving the tab or Agents), `applyStored(...)` and `setStoredChangeNote(...)` (General; the controller's `onSelfConfigChanged`).

**Library editor handles (M31 F3 L05a-L08).** The knowledge create form returns `{focus, destroy}`, the knowledge detail `{updateStatus, updateItems, destroy, ...}` and the connector editor `{destroy, setBusy, setError}`; every one is held in a `mountSlot` (`knowledge-glue.js` keeps ONE slot for both the create form and the detail, `connector-editor.js` has its own), so a mount is torn down exactly once and `isCurrent(token)` gates every late callback. Their **identity keys**: the knowledge glue's `handleKey` is `'create'`, `'kb:' + id` or null; the same key on a re-render is a no-op for the editor (a list notification or a Save never remounts it, so unsaved edits, open folder panels, chunks/source panels, search and scroll survive), and a different key destroys the old mount before the new one mounts. The connector glue keys on the connector id (or the pending create) the same way (`editingConnector`/`pendingConnectorId` in `state`). `destroy()` sets an `alive` flag, so a late response, poll tick or parked focus restore does nothing. Dirty is a value model on both (`isDirty()` compares the current values with a snapshot), so typing a change and reverting it is clean. None of these has an HTML-string sink or an ownership-allowance row.

**`App.ui.util.mountSlot()`** (`js/ui/core.js`): `mount(destroyFn, data)` returns a token; `isCurrent(token)`; `current()` returns `{token, data}` or null; `release(token)` clears the slot without calling `destroyFn` when the token is current; `destroy()` destroys the live mount. Mounting a new one destroys the previous one first (a throwing `destroyFn` is logged and the remount goes on). `destroy` and `release` null the entry BEFORE calling `destroyFn`, so a re-entrant call is a no-op. Late-binding rule: `destroyFn` may close over a variable assigned after `mount`; if mounting throws, call `release(token)` in a catch and re-throw. Async results check `isCurrent(token)` before touching the DOM. The studio `editorGen` counter is a separate mechanism (a discard generation captured by value, bumped by `closeOpenItem`).

**The studio `state` object** (`studio.js`, a closure-local `var state` inside `init`; reached by section modules as `ctx.state`; read and write `state.x` at use time):
- `dirty`, `snapshot`: the agent editor's unsaved-changes flag and form snapshot (owner: `agent-editor-controller.js`, with writes also from `agents-list.js` and the shell).
- `tabHandle`: the mounted editor's `App.ui.tabs` handle (controller).
- `selectSeq`: in-flight select/create token (`agents-list.js`); `editorGen`: discard generation (`closeOpenItem`; read by `mcp-glue.js`, `connector-glue.js`, `providers-section.js` and the controller).
- `mcpSourceFormMode`, `editingMcpSource`, `pendingMcpSourceId`, `pendingMcpSourceFormMode`: MCP editor state (`mcp-glue.js`; exposed to `serializeState` through `self._mcpSource*` getters).
- `connectorFormMode`, `editingConnector`, `pendingConnectorId`, `pendingConnectorFormMode`: connector editor state (`connector-glue.js`, same getter pattern).
- `kbCreateMode`, `kbFocusFolderFor`, `knowledgeDetailHandle`: Knowledge create form, post-create folder focus and the poll target (`knowledge-glue.js`).
- `bootLoad`: the `init()` agents load in flight (shell).
- `knowledgeBases`, `mcpSources`, `connectors`, `providersCache`: the Library section caches, store-fed (`library-pane.js`).
`saveInFlight` is NOT a `state` field; it lives inside the controller's closure.

**Hidden-editor patch**: `_activeSection` stays `'agents'` while the user is on Chats or Settings (only Library switches it), so a self-configuration write that resolves there reaches `onSelfConfigChanged` and patches the hidden editor in place.
