Accepted: the patch is DOM-local and idempotent. Cross-reference squawk 0232.

### Agents list and detail (M25 F3 L3)

Agents is a list column beside a detail pane (flight DD1-DD4, DD8), still served by `areas/studio.js` (DD3: extend the module, do not split it).

- **Sidebar hosts.** The studio sidebar panel holds two sibling list hosts, exactly one visible (`.is-hidden`): `.studio-agents-list` (Agents) and `.studio-library-list` (Library, M25 F3 L4a; it replaced the Library section nav). `mountArea` never re-runs `init`/`deactivate` between Agents and Library (one area module), so **`setActiveSection` owns both the switch and both list panes' lifetimes**: it creates the Agents `App.ui.listPane` on entering Agents and `destroy()`s it on leaving (DD3 (a)/(e)), and the same for the Library pane (destroying the outgoing pane first, so exactly one exists); off Agents there is no Agents pane and `renderAgentList` is a no-op. Flex sizing: `.studio-agents-list { flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column }` and `> .ui-list-pane { flex: 1 }`, so the pane's body is the only scroller inside the `overflow: hidden` aside and panel. `#app-sidebar[data-destination="agents"]` has `padding-top: 0`.
- **The list** (DD4, reference spec 6.1): one `listPane` with title "Agents", `count: true` ("{shown} of {total}"), the title-row action "New" (`App.ui.button` secondary, small, plus icon, aria-label "New agent", testid `new-agent-btn`), a filter labelled "Filter agents" (placeholder "Filter by name, model or provider", testid `agents-filter`), `rowStyle: 'agents'`, testid `agents-list`, and `emptyText(q)`: `No agents match "{q}".` with a query, "No agents yet." once loaded, "Loading agents..." before. Rows are sorted A-Z by `localeCompare`; each is `{id: name, label: name, meta: llm_model (mono via .ui-list-pane-style-agents .ui-list-pane-meta), href: #agents/{name}, testid: 'agent-item', nameSuffix: ', ' + the provider's display name (or "no provider") + ',' when the model follows, lead: the provider dot}`, so the accessible name reads "{name}, {provider}, {model}" (the trailing separator is the flight-end review's: the provider used to run straight into the model). The dot is an `aria-hidden` `.badge.badge--{type}.badge-dot` (an unknown type uses `badge--unknown`; a missing provider the hollow `.badge-dot--none`), so it never carries meaning by colour alone (A31). The filter matches `name + ' ' + llm_model + ' ' + providerType` (trimmed, lower-cased; the provider TYPE slug, never its display name, A16), precomputed per row as `filterText`. `renderAgentList(scroll)` is the ONE builder (from `self._agents` and `self._allProviders`; only `name`, `llm_model` and `provider_config_id` are read, so detail-shaped agents are fine) called from section entry, `loadAll`, both store subscriptions, `doSelect`, create and the dead-id heal; it calls `setItems` THEN `setSelected` (the pending hydrate target, else the open agent; none in create mode), and `scrollToSelected` after a load or a navigation (`scroll` true) or the first fill, never on a bare store notification (so a scrolled list is not yanked). Every Agents hydrate ends with `_syncAgentSelection` (DD3 (c)). A filter that hides the open agent's row leaves the editor showing.
- **The detail pane.** No agent open: the Agents start page (M25 F6 L4c, DD15) in `.studio-section-content`: the visible `root-heading` `h1` "Agents" (a sibling, so it survives a `loadAll` failure) then `div.start-page` (testid `agent-select-empty`, or `agent-empty` with zero agents) holding the description of what an agent is, one variant line ("Pick an agent from the list to edit it." / "No agents yet.") and a primary "New agent" (`agent-empty-new`), built with DOM APIs by `renderAgentsEmpty` and inserted after the heading; nothing before the first load; only its own node is swapped, so the `loadAll` failure line beside it survives. An agent open: the tabbed editor (`renderEditor`) with only its chrome changed: `agent-editor.js` (since M31 F2 L06a through `App.ui.editorShell`) shows `<div class="editor-breadcrumb"><a class="editor-breadcrumb-link" href="#agents" data-testid="editor-breadcrumb-link">Agents</a></div>` (11px `--fg-muted`, DOM APIs, the `href` from `App.router.href`; the crumb became a link to the destination root in M25 F3 L4b, see "Item header chrome" under the Library section) as the first child of `.editor-header-text` after setting its markup; **Back (`back-agent-btn`, `handleBack`) is retired** (DD2; the testid is in the registry's retired table and fenced by the `RETIRED_SELECTORS` guard). `updateContentVisibility` shows the editor when an agent is open or the create form is, else the empty state. The tile grid (`.entity-tile-grid`, `.entity-tile.agent-item`, its page header and `agent-tile-*` counts) and its capability-chip fetch (`fetchProviderModelCaps`) are deleted.
- **Unsaved indicator** (DD2, A15). The header's `.form-actions` begins with a stable `<span class="editor-unsaved" role="status" data-testid="editor-unsaved">` (part of the baselined markup string). `checkDirty` sets its text to "Unsaved changes" or clears it from the same `dirty` flag that drives Save `disabled` (and Cancel's on an open agent; see Create), never creating or removing it; `renderEditor` calls `checkDirty` after rebuilding the header. It stays rendered while empty (no `display: none`), so the live region is in the tree when it speaks. The dirty baseline is re-taken when the model list arrives while the form is still clean (`fetchModelsForProvider`): it used to be taken before that list, so an edit the user reverted still read dirty.
- **Create** (DD8). "New" and the empty-state action call `go(href({destination: 'agents', create: true}))` (an intent go, so the router's leave guard asks once about a dirty open agent); with the clean create form already open on its route, "New" only refocuses the name field (no navigation, no re-render). `enterCreateMode` and its inline `App.nav.confirmLeave` gate are deleted; `doEnterCreateMode` (`self._enterCreate`) is the hydrate target of `agents/~new` (it waits for the providers through the store, since a cold deep link can arrive before `loadAll`) and focuses `#studio-name`. `hydrateState` compares the RAW sub `agents/~new` before decoding, closes the open item first (unless the create form is already open), and an agent sub closes an open create form; `serializeState` returns `agents/~new` in create mode, ahead of the pending/selected agent. Save-create keeps `replaceWithAgent_` (a replace `go`, so `history.length` is unchanged; the commit announces "Agents — {name}"), clearing `dirty` first. **Staleness check** (the flight-end review; the knowledge-base create's `stillOnCreate()` pattern): `save(true)` captures `editorGen` before the POST, and `onThisForm()` holds while the generation is unchanged (no `closeOpenItem` since Save) and `App.router.current().route` is still `agents/~new`. After the POST, `dirty` and `_createMode` are cleared only when `onThisForm()` and `_createMode` still hold (`dirty` is one flag shared by every studio editor, and a re-entered create form owns `_createMode`); after the `loadAll()` reload, the replace (and the "Agent created" flash) runs only if that held, `onThisForm()` still holds and `!App.router.isDeciding()`. A user who opened another agent, the Agents root or another destination while the POST or the reload was in flight stays there, with no dialog, and the list still gains the new row (`test_save_create_does_not_pull_back_a_user_who_moved_on`: the reload `GET /api/agents` held, both moves; falsified). Cancel on the create form runs `discardOpen_()` then `go(#agents, {replace, bypassGuard})` and focuses the list's tab stop, else "New"; Cancel on an open agent reverts in place. **Create-form Cancel is never gated on `dirty`** (the flight's behavior-gate fix, `list-and-detail` L6): it abandons creation, so it is enabled from first render and stays enabled while the form is in create mode, typed into or not (`checkDirty`: `disabled = !dirty && !self._createMode`; the markup omits `disabled` when `isCreate`). The same holds for the MCP, connector and provider create forms (their markup and `checkDirty` gate Cancel on `isEdit && !dirty`; the connector's `cancelBtn.disabled = !isCreate`), matching the knowledge-base `~new` form, whose Cancel was never gated; in edit mode every editor's Cancel reverts and stays disabled while clean (`test_cancel_on_a_pristine_create_form_abandons_it` in `test_flow_agents_list.py`, `test_flow_studio_library_list.py` and `test_flow_settings_items.py`, plus the clean-again and edit-mode pins).
- **Delete** (DD8). `handleDeleteEditor` reads the deleted row's visible index BEFORE the request, and on success tears down (`discardOpen_`), replaces to the `#agents` root (`replaceWithAgentsRoot_`; the announcer speaks "Agents") and focuses the nearest surviving row (the next, else the previous), else "New". The save-then-concurrent-delete race and `_refresh` finding the open agent gone also land on the root, never on another agent (the old `self._agents[0]` fallbacks are gone).
- **Tests.** `tests/browser/test_flow_agents_list.py` (AC1 in Chromium and WebKit through `credentialed_context`, AC2-AC6), `test_agents_library_isolation.py` (the DD3 walk and in-flight switches, with requests HELD rather than timed), `test_flow_agents_scale.py` (the throttled filter ratio, `GET /api/agents` at 60, 35 rows intact, the 1024px tab strip in both engines). All in the `agents` lane (`test_flow_agents_*`, `test_agents_*`).

### Editor Save/Cancel contract (squawk 0118)

Every item editor -- the agent (edit and `~new`), the provider, the MCP server, the connector, the knowledge-base detail and its `~new` form, and the built-in tool editor -- follows one contract for its header actions. The rule was unwritten until M25 F4 L6; M25 F3 shipped every create form with Cancel gated on `dirty`, which only a behavior run caught (fixed in `56f7d75`).

- **Create mode (`~new`).** **Cancel is always enabled**, from first render, typed into or not: it abandons the create (Flight 3 DD8), so it never waits for a change. **Save** (labelled "Create") waits for a change: the agent, provider and MCP server forms enable it on the first edit (`dirty`), the connector form on the first field change (`markDirty`); the knowledge-base `~new` form's Create is enabled from first render. Required fields are checked when it is clicked ("Name is required.", no request sent), not by keeping it disabled. The `editor-unsaved` caption follows `dirty` as in edit mode.
- **Edit mode.** **Save and Cancel are enabled only while dirty** (Cancel reverts in place, and there is nothing to revert while clean); the `editor-unsaved` caption ("Unsaved changes", a stable `role=status` span) shows exactly while dirty. The agent editor additionally keeps Save disabled while it shows a not-freshly-loaded agent (squawk 0090); Cancel is never gated that way.
- **Focus (DD8's one rule).** After a Cancel on a create form, a close (the breadcrumb root link) or a delete, focus goes to the item's own row, else the neighbouring row (the next visible one, else the previous), else the list's primary action ("New", or a Library section's Add). A create form has no row: its section's first row, else the primary action.
- **The save-create staleness guard.** A create whose POST (or the list reload after it) resolves after the user moved on must not open the new item or clear another editor's state: `studio.js`'s knowledge-base create form checks `stillOnCreate()` (its `kbGen` still the current `editorGen`, still in create mode, the router still on `library/knowledge/~new`), and the agent create checks `onThisForm()` (`createGen === editorGen` and the router still on `agents/~new`), before clearing `dirty` and before its bypass `go()` replaces `~new` with the new item.
- **The single `dirty` flag** (Flight 4 DD8). Studio's editors share ONE closure `dirty` flag (agents, MCP servers, connectors, knowledge bases), which the router's leave guard reads through `App.nav`; a header action that leaves (the agent editor's Chat, the drawer's Open agent settings, leg 7's palette) goes through `App.router.go`, so a dirty editor asks once. Splitting it per editor is next-mission editor work.
- **Where the code is** (grep-derived, M25 F4 L6): the `checkDirty` sites are `areas/studio/agent-form-data.js` (`function checkDirty`, the agent editor's: Save from `dirty` and the squawk 0090 block, Cancel from `!dirty && !createMode`, the caption), `areas/studio/agent-editor.js` (the header markup: Cancel enabled in create mode, Save disabled, the caption span; it calls no `checkDirty` itself), `areas/studio/mcp-server-editor.js` and `areas/studio/provider-editor.js` (Save `!dirty`, Cancel `isEdit && !dirty`), `areas/admin/tools-editor.js` (edit only: both `!dirty`) and `areas/studio/knowledge-detail.js` (the detail: both `!dirty`). The connector editor (`areas/studio/connector-editor.js`) has no `checkDirty`: `markDirty` enables both and `reportDirty` sets the caption. `createGen` is in `areas/studio/agent-editor-controller.js`; the knowledge-base `stillOnCreate()` lives in the KB create form's code.

### Library list and detail (M25 F3 L4a)

Library is one grouped list column beside a detail pane (flight DD5, DD8), served by `areas/studio.js` (DD3) like Agents.

- **Host.** `.studio-library-list` (beside `.studio-agents-list`; same flex rules) holds the Library `App.ui.listPane`; `setActiveSection` creates it on entering any Library section from Agents (or a cold boot) and destroys it on leaving for Agents, so exactly one pane exists; a move between Knowledge, MCP and Connectors keeps it. `#app-sidebar[data-destination="library"]` has `padding-top: 0`. The Library section nav (`renderSectionNav`, `LIBRARY_ROUTE_SECTION`, `.studio-section-nav`) is gone from studio; `section-nav.js` kept only its Settings caller, and was deleted in leg 5a (see "Settings nav and pages").
- **The list** (DD5, reference spec 7.1): title "Library", intro "Things agents can use. Attach them from an agent's Knowledge and Tools tabs." (A19), `rowStyle: 'library'`, testid `library-list`, no filter and no count, and an **Add** `App.ui.menu` (its first production use): trigger "Add" (`App.ui.button` secondary, small, plus icon, `library-add-btn`), `align: 'end'` (the trigger sits at the right of the 300px `overflow: hidden` aside; every item stays inside it at 1440x900 and 1024x768, checked by item rect and `elementFromPoint` at each item's four inset corners), items "Knowledge base" / "MCP server" / "Connector" (`library-add-knowledge|mcp|connector`), each a `go()` to its `~new` (choosing the create form already open on its route only refocuses its first field, dirty or not; never a same-route guarded `go`). `menu.destroy()` runs with the pane. Groups, in order, each A-Z by `localeCompare` and with an id for `scrollToGroup`: Knowledge bases (`knowledge`, "No knowledge bases yet."), MCP servers (`mcp`, "No MCP servers yet."), Connectors (`connectors`, "No connectors yet."). Item ids are namespaced (`knowledge:`, `mcp:`, `connector:` + id). Rows: `<a href="#library/{knowledge|mcp|connectors}/{id}">` with the kept card testids (`knowledge-base-card`, `mcp-source-card`, `connector-card`, from `LIBRARY_GROUPS` where the registry guard sees the literals), an `aria-hidden` icon (`database`, `server`, `plug`; `server` and `plug` were added to `icons.js` at lucide v0.265.0 geometry) and a visible meta (so it is in the accessible name): `{n} agents` (`App.usage.kbAgents`), `{state} · {n} agents` for an MCP row, where the first part is built from ONE `App.usage.mcpState` call (M31 F5 L11, B6): `{t} tools` when connected, else `App.usage.mcpStateLabel(state)` ("Not connected", "Couldn't connect", "Sign-in needed"; an errored or signed-out row hides any cached count), plus a Local (stdio) or Remote (streamable-http) badge of the neutral `unknown` kind (`tests/test_mcp_state_words_static.py` pins that `library-pane.js` and `mcp-connection-status.js` carry none of the four state words as literals), `{n} agents` for a connector (`connectorAgents(c).length`: `default_agent` plus `channel_map` values, de-duplicated); singular "1 agent" / "1 tool". Agent counts appear only once `agents` has loaded (`peek('agents').value`); until then a row shows only what is known (never "0 agents"), and a row with nothing known keeps an empty meta line (a no-break space, which the accessible name trims), so every row stays two lines (reference spec section 3's 50px) and nothing shifts when the counts arrive: a shift would push a scrolled-in group back out of view.
- **One builder, gated first fill.** `renderLibraryList(scroll)` (in `studio/library-pane.js`, handle `render`) calls `setItems` then `setSelected`. It is a no-op until all three collections have SETTLED (`peek(name).loaded`, or this pane's own get settled either way), so no group flashes "No ... yet." before its data; meanwhile the pane's function `emptyText` reads "Loading library...". A collection with no value whose get failed shows "Couldn't load {group}." as its group line; a failed refresh with a last-good value keeps the stale rows. The selection is the open item, else a pending deep-link id (a cold deep link selects its row as soon as the list loads), none at the Library root or on `~new`. After a load or a navigation (`scroll`) or the gated first fill, the selected row scrolls into view (leg 3's `document.fonts.ready` re-scroll included; there is no group scroll at the root, since every root is the one start page); a bare store notification never scrolls. Every Library hydrate ends with `syncLibrarySelection(true)`.
- **The Library root (model-providers sortie, DD1-DD3).** `#library` is Library's own route (`DESTINATIONS` Library `root: 'library'`; `resolve()` has a `library` special case beside `agents`, so the generic "bare destination redirects to its root section" branch is Settings-only), and studio's pseudo-section `_activeSection === 'library'` represents it: `hydrateState('library')` runs `closeOpenItem()` (which bumps `editorGen`, so an in-flight save landing after a breadcrumb go fails its generation check and cannot sync the URL back to the item: `test_router_guard.py`, a held connector POST) then `setActiveSection('library')`; `serializeState` returns `library` with nothing open; `isLibrarySection`, `atLibraryRoot` (now simply `_activeSection === 'library'`), `librarySelection` (none), `renderLibraryEmpty()` (no group argument), `setActiveSection`'s dispatch, studio activate and `focusClosedItem_` (a Library close must land at the root) each have an explicit `library` branch; `editorOpen()` is false there. A bare section URL (`#library/knowledge|mcp|connectors`, no id, no `~new`; any Library row in `SECTIONS`) is an **alias** for `#library` in `resolve()` (`redirected: true`, one `replaceState`, never written), the `settings/general` mechanism, and **nothing produces one**: breadcrumbs, create-form Cancel, the Delete callbacks, `closeOpenItem`'s root, the `mcps-tab.js`/`knowledge-tab.js` links and the heal and poll paths all go to `library` (the heals through `setActiveSection('library')`, whose `writeUrl_` syncs; a bare `sync` would write the URL without hydrating). `tests/test_nav_helper.py::test_no_bare_library_section_producers_in_static_js` pins that. `describeRoute` speaks it "Library". `_nav.canonical` folds `library` and bare `library/{section}` to `library`. **Tests migrate to `library`**: `expect_route`/`goto` use `"library"`; `_lists` keys such as `"library/knowledge"` remain LIST names (which list a row belongs to), not routes.
- **The detail pane.** The Library root shows the Library start page (M25 F6 L4c, DD15): the visible `root-heading` `h1` "Library" then `div.start-page` (`library-start`) with one intro line and, in `LIBRARY_GROUPS` order, a `.start-page-card` per type (icon, `h2` card title that is deliberately not `.section-title`, description from `LIBRARY_EMPTY`, and its Add button `library-start-{group}-add` calling `goLibraryCreate`); the same three cards at the one root, and no `.page-header` (a test pins that). The page is left-aligned with the heading sharing one 720px max-width rule in `studio.css`. A pending deep-link id still resolving shows nothing (never the empty state first). An item open mounts the existing editor (`renderKnowledgeDetailView`, `renderMcpSourceEditor`, `renderConnectorEditor`) beside the list; the grids (`renderKnowledgeGrid` and its inline create form, `mcp-servers-grid.js`, `renderConnectorsGrid` and its unstyled CTA) are deleted. Leg 4a's interim Back controls (an intent `go()` to the section root) were retired in leg 4b: the item header breadcrumb's group link replaces them (next bullet); the connector's former inline `App.nav.confirmLeave` gate is gone (the router guard handles a dirty editor). Opening another knowledge base stops the previous one's poll at once.
- **Item header chrome** (M25 F3 L4b; DD2 and its refinement, DD8). Every Agents and Library item header -- the agent editor (edit and `~new`), the knowledge-base detail and its `~new` form, the MCP and connector editors (edit and `~new`) -- has exactly one `h1` and, as the first child of `.editor-header-text`, the breadcrumb (`.editor-breadcrumb`, 11px `--fg-muted`): "Agents", or "Library / Knowledge bases", "Library / MCP servers", "Library / Connectors". The link is an `<a class="editor-breadcrumb-link" data-testid="editor-breadcrumb-link">`: on the agent editor the last segment, "Agents" -> `#agents`; on a Library editor (DD3 of the model-providers sortie) "Library" is the link, to `#library` (the Library root), and the group name is plain text after it (`href` from `App.router.href`, DOM APIs only). The breadcrumb stays a `div` (one link is not a landmark), and the link's text is its destination. Insertion follows the build style: the string-built agent and MCP editors insert it after `innerHTML`; the DOM-built connector editor and both knowledge-base forms (`_breadcrumb()` in `knowledge-shared.js`) build it during construction, so the knowledge-base detail's Save re-mount re-adds it (and the 5s poll restarts with the mount, unchanged). CSS (`components.css`): the link inherits the breadcrumb's colour and size, is underlined at rest (`text-underline-offset: 2px`; so it is not told from the plain "Library" / "Settings" text by colour alone, WCAG 1.4.1, the flight-end review), thickens the underline on hover, and shows the standard 2px `--accent` `:focus-visible` ring (pinned in `test_design_tokens_a11y.py`; the rest underline also in the Agents, Library and Settings breadcrumb browser tests). **Back is retired** for all three Library editors (their three Back testids, now in the registry's retired table and fenced by the `RETIRED_SELECTORS` guard; their `onBack` callbacks and studio's `onBack` wiring): the list closes an item by another row or its section's empty state, and the breadcrumb link closes it to the root. **Routing and focus:** `studio.js` listens for clicks on `content` (ahead of `nav.js`'s document-level interceptor). A plain activation of a `.editor-breadcrumb-link` (a click, or Enter; modified clicks keep the browser's behaviour) is `preventDefault`ed and becomes `App.router.go(href, {opener: link})`, so the router's guard asks once about a dirty editor (Stay returns focus to the link through the dialog's opener) and the root's hydrate closes the item (`closeOpenItem`). When the go resolves `true`, focus moves to the closed item's own row (DD8: `focusAgentRowNear(visibleIndexOf(name))` / `focusLibraryRowNear(libraryRowIndex(id))`; a row the filter hides yields its nearest visible neighbour); a `~new` form has no row, so focus goes where Cancel on a create form sends it (the section's first row, else the list's primary action). The focus runs one task later (`setTimeout(0)`): after a Continue, `nav.js` focuses `<main>` from its own `setTimeout`, and the row must land last (falsified: without the deferral the Continue cases end on `<main>`). It is skipped if the user has moved on (another section, or an item open again). Leg 5b does the same for "Settings / Providers" and "Settings / Built-in tools". **Unsaved indicator** (A15): each Library editor and the knowledge-base `~new` form open `.form-actions` with the stable `<span class="editor-unsaved" role="status" data-testid="editor-unsaved">` the agent editor has had since leg 3, its text set to "Unsaved changes" or cleared by the same report that feeds studio's `dirty`: the knowledge-base detail's `checkDirty`/`recapture`, the create form's `reportDirty`, the MCP editor's new `reportDirty(d)` (all three of its `onDirtyChange` sites go through it; the span is in the baselined markup string, so no new sink) and the connector editor's `reportDirty` (from `markDirty`). It shows on an edit and clears on Save, Cancel and Continue, and is toggled, never re-created, within a mount. A connector **edit-mode Save** now leaves the editor clean (caption cleared, Save and Cancel disabled, as in the other editors); before, both stayed enabled after a save while studio's `dirty` was already false. It calls no `onDirtyChange` there: studio's `onSaveSuccess` clears `dirty` under its generation guard. **Create opens the item** (DD8), checked per type: the knowledge base `go`-replaces from `~new` (leg 4a: detail mounted with its breadcrumb, row selected, name announced, `history.length` unchanged); MCP and connector keep the editor's render path and `sync` the URL to `/{id}` with no hydrate and no history entry, and nothing tears the editor down after its create-to-edit render: the MCP auto-run Refresh result (the Test result, or the OAuth "Sign in" consent prompt) and the connector's entered fields and a pairing code generated after the create survive the URL change, the announcement and the list reload. `dirty` is clear after each, so the next row click opens with no prompt.
- **Providers section (sortie 02, leg 1).** Model providers are the Library list's FIRST group (`LIBRARY_GROUPS` row `providers`, label "Model providers", icon `sparkles`, testid `provider-card`, row prefix `provider:`; the Add menu's first item `library-add-provider`, the start page's first card `library-start-providers-add`). Rows are `<a href="#library/providers/{id}">` with the name, an `App.ui.badge` type badge (`KNOWN_BADGE_TYPES`; an unknown type is `unknown`) and "{n} agent(s)" meta from `App.usage.providerAgents` through an EXPLICIT branch in `libraryItem` (any group that was not named used to fall through to the connector count). Pane creation and studio activate force-get FOUR collections. **Two views of providers in studio, one source of truth (`App.store`'s `providers`):** the Agents list's gated `self._allProviders` (the `loadAllDepth`/`_agentsLoaded` subscription, unchanged) and the Library cache `providersCache` (the new ungated subscription, which also re-attempts a pending consume and renders the list only while the pane exists). **The module interface (DD4).** `areas/studio/providers-section.js` is `App.studio.providersSection.create(ctx)`, called once in studio's init (studio keeps the handle as `self._providersSection`, read by `serializeState`/`hydrateState`). It owns the item state (form mode, the provider edited, the pending deep link), `PROVIDER_TYPES`/`fetchProviderTypes` and `TYPE_DEFAULTS` (the only copy) and reaches studio ONLY through `ctx`: `host()`, `getDirty()`/`setDirty(b)`, `editorGen()`/`bumpEditorGen()`, `isActive()`, `discardOpen()`, `reloadLibrary(name)`, `syncLibrarySelection(force)`, `libraryRowIndex(id)`, `focusLibraryRowNear(idx)`, `focusLibraryGroupStart(id)`, `cache()`, `showRoot()` (= `setActiveSection('library')`, the start page plus the URL heal). The handle it returns is `formMode()`, `editingId()`, `pendingMode()`, `pendingId()`, `setPending(mode, id)`, `consume(allowCache) -> bool`, `heal()`, `render()`, `reset()`, `selectionId()`, `createOpen()`, `editorOpen()`, `focusCreate()`, `serialize()`. A needed studio state beyond `ctx` is the flight's Divert criterion (re-plan the interface). Studio plugs it into `PENDING_KINDS['providers']` (collection `providers`), `closeOpenItem`/the outgoing-section cleanup (`reset()`, which also calls `App.studio.clearProviderSignInHandoff()`, so an abandoned create-to-edit hand-off cannot fire a sign-in later), `editorOpen()`, `librarySelection()`, `libraryCreateOpen`/`focusLibraryCreate` and `setActiveSection`'s dispatch. **Routes:** `SECTIONS` row `{dest:'library', route:'providers', sub:'providers', create:true}`; the area subs are `providers/new` and `providers/{id}` (matched before the item prefix); a bare `#library/providers` is the alias for `#library`; `ITEM_COLLECTIONS['library/providers']` = `{collection:'providers', noun:'Model provider'}`; `LIBRARY_LABELS` (destination-keyed; the route-keyed `SECTION_LABELS` holds only the lazily merged Settings pages). A dead deep-link id heals through `heal()` -> `ctx.showRoot()`. **Chrome:** the editor is `App.studio.renderProviderEditor(container, provider, types, {host: 'library', ...})`: there is ONE host (Library; the interim `callbacks.host` option was deleted in leg 2): the pane's one `h1.editor-title`, the `Library / Model providers` breadcrumb (Library = `editor-breadcrumb-link` to `#library`), and no "Provider created" flash across the create re-mount. **Save-create staleness guard:** `onSaveSuccess` checks `editorGen()` and, for a create, that the router is still on `library/providers/~new`, before `setDirty(false)` and the `sync('studio', 'providers/{id}', {name})`; a stale create still refreshes the list. **Stale-callback rule (review fix round 2):** `editorGen` is bumped only by `closeOpenItem`/the discard, not by a section change or a same-section A to B consume, so every editor callback (`onCancel`, `onSaveSuccess`, `onDeleteSuccess`, `onDirtyChange`) also captures a module-local mount token (bumped on every `render()` and `reset()`) and goes inert when the token is stale, `editorGen` moved or `!ctx.isActive()`: a stale save/delete only refreshes the list (`reloadLibrary('providers')`) and never clears `dirty`, syncs, goes, discards or focuses. Tests: the three `held` tests in `test_flow_provider_library.py` (revert the field before leaving so no discard bumps the generation).
 The create-to-edit re-mount is kept (the anthropic-plan `pendingAutoSignInId` hand-off). Accepted risk: a provider whose id is exactly `new` is unreachable at its item route (the other Library groups carry the same). **Settings no longer has providers (leg 2, DD8):** `#settings/providers…` and `#admin/providers…` are unknown routes (boot lands on `#chats`; no redirect, old links drop). CSS: the editor and model-list rules live in `studio.css` under `.area-studio` (`admin-pf-*`/`plan-*` names kept: accepted naming debt); `.cap-chip*` is in `components.css`. Tests: `tests/browser/test_flow_provider_library.py` (the `providers` lane, with the other `test_flow_provider_*` files); `tests/browser/_providers.py` (`block_claude_ai`, `studio_dirty`).

- **Knowledge-base create** (DD8; closes squawk 0101). `#library/knowledge/~new` (`hydrateState` matches `knowledge/new` before the `knowledge/` prefix; `serializeState` checks the create flag first; `editorOpen()` includes it; `closeOpenItem`, leaving Knowledge and an item hydrate clear it; the hydrate closes an open item unless the form is already open). `App.studio.renderKnowledgeCreate(container, {onDirtyChange, onCancel, onCreateSuccess})` (`knowledge-create.js`, DOM-built): its own header (breadcrumb "Library / Knowledge bases", `h1.editor-title` "New knowledge base", the pane's only `h1`), Name (`kb-create-name-input`, the 43-code-point hint `kb-create-name-hint`), Description (`kb-create-description-input`), the error line (`kb-create-error`, server and network failures only), and `.form-actions` Cancel (`kb-create-cancel-btn`) / Create (`kb-create-submit-btn`; enabled; an empty name shows "Name is required." under Name (`kb-create-name-error`) with no request; disabled from submit until the request fails, so a second click never POSTs twice). On success, in order: the module invalidates `knowledge-bases`; studio clears `dirty` at once only while still on this form (same `editorGen`, route still `~new`: `dirty` is one flag shared by every studio editor); awaits `reloadLibrary('knowledge-bases')` (the row exists, and the router names the opaque-id item from the store); re-checks the generation, the route and `!App.router.isDeciding()`; then `go(href(kb), {replace, bypassGuard})` (if the reload fails it still goes; the announcement then falls back to the noun). The commit announces "Library -- Knowledge -- {name}", and focus moves to `kb-folder-path-input` once the detail mounts. Cancel runs `discardOpen_()`, replaces to `#library/knowledge` and focuses the group's first row, else Add.
- **Connector URLs** (DD8). `connectors/new` and `connectors/{id}`, with the MCP pending shape: `_pendingConnectorFormMode` / `_pendingConnectorId` (always overwritten by a hydrate; `serializeState` returns a still-pending id, so a cold deep link is never synced to the root early), the within-section skip-or-close branch, an `add` pending state consumed at once, and the pending-item rule (a fresh hydrate on an unknown id heals to `#library/connectors`). Rows are hrefs and the Add menu goes to `~new`; nothing renders in place. Save-create re-renders the editor in edit mode from the saved row with **no navigation and no hydrate**: `reloadLibrary('connectors')`, clear `dirty`, then `App.router.sync('studio', 'connectors/' + encodeURIComponent(id), {name})`, which announces through leg 2's create->item rule. Cancel: edit mode re-renders from `editingConnector` in place (the URL stays the item); create mode discards and replaces to the root (focus as for the knowledge base).
- **Delete** (DD8, every type). The callback reads the deleted row's flat visible index first (the row is still listed: `invalidate` notifies nobody), tears down (`discardOpen_`), replaces to the section root, then `reloadLibrary(name)` and focuses the row now at that index (the next, which may be in the next group), else the previous, else Add.
- **Tests.** `tests/browser/test_flow_studio_library_list.py` (AC1-AC6, loading and failure; leg 4b's item header chrome: breadcrumbs in edit and `~new` modes and across a Save re-mount, the root link closing each type with row focus and asking once when dirty, the unsaved indicator on all four forms, and create-opens-item with the preservation checks; in `library_pat`), `test_flow_agents_list.py` (the "Agents" link), `test_agents_library_isolation.py` (one pane per destination, the same pane across Library sections), `test_store_library.py::test_library_list_request_counts` (the fetch policy), `test_ui_library_contract.py` (`scrollToGroup`, both engines).

### Settings nav and pages (M25 F3 L5a)

Settings is a grouped nav of links (reference spec section 8.1, flight DD6/DD13) beside the page. Built-in tools opens its items below the list and has no Back control: see "Settings item pages (M25 F3 L5b)". Providers left Settings in sortie 02 (they are in Library).

- **The nav.** `admin.js` `init` builds one `App.ui.navGroups({label: 'Settings', heading: 'Settings', testid: 'settings-nav', groups})` and mounts it on a child of the admin sidebar panel, `<div class="admin-settings-nav">` (never on the panel itself, which keeps `.area-admin`; the former `<nav class="admin-section-nav">` wrapper is gone). Groups, in order: **General** (Appearance, Date and time, Prompts, Encryption), **Defaults for agents** (Built-in tools, Knowledge indexing). Since M25 F4 L7 the groups and items are built from the shared page table **`App.settingsPages`** (`static/js/settings-pages.js`, rows `{section, label, group}` in nav order, `section` the ROUTE segment), so `admin.js` holds no page label or group name of its own (its page headers read the table too, through `pageLabel(category)`); the area's internal `ROUTE_SECTION` map (`knowledge` -> `knowledge-indexing`) stays in `admin.js`. Each item's id is its ROUTE section (the area's `knowledge` category is the `knowledge-indexing` page), its `href` is `App.router.href({destination: 'settings', section})` and its test id is `settings-nav-{section}`. The items are plain `<a>` links with **no click handler, no tabindex and no keyboard handling** (`CATEGORY_ROUTE_SECTION` and the in-area `setCategory` are gone): a click is a user intent `App.nav`'s interceptor routes through `go()`, so the router's leave guard asks once about a dirty editor, and a click on the current page goes to its root (closing an open provider or tool editor through `_setCategory`'s same-category close). Keyboard: links in Tab order (DD10; the board's `<a>` items), Enter activates.
- **Selection.** `aria-current="page"` (A12) is on exactly the current page's link. `_setCategory` calls `settingsNav.setSelected(section)` (it replaced `App.layout.updateActiveSection`), and `hydrateState` ends with `_syncNavSelection()` on every hydrate, item routes included: at `#settings/tools/{name}` "Built-in tools" stays selected (the section id, never the item id).
- **Column CSS.** `base.css`: `#app-sidebar[data-destination="settings"]` is `--settings-nav-w` (260px, A2) at every width and, since L5a, `padding-top: 0`. `admin.css`: `.area-admin .admin-settings-nav { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; padding: 14px 10px }` -- the host is the column's own scroller, because the sidebar panel is `overflow: hidden`, so the Developer group never clips at 1024x768. With the factory's 10px heading indent and 10px item padding, the heading's text and the items' text both sit 20px from the column edge (A3, ±1px); items are 28px.
- **The General split.** The single General page (`system-page.js`'s `renderSystemPage`, three blocks) is three pages with their own routes and area subs (`appearance`, `date-and-time` (leg M28 F2 L3), `prompts`, `encryption`; `GENERAL_SUBS` on the admin area descriptor), rendered by `App.admin.renderAppearancePage`, `renderPromptsPage` and `renderEncryptionPage` (DOM-built, no HTML-string sink; `renderSystemPage` is removed). Each renderer owns its whole page and wires only its own block: the theme picker (an `App.ui.radioGroup` in its card look, built in `system-page.js`: `theme-picker`, `theme-option-{light|dark|auto}`; the page's own `THEME_EVENT` listener prunes itself on the first event after the group leaves the document (M31 F4 L08a, DD8-19); `theme.js` keeps only `App.theme.get/set/resolved`, the chrome signal and the event dispatch; `App.admin.renderPage` delegates the header to `App.ui.pageHeader`), the read-only prompt layers in assembly order (M27 F3 L3, changed in Flight 2 leg 1c: the Platform prompt as one list of blocks in assembly order with the tool-free Identity beside Identity and an identical tool-free Formatting shown once (no variant sub-headings; `platform-section-*`; `base-prompt-content` is retired), Rules `rules-prompt-content` and Notes `notes-prompt-content`; `/api/info` adds `rules_prompt_example` and `notes_prompt_example`, rendered by the real `render_rules_block`/`render_notes_block` from placeholder content so they cannot drift) and the rotate flow (`rotate-key-btn`, `App.ui.confirm`; see "Encryption-rotation invariant" for the status route and result). The Prompts layers are `App.ui.section` stacks with an `h2` layer title (B11) and every text an `App.ui.codeBlock` (trimmed, which owns `code-block` since L08b); the Notes and Date variants are plain `prompt-variant` sub-headings (the Platform prompt has none) and a platform block's title an `h3`. The block's former in-page title is now the page's `h1`, so nothing repeats it; the Appearance `h1` carries `id="admin-appearance-heading"`, the radiogroup's `aria-labelledby` target, the picker's fieldset is named "Theme" by a hidden legend. `renderContent` dispatches through a `GENERAL_RENDERERS` map, and `_refresh` re-renders the current General page. Routes: see "Hash routing" (the `settings/general` alias and the legacy redirects).
- **Page headers (section 4, AC4).** Every Settings page renders `<div class="page-header"><h1 class="page-title">{label}</h1>[action]</div>`, one `h1` (item pages included: an open tool's editor title is an `h2`, the flight-end review; `test_settings_item_pages_keep_one_h1_with_an_h2_editor_title`), no border and no breadcrumb, with the board's labels: Appearance, Prompts, Encryption, **Built-in tools** (was "Built-in Tools"), and **Knowledge indexing** (was "Knowledge"). The route announcer's section labels match: since M25 F4 L7 the router reads the Settings ones from `App.settingsPages` (`tools` is "Built-in tools"). The section 4 geometry is scoped to Settings because `.page-header` is a shared rule (Agents and Library use it): `.area-admin .page-header { padding: 16px 32px }` and `.area-admin .admin-form { padding: 0 32px 40px; max-width: 880px }`. Since leg 5b the tool list sits on the same 32px inset (`.settings-list`).
- **Section nav retired.** `areas/layout/section-nav.js` (`App.layout.renderSectionNav` / `updateActiveSection`, a `<button>` list with roving tabindex) lost its last caller and is deleted, together with its `index.html` tag (the cache-busting count goes 72 -> 71), its CSS (`.section-nav`, `.section-nav-header`, `.section-nav-item`) and its control-inventory row; the Design language page's surfaces section no longer draws a section-nav sample (the `navGroups` demo in "Library: lists and navigation" replaces it). `App.layout` no longer exists. The retired-selector guard keeps `section-nav-item` in `RETIRED_SELECTORS`.
- **Tests.** `tests/browser/test_flow_admin_settings_nav.py` (settings lane: the nav's groups, links, heading and mount; three groups without dev mode; geometry at 1440 and 1024 in Chromium and WebKit; `aria-current` across navigation, item routes and Back/Forward; every link opens its page under one page header and one `h1`; the General pages' blocks; the theme flip; the Models group's absence (sortie 02); the `settings/general` alias cold and in-app with its suffix dropped; the legacy hashes written directly; the rail's first-use href and `lastRoute`; a dirty tool editor plus a nav link asks once). `test_flow_keyboard.py`'s former roving-tabindex test is now `test_settings_nav_links_are_in_tab_order` and `test_settings_nav_enter_navigates_and_moves_aria_current`.

### Date and time page (M28 F2 L3)

`#settings/date-and-time`, second under General (rows in `settings-pages.js` and `router.js` `SECTIONS`; `GENERAL_SUBS` and `GENERAL_RENDERERS` in `admin.js`). `system-page.js` exports `App.admin.renderPage`; the page is `areas/admin/date-time-page.js` (`App.admin.renderDateTimePage`, DOM APIs only), rendered as one `div.date-time-page` root inside `renderPage`. That root is the page's mounted check (`root.isConnected`); there is no `activate` hook, so every Settings entry re-mounts and re-fetches `GET /api/time-settings` and `GET /api/time-zones`.

- **Nodes are built once and patched.** After the first successful load it builds the radio group (`time-zone-mode`, testids `date-time-mode`, `-system`, `-zone`), the zone option's slot (the "Selected:" line `date-time-chosen`, tied to the search input by `aria-describedby`, and an `App.ui.searchList`), the mismatch hint, the status and error lines and the preview. A response patches them in place: `radioGroup.setValue`, `textContent`, then `list.refresh()` with `scrollTop` kept and `setActive(<stored canonical row>)`. Focus never moves. A failed zone list shows an error line in the slot and the radio and preview still work.
- **View mode.** The page keeps a local `viewMode` (which radio is checked and whether the chooser shows), separate from the saved `mode`: "Choose a time zone" with nothing stored shows the chooser and saves nothing. It is re-seeded from the server only on page load and on a PUT's success or failure.
- **Saving and ordering.** One module-level `App.ui.util.latest()` (`gen`, M31 F4 L09; it replaced the hand-rolled `GEN` counter) is shared across mounts. Every PUT and page load takes a token (`gen.next()`, `isCurrent(token)` in the handlers) and only the latest response is applied; a response for a page that is no longer in the document updates only `App.time` (via `App.time.applySettings`), and never any DOM. A failed PUT shows the server `detail` in `date-time-error` and re-GETs under a new generation. Picking a zone row or an alias item PUTs `{mode: 'zone', zone: <its name>}`; "Use the system time zone" PUTs `{mode: 'system', zone: <stored or null>}`.
- **Canonical comparison.** `canon(name) = links[name] || name` (`links` from `/api/time-zones`). The hint (`role=note`, `.banner-info`) shows when the browser's canonical zone differs from `effective.canonical`. Its wording differs by the saved mode (system: "the machine running the-one reports ..."; zone: "agents are set to ..."), and the "Use {browser zone}" button (named by the same `placeOf` as the hint text, "Use Paris", HAT ledger 53) shows only when the reported name is in the list or `links`. Alias items (a non-blank query only) carry the id `alias:<name>`, and their row `data-zone` is the name that would be saved.
- **Active row and centring (M31 F4 L09).** The list is the inline `App.ui.searchList` (`inline`, `keepScroll`, `describedBy` the chosen line, `scrollActive: 'center'`); the chosen zone's row is marked by the variant's `selected: true` (a check in a fixed lead; the page's old `checkLead`/`date-time-lead-spacer` and all `ui-search-list*` overrides are gone from `admin.css`), and an unfocused list paints no active row (the variant's own rule). `syncActive` stays page code (the variant does not know the stored zone): it calls `setActive(id, {scroll})` and centres only once, the first time the list is shown or when the stored zone changed from outside the page's own picks (a reload or another tab; `centrePending`); a pick never scrolls the list and typing never calls it. B8: the "Selected:" line's id and offset sit in `span.date-time-chosen-id` in the list's mono font; a zone whose label is its id (`UTC`, `Japan`) is named once ("Selected: UTC · UTC+00:00", and an alias row reads "Same clock as …"; HAT ledger 54).
- **Library parts (M31 F4 L09).** The hint is `App.ui.banner({tone:'info', className:'date-time-mismatch', children:[text, Use button]})` and leads with city names (B9: `placeOf(zone)` = `cityOf(canon(zone))`, the raw id for a zone with no city such as `UTC` or `Etc/GMT+5`; two cities of the same name are not told apart; the raw ids are in the banner's `title`). Sections are `App.ui.section({layout:'stack'})`: "Time zone" holds the hint, the radio group (`slotIndent: 'label'`), the status and the error; "What agents see" is an `App.ui.facts` list (`mono` for the tool line; the prompt row holds two nodes, `date-time-preview-prompt` and `date-time-prompt-muted`). `date-time-status` is a persistent `role=status` div that holds one `App.ui.status` only while there is text, so an empty status costs one stack gap (B10); the save error is a `role=alert` status `date-time-error`; the load and zone-list errors are statuses too. DD8-13: a successful "Use {zone}" (the `focusChosen` flag, current token only) moves focus to `p.date-time-chosen` (`tabindex=-1`, visible ring) before `patchHint` hides the banner; a failed one leaves the button visible and focused.
- **Tests.** `tests/browser/test_flow_settings_date_time.py` (settings lane): system and chosen flows, search and alias, mismatch, 422, stale and torn-down responses (a second instance mounted into a scratch node), keyboard, clocks following. Server zone is pinned with `extra_env={"TZ": ...}`, the browser's with `credentialed_context(timezone_id=...)`.

### Focus after an in-place action (M25 F6 L1b)

An in-place Save, edit-mode Cancel, Test or a Create that turns into edit mode re-renders or disables the control a keyboard user just pressed, which drops focus to `<body>`. `App.focusAfter` (`static/js/focus-after.js`, loaded right after `nav.js`; not an `App.ui` factory) puts it back. `begin(control)` runs when the action **starts**, before anything disables the control, recording whether it was focused (`hadFocus`) and a sequence number (a newer `begin` supersedes). `restore(token, target)` runs **after the editor has mounted or patched its final state** and returns a boolean: it does nothing unless the token is non-null, had focus, is still the latest uncancelled token, and **focus is lost** (`activeElement` null, `<body>`, `<html>`, the token's own control, a disabled element or a detached one). Then it focuses `target` (an element, or a function resolved when the timer fires) in a `setTimeout(0)` (after `nav.js`'s `setTimeout(focusMain)` and after synchronous re-renders), skipping a disabled or detached target. That is the **never-steal rule**: a control the user moved to while the request ran keeps focus. `cancel()` (called from every teardown: `studio.js` `closeOpenItem`; `admin.js` `_discardEditor`, `_setCategory`, `_closeOpenItem`) invalidates every token and the deferred one. `defer(token, targetFn)` parks one pending restore for an **async mount** (agent create via `doSelect`, connector create and edit-mode Cancel via `renderConnectorsSection`, MCP create via `ready.then`, provider create via `mountProviderItem`, knowledge-base Save via `mountKnowledgeDetail`); the mount site calls `flush()` right after its final render.

Targets: Save and edit-mode Cancel and a Create go to the editor's `.editor-title` (the five studio titles gained `tabindex="-1"`; the provider and tool titles already had it; one shared `.editor-title:focus-visible` ring in `components.css`); a Test returns to its own button once re-enabled (called once from the handler's re-enable, or MCP `settle()`; the auto-run Refresh after an MCP create passes a null token so it never supersedes the create's parked restore); a failed Save returns to Save. **Excluded:** closing actions (create-mode Cancel, Delete, the breadcrumb close) keep DD8's nearest-surviving-row rule; **knowledge-base create** keeps focusing the folder-path input (`kbFocusFolderFor`, squawk 0101). The provider's old `keepFocus`/`hadFocus` path is gone (it read `activeElement` after Save had disabled itself); a provider create's `reveal` and `restore` stay exclusive. Tests: `tests/browser/test_ui_focus_after_contract.py` (`contracts` lane, Chromium + WebKit), `test_flow_agents_focus_after.py`, `test_flow_settings_focus_after.py`, `test_flow_studio_library_focus.py`.

**What a held save can steal.** An editor that re-renders on save (the agent editor's `renderEditor`) loses focus on a field *inside* the editor to the re-render itself, so `restore` then sends focus to the title, because focus was lost. An editor that patches in place (the provider editor) keeps the field's focus. The never-steal rule, and any test of it, must therefore target an element that survives the re-render: the agent pin (`test_agent_save_never_steals_focus_the_user_moved` in `test_flow_agents_focus_after.py`) moves focus to the Agents list filter (`agents-filter`), and the provider pin (`test_flow_provider_focus_after.py`, moved from the settings file by squawk 0189) uses the `#pf-name` field.

### Settings item pages (M25 F3 L5b)

Settings item pages keep their list visible (flight DD7): an open built-in tool renders its editor **below** its list, in the same scrolling page (`.admin-content`). Below, not beside: the Settings nav already takes the list column, and a third column would leave the editor 432px at 1024px (a DD14 checkpoint question).

**Category tab switch closes the editor (HAT step 3, M31 F4).** `tools-grid.js` takes `onBeforeCategoryChange(id, commit)` and passes it to `App.ui.tabs` as `onBeforeChange` (user switches only; a programmatic reveal and arrow-key focus moves do not use it). `tools-page.js::handleBeforeCategoryChange` commits at once when no editor is open or pending; otherwise it calls `App.router.go('#settings/tools', {opener: tab})` and commits the tab only if navigation happened, so the leave guard asks once and Stay leaves the tab, editor and edits untouched.

- **The page scaffold.** Each category render of Built-in tools (`renderToolsPanel`, via `renderContent`) builds the scaffold once (`mountScaffold`): the section 4 page header (`h1.page-title`), then `.settings-list`, then `.settings-item`. `scaffold = {category, listEl, itemEl}`; `scaffoldFor(category)` returns it only while that page is showing (`renderContent` resets it for every other page). **List renders target `.settings-list` only and editors mount in `.settings-item`** (`renderToolsEditor(itemEl, …)`, whose own `innerHTML` and the tools Cancel re-mount stay inside it), so a list refresh never touches the editor and opening an item never touches the list. The guards that suppressed list refresh while an editor was open (`toolFormMode` in `populateToolGrid`) are gone: the list refreshes with an editor open. Closing an item empties `.settings-item` (`clearItemSection`, called by `_closeOpenItem`): a root hydrate on the same page (`_setCategory`'s scaffold branch: the breadcrumb, the nav's current-page link, Back/Forward) closes the item IN PLACE with the list untouched, an item hydrate on the same page closes the open one and mounts the next there, and the App.nav `discard` (`_discardEditor`) empties it too, so a discarded editor never lingers under the list (`editorDomStale` is now set only when no item page is showing). `editorGen` still voids every in-flight editor resolver on a close. A flash is never tied to an editor: `App.ui.flash` appends to the page-level `div.app-flash-region` (M31 F6 L03a; the old per-editor container and its `rescueFlashes` move are gone), so a toast outlives the editor that raised it for its 5 s dwell. CSS (`admin.css`): `.settings-list` is `padding: 0 32px; max-width: 880px` and takes the page's remaining height only while `.settings-item` is empty (`:has(+ .settings-item:empty)`); an open item is `margin: 24px 32px 0; max-width: 816px` under a hairline, its header and sections on the list's inset.
- **The tools page controller (M31 F4 L02a).** The scaffold, the tools state (list cache, in-flight toggles, the open item, the pending hydrate name), the list/item/select/fetch/toggle code and the open/close click delegation live in `static/js/areas/admin/tools-page.js` (`App.admin.toolsPage.create(ctx)`, loaded after `tools-editor.js` and before `admin.js`; the shell-before cache rule in `tests/test_index_asset_cache_busting.py` pins the order). `admin.js` (about 350 lines) keeps the descriptor, the Settings nav, the content shell (DOM-built, no HTML sink), `GENERAL_RENDERERS`/`renderContent`, the Knowledge indexing bridge, `_refresh`, the `App.nav` dirty source and `_discardEditor`, `ROUTE_SECTION` and the shared part of `_setCategory`. The tools page reaches the area only through `ctx`: `getCategory()`, `setDirty(bool)`, `getGen()`/`bumpGen()` (one discard counter owned by the area), `setDomStale(bool)`, `contentEl()`, `content` (the click-delegation target), `serializeState()` (`selectTool` syncs the router with exactly `App.router.sync('admin', ctx.serializeState())`) and `pageLabel(category)`; `tests/test_settings_pages_static.py` fails if the file names an area closure variable or a bare `self`. The handle: `render()`, `reset()`, `scaffoldFor(category)`, `onCategoryChange()`, `hasItemOrPending()`, `isOpen()` (`!!toolFormMode`), `refresh()` (`fetchToolConfigs` then `selectTool`), `serialize()`, `parseSub(sub)` (also static, `App.admin.toolsPage.parseSub`, because `hydrateState` can run before `init`), `closeOpenItem()`, `tryConsumePending()`, `syncItemSelection()`, `setOpenIntent(bool)` and get/set accessors `toolFormMode`, `editingTool`, `pendingToolName`, `pendingToolFormMode`. The area keeps every `_*` seam tests read (`_toolFormMode`, `_editingTool`, `_pendingToolName`, `_pendingToolFormMode`, `_closeOpenItem`, `_tryConsumePendingTool`, `_syncItemSelection`, `_setOpenIntent`, `_setCategory`, `_syncNavSelection`, `_refresh`, `_discardEditor`, `_dirty`, `_currentCategory`) as delegates; the handle is `admin._toolsPage`.
- **Knowledge indexing page (M31 F4 L10).** `areas/admin/knowledge-settings-page.js` is DOM-built on the library: an `App.ui.editorShell` page variant (Cancel `knowledge-settings-cancel-btn` and Save in the header, the unsaved caption, the intro as the header subtitle, `errorRole: 'alert'`) over four `App.ui.section`s (Chunking, Monitoring, Embedding, Vector store; one rule each, from the title). Fields are `App.ui.field` + short `textInput`s (hints linked by `aria-describedby`; an error replaces the hint while it shows; the overlap is checked against `floor(size / 2)` even when the size is invalid); watching is `App.ui.toggle`; the interval is echoed in words (`humanSeconds`, blank when invalid); the read-only rows are `App.ui.facts` with humanised values. Save model: the baseline is the server's last `{chunking, monitoring}` response and the fields are never overwritten by one (typing during a PUT stays dirty); Save is busy through the shell while a PUT is out; the "Saved." status has one timer cleared by each attempt; a 422 maps onto its field through `App.api.fieldErrors` (an unnamed one onto `knowledge-settings-error`); focus returns through `App.focusAfter` (to Save, or the title when Save is disabled). The renderer returns `{destroy()}`, which `admin.js` calls on every re-render and in `_discardEditor` (late GET/PUT responses are ignored); a clean `_refresh` still re-mounts from a fresh GET and drops "Saved." on purpose. `onDirtyChange` fires on every edge (Cancel and a successful Save included). `App.ui.facts` aligns values in a subgrid (see the control catalogue). The `.page-header` geometry is the one components rule (16px 32px); the `.area-admin` copies, the admin switch rules, `.area-admin .field-error`, `.admin-system-flash*` and `.admin-kv-*` are gone (`admin-form`, `admin-system-section`, `admin-system-text` stay: the other Settings pages use them).
- **Settings close (M31 F4 L11).** `admin.css` now holds Settings rules only (nav host, content column, the page layout and typography classes, the Prompts, Knowledge indexing, Date and time and tools Test row parts, `.settings-list`/`.settings-item`); the catalogue's "Declared, not controls" section lists them and the kept `.admin-form`/`.admin-system-section`/`.admin-system-text` (page layout, not a control). The `.area-studio` halves of the legacy `.tool-enable-switch*` rules (deleted in M31 F5 L07) and of the string-built chip-list block (deleted in M31 F5 L06) moved to `studio.css` for Flight 5's MCP editor, with their `OWNERSHIP_ALLOWANCE` rows (`chip` 1, `chip-list-editor` 5, `chip-list-input` 1, `ui-chip-list` 5, `ui-chip-list-input` 1); the `.area-admin` halves had no user and were deleted with their rows. The open tool's header is found through the `tool-editor-header` testid (`editorShell` `testid`), not class queries, so `tools-page.js` has no ownership row. The item sits `padding-top: 4px` under its hairline in place of a `.settings-item .editor-header` padding rule. `.editor-header--flush` no longer needs `!important` (it is `.editor-header--flush:is(.editor-header, .page-header)` to beat the later padding rule on specificity; `test_inset_none_removes_horizontal_padding_under_area_admin` runs under both area ancestors); `.editor-section--flush` keeps `!important` against the one remaining `.area-studio .editor-section` rule, to be removed with it in Flight 5. The lane `settings-tools` holds `test_flow_admin_tools_*`, `test_flow_admin_tool_config_fields.py`, `test_flow_settings_items.py`, `test_flow_settings_focus_after.py` and `test_flow_settings_visible_fixes.py`; `settings` is the rest of `test_flow_admin_*`/`test_flow_settings_*`.
- **Providers are not here (sortie 02, leg 2).** The Settings Providers tiles (`providers-list.js`), their tile data, the provider-below-the-tiles item page, the `providers`/`agents` subscriptions and the provider half of the scaffold were deleted; providers live in Library (see "Providers section"). `#settings/providers…` is an unknown route.
- **Built-in tools: the item below the list.** The tabs and the tile cards stay (the flight's accepted variation). **The list (M31 F4 L06)** is `areas/admin/tools-grid.js`, `App.admin.toolsGrid.create(listEl, {onToggle})` -> `{render(toolConfigs, {selectedName, inFlight}), setSelection(name), setLoading(), setError(text, onRetry), destroy()}`: one `App.ui.tabs` (`idPrefix: 'tools'`, so `tools-tab-{cid}` / `tools-panel-{cid}`, testids `tab-{cid}`, `panelPadding: 'none'`) over one `App.ui.toolTile.grid` per category, built once and reconciled by tool name (`syncChildren`), so a toggle, a refetch or a selection change patches the tiles it concerns and never rebuilds the list. A tile is `App.ui.toolTile` (`tool-card`, `data-name`): the **tool name** is the item link (`a.tool-tile-link`, `href` from `App.router.href`), no category badge (A6), the description is the served `summary` (B1; the model-facing `description` only when a summary is missing), and the enable switch is an `App.ui.toggle` (`role=switch`, `tool-toggle`, named "Enable {name} for all agents", DD8-1). The tile is **not a click target** (the name is the link) and the **toggle never navigates**. The open tool's tile carries the selected wash (`ui-tool-tile--selected`, still `active`; under forced colours a `Highlight` border and outline) and `aria-current="page"` on its link. `loading` is a `tools-loading` status; a failed first load is a danger status `tools-load-error` ("Couldn't load the built-in tools." plus the server's reason, `role=alert`) with a Retry button `tools-load-retry` (DD8-2; a refetch that fails after the list loaded keeps the list). An empty category shows `App.ui.emptyState` "No tools.". **Category on open**: opening a tool (or a deep link to one) switches the tabs to its category ONCE (module-level `revealedName`; `activeCategory` is module-level too, so the chosen tab survives a re-render and leaving and re-entering Settings); an unknown name keeps the current tab. **A toggle patches its tile**: `render` calls `setDisabled(true)` for `inFlight` names and `setChecked` from the cache, a failed PUT reverts the cache and shows `App.ui.flash` with the server's reason (error, in `ctx.contentEl()`). A browser blurs a control that becomes disabled, so the grid remembers which tile's switch had focus when its PUT disabled it and gives focus back when that tile is enabled again (and nothing else took it); when the focused tool leaves the list focus goes to the next tile's switch in its panel, else the previous one's, else the panel's tab. `tools-page.js` fetches through `App.api.fetchJson` (the list with a `latest()` token), and `selectTool` treats `err.status === 404` as a dead id (clears, heals the URL to `#settings/tools`) and ANY other failure as a stranded one (DD8-3): it clears the pending name, syncs the route to `tools`, keeps the list usable and flashes the error, unless an editor is already open (a failed re-read leaves it standing). It drops a response a close or a newer open voided (`editorGen`). Breadcrumb "Settings / Built-in tools" (`#settings/tools`), no Back, the stable unsaved caption. No create: Cancel reverts in place; closing a tool is the breadcrumb, the nav's "Built-in tools" link or another tool's link. `App.tabs` and `js/tabs.js` are gone (the `test_ui_tabs_contract.py` parity tests against it were deleted with it; the library's own keyboard contract is stated explicitly in that file).
- **The tools editor (M31 F4 L07a-L07d).** `areas/admin/tools-editor.js`, `App.admin.renderToolsEditor(container, toolDetail, callbacks)` -> `{setEnabled(bool), destroy()}`, is built once per mount from the library, with no HTML-string sink and no ownership row. **Frame:** `App.ui.editorShell` (default variant, `inset: 'none'`, `titleLevel: 2`, `errorRole: 'alert'`): the breadcrumb "Settings / Built-in tools", the tool name as the title (the page header owns the one `h1`; the shell gives the Library editors' title size, B2), the category's display name as the subtitle (A13), `editor-unsaved`, Cancel (`cancel-tool-btn`) and Save (`save-tool-btn`). Below: a Prompt section (`App.ui.codeBlock`, A14: its own section, so the header does not reflow when the editor becomes dirty), a Configuration section (`layout: 'stack'`) with the Enabled switch (`App.ui.toggle`, `data-field="enabled"`, id `tef-enabled`, a static label "Enabled for all agents", DD8-4/A9) and one control per served property from `App.toolConfigFields.render(..., {mode: 'admin'})` (full-column fields, numbers short, B3; "No additional configuration." is a flush `App.ui.emptyState`, A12), then Test connection for `web_search` and `fetch_url`. **Value model (DD5):** `snapshot()` is `{enabled, config}` read from the Enabled switch and each handle's `value()` (never the DOM); dirty is `snapshot !== baseline`. **Save and Cancel patch in place:** nothing remounts. Save sends the snapshot (a `show_if`-hidden row is still sent), then re-baselines from the SERVER'S RESPONSE field by field: a control that still holds what was submitted follows the response (a secret becomes its mask, DD8-8); one the operator changed meanwhile keeps its value and stays dirty (DD8-6); a field the response omits is at its Config default; `baseline.enabled` is that response's `enabled`, and the Enabled control follows only if the operator has not touched it. Cancel writes the baseline into every handle, clears field and editor errors, re-applies `show_if` and clears the Test result when it changed a value. While a Save is in flight `editorShell.setBusy(true)` disables Save, Cancel and Test (the fields stay live); a Test in flight blocks Save (the Test reads the SAVED row; the Test input shows "Tests the saved settings." while the editor is dirty). **Identity (DD8-7):** every tool editor mounts into the same item section, so `tools-page.js` mounts through a `mountSlot` and passes `isCurrent()`; the editor writes shared state (flash, `onDirtyChange`, `onCancel`, `onSaveSuccess`) only while current. `onSaved(updated)` is separate and ALWAYS runs on a Save response: the list cache and the tiles follow the server (a list toggle still in flight for that tool keeps its optimistic `enabled`; its own response decides). **The Enabled race:** the list toggle's PUT and a Save's PUT can overlap; the server applies writes in the order its routes are continued (the later one wins), and each response sets `baseline.enabled` from itself, so the editor's switch and the tile follow the final server value whichever response arrives first (test: `test_enabled_follows_the_server_when_a_save_and_a_list_toggle_race`, oracle a final `GET`); arrival order at the browser is the caveat, which is why the rule is "every response sets the baseline". **Errors (DD3, DD8-9):** a 422 goes through `App.api.fieldErrors(err, fieldNames)`: a `loc` that names a rendered field puts the humanised message on that field (`aria-invalid`, cleared when the field changes), the rest (a model-level error, a field the form hides) goes to `editorShell.setError`; any other failure puts `err.message` there. One `role=alert` region receives text per error and a field's message is never repeated at editor level. The `.editor-error-banner`, the row highlight and both `humanizeValidationMsg` copies are deleted. **Test (A15, A16):** `App.ui.field` + `textInput` (`data-test-param`) with `test-tool-btn` on its line, the result a polite live region `test-result` (`data-state`) holding one `App.ui.status` with a FIXED headline chosen from the route's `outcome` ("Returned a result ({N} ms)", "Blocked by this tool's settings ({N} ms)", "Request failed"); `POST /api/tools/configs/{name}/test` returns `{output, elapsed_ms, outcome}` with `outcome` `ok` | `blocked` | `failed` computed server-side (`_test_outcome`: `[error] [constraint]` is blocked, other `[error]` is failed), so the client never parses the model-facing string. The raw output (the first 2 KB, "…" at the cut; HTML entities in a search result come from the tool and are shown as the agent sees them) is a code block (`tool-test-output`) in a collapsed "Technical details" disclosure (`tool-test-details`); an empty output has none. A non-200 reply (400/404/500, JSON or not) reads "Request failed" with the reason under the details. The Test's busy flag and result are per mount (a stale response writes nothing). **Remount side-effect inventory:** `show_if` visibility, field errors, the editor error and the masked secrets are re-run by Cancel; the Test result is kept by Save and cleared by a Cancel that changed a value; the tile and the leave guard follow through the identity-gated callbacks; focus goes to the title after Save and Cancel (the control on failure); the unsaved caption is the shell's stable node. (`tools-form-helpers.js`, which kept the string-built chip list for the MCP editor, was deleted in M31 F5 L06.)
- **A toggle while a tool editor is open** (design review issue 2). `handleToolToggle` re-renders the list only (before and after the PUT). When the toggled tool is the open one, the PUT's success updates the editor's Enabled control and its saved snapshot IN PLACE through `onMount(api).setEnabled(value)` (the tools editor calls `callbacks.onMount({setEnabled})` after every mount, Cancel's re-mount included): no re-mount, so an unsaved edit elsewhere in the form is kept and the saved enablement reads clean; if the user had already changed the Enabled control, their value stays and dirtiness is recomputed against the new snapshot. The editor's own Save now also refreshes the list (the tile's toggle shows the saved enablement) and makes Cancel re-mount from what was just saved (before, Cancel after a Save reverted to the pre-save values).
- **Opening, closing and focus.** `hydrateState` marks every item route as an intent (`_setOpenIntent`: a name link, Back/Forward, a deep link or boot); the item, once mounted, scrolls its editor header into view (`block: 'nearest'`) and moves focus to its title (`h2.editor-title`, `tabindex="-1"` in both string-built headers; an `h2` since the flight-end review, so the page header's `h1` stays the page's only one), one task later so it lands after `nav.js`'s Continue focus on `<main>` (`revealItem`). Store notifications, `_refresh` re-reads and save-create never reveal. One delegated `click` listener on the admin content (ahead of App.nav's document-level interceptor) routes the item header's breadcrumb link as an intent `go(href, {opener: link})` and, once it navigated, focuses the closed item's row link (a tool's name link; after a `~new` form, which has no row of its own, Built-in tools have no create form, and a closed tool always has its own tile) (DD8); and a click on the list link of the item ALREADY open (its row, its tool name) is not a navigation: no dialog, no re-mount, the item is just revealed.
- **Tests.** `tests/browser/test_flow_settings_items.py` (settings lane, `test_flow_settings_*`): it is tools-only since sortie 02 (the provider scenarios moved to `test_flow_provider_items.py` / `test_flow_provider_library.py` on the Library routes): the tool editor below the list, toggle isolation (another tool and the open tool), the deep-link category, the tool breadcrumb close, the unsaved caption for both editors, focus discipline under store notifications and `_refresh`, the Tab order, and, under Chromium forced-colours emulation, the open tool's `Highlight` border and outline. The settings-lane and shared files were migrated off the tiles and the two Back controls (see the flight log); the retired-selector guard has exempted no file since (its allow-list was removed in M25 F3 L6).

- **Flight 6 leg 1a (alignment).** (DD3) Both tile kinds put the name and the badge in a `.tile-title-row` flex row (`tools-grid.js` inside its existing HTML string; the providers tiles it also covered were retired in sortie 02); the badge is in the flow, so it cannot overlap the name and the old `calc(100% - 128px)` reserve is gone. (DD4) `create_provider` and `create_chat_session` catch `ValidationError` and raise 422 with `e.errors(include_context=False, include_input=False)`; `performSave` uses `App.api.fetchJson`. (DD10) The provider editor's Models section is "Available models" with `provider-models-help` ("Each agent chooses one of these in its Model section." plus an Agents link), its `.admin-pf-model-item` rows are plain information (`cursor: default`). (DD11) `studio.js` builds an `h1[data-testid=root-heading]` (visible since leg 4c, the start page's title, class `start-page-title`) at an Agents root (synced by `updateContentVisibility`) and a Library root (beside `renderLibraryEmpty`'s empty state); absent when an editor is open, where `.editor-title` is the only `h1`.
### Client data store (`App.store`)

`static/js/store.js` (M25 F2 L7, DD8; Library collections M25 F3 L2, DD9; `sessions` and
`prime` M25 F4 L1, DD4), loaded right after `api.js`. The shared client data layer for six
**list-shaped** collections:

| Collection | Endpoint | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `agents` | `GET /api/agents` | list shape (no `kb_tool_names`) |
| `providers` | `GET /api/providers` | |
| `knowledge-bases` | `GET /api/knowledge-bases` | |
| `mcp-sources` | `GET /api/mcp-sources` | the PLAIN list: self-configuration surface rows are hidden. The agent editor's `?include_agent_surfaces=1` variant (`agent-data.js::fetchMcpSources`) is detail-shaped and stays a direct fetch |
| `connectors` | `GET /api/connectors` | `config` carries `default_agent` and the optional `channel_map` |
| `sessions` | `GET /api/chat/sessions` | rows `{id, agent_name, title, created_at, updated_at, platform}` (`platform` null for a UI session; `_session_summary`). The chat area PRIMES it from every WebSocket `session_list` reply, mapped to this shape, so while Chats is active the WebSocket is its source; off Chats, `get` fetches over REST |

Tools, provider model caps and every by-id fetch are not in it. `peek`/`get` of any other
name throws.

- `get(name, {force}) -> Promise<copy>`. Joins an in-flight request; a loaded, fresh value is
  served from cache with no request; `force` joins an in-flight request, else refetches.
  **Rejects on failure** with the `App.api.fetchJson` error (so `loadAll`'s `Promise.all`
  still rejects and the squawk-0014 load-error banner shows); the failure is never cached.
- `peek(name) -> {value, loaded, ok, error}`: `value` is a copy (or `null` before the first
  load); after a failure it keeps the **last good value** with `ok:false`; the next `get` retries.
- `invalidate(name)` bumps a generation: a request started earlier is not joined by later
  `get`s and **does not populate the cache**, so post-mutation reads never see pre-mutation data.
- `prime(name, rows)` (M25 F4 L1): sets the value to a copy of `rows` pushed from elsewhere,
  with `loaded`, `ok` and `fresh` all true; bumps the generation and clears the in-flight
  request (as `invalidate` does), so a `get` started earlier resolves to its own caller with its
  own data but neither overwrites the primed value nor notifies, and a later `get` is served
  from the primed cache with no request; then notifies subscribers with a copy. An unknown name
  throws. Pinned by `tests/browser/test_store.py` (the `prime` tests) and
  `tests/test_store_static.py::test_store_prime_bumps_the_generation_and_is_exported`.
- `subscribe(name, fn) -> unsubscribe`: `fn(copy)` after every successful load or prime, including the first.
- Every returned value is a JSON copy; mutating it never reaches the cache. The store holds
  **list-shaped** agents; Studio's detail-shaped objects (`kb_tool_names`, from
  `GET /api/agents/{name}`) stay on Studio's own `self._agents` copy and are never written back.

**Freshness bound.** REST is the source of truth, `invalidate` the rebuild trigger.
Self-configuration writes also happen server-side with no WebSocket notice, so destination
activation uses `get(…, {force:true})` (Chats `refreshAgentsAndSetup`, Studio `_refresh` /
boot `loadAll(true)`; Settings reads no collection at all since sortie 02). Chats reads agents + providers **once** per
activation and keeps its prior setup state on failure (errors are swallowed).

**The Library reads (M25 F3 L2; the Library list's fetch policy, L4a).** Since leg 4a one
Library list pane shows all three collections, so the per-section grid fetches
(`fetchKnowledgeBases_` / `fetchMcpSources_` / `fetchConnectors_`, the knowledge-base rename's
manual cache patch and `_refresh`'s MCP-only refetch) are gone. The policy (DD9's freshness
bound, pinned by `tests/test_store_static.py::test_library_fetch_sites_read_through_the_store`):
- **Pane creation** (entering Library from Chats or Agents, a cold boot onto a Library route):
  `loadLibrary(true)` (`studio/library-pane.js`, handle `load`) force-gets `knowledge-bases`, `mcp-sources` and `connectors`, and plain-gets
  `agents` (reusing the cache Agents loaded). A first activation joins `loadAll`'s in-flight
  knowledge-base request.
- **Studio `activate` while on a Library section** (the pane survives a trip to Chats or
  Settings): `_refresh` calls `libraryPane.load(true)` (the pane's `loadLibrary`); the hydrate that follows joins it.
- **A move between Library sections fetches nothing** (the pane and its data stay).
- **After a mutation** the editor invalidates (the map below), then the studio callback calls
  `reloadLibrary(name)` (`library-pane.js`, handle `reload`; the glue modules alias it locally): a plain `get` (it refetches after `invalidate`) whose result is also
  rendered, because `invalidate` notifies no subscriber. Every create, save, rename and delete
  callback does this; the MCP editor's `onListStale` callback (fired after its connect-success
  and Reset-connection invalidations) wires the tool count to it too.
- **The pending-item rule** (MCP and connectors, one rule): a section root fetches nothing; a
  pending id on a FRESH hydrate force-gets (joining the pane's or activate's get), consumes, then
  heals a dead id to the root; on an intent it consumes from the section cache, else after a
  plain `get` or on the next notification, and never heals on non-fresh data.
- `fetchKnowledgeBases(force)` (`loadAll`) forwards `loadAll`'s own `force`;
  `refreshKnowledgeBasesCache` (the agent-edit mount) forces, keeping DD-15's refetch on every
  editor mount; its `.catch` keeps the prior cache and sets `_knowledgeBasesLoaded = false`.
- `fetchJson` rejects on any non-2xx: a collection that never loaded shows its group's
  "Couldn't load {group}." line; a failed refresh with a last-good value keeps the stale rows.

Request counts (Playwright, `tests/browser/test_store_library.py::test_library_list_request_counts`,
16 steps, (providers, knowledge-bases, mcp-sources plain, connectors) per step; the providers column
joined in sortie 02): every pane creation and every studio activate on a Library route is exactly
(1, 1, 1, 1); every move between Library sections is (0, 0, 0, 0). (Leg 2's per-section loads -- a section's own list on its first move -- are gone.) Settings activation fetches neither `/api/agents` nor `/api/providers` (`tests/browser/test_store.py`, pinned `(0, 0)`): with the Providers page gone, the admin area has no store subscription of its own for them.

**Invalidation map** (every successful mutation calls `App.store.invalidate`, on the success
