- **Links and canonical names (M28 F2 L1, DD6).** `zone_links()` (separately cached; `_parse_tzdata_zi`'s arity is unchanged) maps each `L <target> <alias>` alias to its canonical zone with chains resolved, so every value is in `canonical_zones()` (`Asia/Calcutta` to `Asia/Kolkata`, `UTC` to `Etc/UTC`, `US/Pacific` to `America/Los_Angeles`). `canonical_name(key)` is the key for a canonical name, `links[key]` for an alias, else `None`. `effective.canonical` is `canonical_name(effective.zone)` (`null` for an unnamed system zone). `zone_rows` also drops the no-slash canonical names (`CST6CDT`, `EST5EDT`, `MST7MDT`, `PST8PDT`; they still validate through `zone_names()`).
- **Packaging.** `tzdata>=2025.2,<2026.5` and `tzlocal>=5.3` are base dependencies; the Windows spec collects tzdata's data files and the submodules of both. The `time zones` doctor gate is the frozen-build judge.

## `App.time` (M28 F2)

`static/js/time.js` is the one place the browser reads the operator's time zone, and the only module allowed to call the `Date` getters or locale formatters (`tests/test_time_getters_static.py` greps `static/js/**/*.js` for `getHours|getMinutes|getDay(|getDate(|getMonth(|getFullYear(|toLocale` and fails on any hit elsewhere; other code uses `App.time.parts` and `dayNumber`).

- **API.** `setZone(key | null[, fallbackOffsetMinutes])` (when the engine's `Intl` throws `RangeError` for an IANA key and a fallback offset is given, the zone becomes that fixed-offset key, so the app still agrees with the server; with none it falls back to the browser zone as before — squawk 0183), `zone()`, `parts(date)` returning integer `{year, month (0-11), day, weekday (0-6, Sunday 0), hour (0-23), minute}`, `formatClock(date)` (`h:mm AM/PM`), `dayNumber(date)` (`Date.UTC(y, m, d) / 86400000`, an integer, so a day difference is DST-proof), `onChange(fn[, ownerEl])` (returns an unsubscribe; with an `ownerEl` the listener is also dropped once that element is no longer connected, pruned on every subscribe and every zone change, so a view with no teardown hook cannot accumulate listeners — squawk 0176; `_listenerCount()` is a test-only read) `zoneFromSettings(s)` and `applySettings(s)` (`setZone(zoneFromSettings(s), offset minutes)`; what the boot and the Date and time page call).
- **Zone kinds.** `null` is browser-local (the Date getters). An IANA key builds one cached `Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {timeZone, hourCycle: 'h23', ...})` read through `formatToParts`. An offset key `+HH:MM` is hand-built with no `Intl` (`new Date(t + offsetMs)` through the UTC getters), so it is engine-independent. An invalid key (`RangeError`) falls back to local with one `console.warn` and `zone()` returns `null`. `onChange` listeners fire once after the effective key changes, never on a same-key `setZone`.
- **`formatDate(d)`** (sortie 03) is "Oct 12, 2026": the calendar date in the operator zone from `parts`, locale-neutral like `formatClock`; the provider editor's token expiry line uses it. `tests/browser/test_ui_time_contract.py::test_format_date_is_zone_aware_across_the_date_line` reads one UTC instant near midnight in three zones.
- **`zoneFromSettings`** deliberately prefers `effective.canonical` over `effective.zone`, so `App.time.zone()` can differ from a stored alias; otherwise `effective.zone`, otherwise the fixed-offset key built from the additive `effective.offset_minutes` (int, e.g. `330`, `-420`, computed server-side by `time_zones.offset_minutes(now, eff)` from the same `utcnow()` instant; a response from an older server without it falls back to parsing the `offset` label `UTC+05:30`), otherwise `null`. A malformed body gives `null`, never a throw.
- **Boot** (`app.js`, `DOMContentLoaded`): `GET /api/time-settings` runs in parallel with `/api/config`. The settings chain never rejects (`r.ok` check plus its own catch; a 401 or failure keeps local time). The router starts when `/api/config` has settled and either the settings response has arrived or 2 seconds have passed (`Promise.all([config, Promise.race([settings, 2s timer])])`, the timer cleared when settings win); a response arriving later still calls `setZone`, which re-renders the subscribers.
- **Callers.** `chats-list.js` (recency groups by `dayNumber(now) - dayNumber(d)`, weekday and `Mon D` labels from `parts`), `transcript.js` (the attribution's time span carries `data-ts="<iso>"`; `App.ui.message.relabel`) and `rules-notes-tab.js::formatWhen`. Subscribers: the chat area subscribes once (never released; it re-renders the Chats list and re-labels every `.ui-message-attribution-ts[data-ts]` in the log in place through `App.ui.message.relabel`); each Rules and notes notes-section build subscribes and unsubscribes itself when its list is no longer connected (the tab has no teardown hook). The Knowledge "Last checked …" and "Indexed …" lines (`knowledge-folders.js`, through `formatDate` + `formatClock`, the precise value in `title`/`data-indexed-at`) subscribe once per folders mount with the Folders section as owner and unsubscribe in `destroy`; a zone change re-patches them in place.
- **Testing rule.** Never `page.clock.install()` (it breaks `_nav.wait_settled`); use `page.clock.set_fixed_time` before `goto`. A browser test asserting clock, weekday or day-group labels pins the operator zone to the browser's zone (`PUT /api/time-settings` with `Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone`) so both agree by construction, or sets `App.time.setZone(null)` after `wait_settled` for a pure formatter check (never right after boot: it races the boot's own late `setZone`).

## Prompt evals (Mission 30)

`evals/` (repo root, run as `python -m evals`, `make eval`) measures how the platform's prompt text behaves on real models. It is a developer tool: it imports `the_one.*` (an AST test in `tests/test_evals_outcomes.py` pins that nothing under `the_one/` imports it) and drives the real `AgentFactory`, `build_system_prompt`/`compose_system_prompt` and `Agent.step` in a scratch data directory and home. It reads the prompt assembly (`platform_prompt.render_platform`/`render_tool_free`, `_build_degraded_messages()`, the rules and notes layers, the date line) and the starting tool schemas to measure the platform text by part; it changes none of them. Anyone editing prompt text or a tool description should run `make eval ARGS="measure-prompt"` and compare against the committed baseline in `evals/baselines/`. Usage, formats and the gate rules are in `evals/README.md`; the project-level summary is in CLAUDE.md "Prompt evals".

- **Layout options (M31 F6 L03b, DD6, DD7, DD13).** Twelve area-CSS restyles of library controls and the picker's footer class became library options, so `chat.css` and `studio.css` hold no ownership-allowance row (the allowance fell from 21 rows / 30 uses to 8 rows / 14 uses). `searchList({variant: 'chooser'})` (root `.ui-search-list--chooser`: zero gap, the `0 20px 12px` field band at the body-lg size, the list under a hairline padded `0 12px 12px`, the empty state under the same hairline; no area ancestor, so it works in the picker's `<dialog>`); the old picker rule's `height: 40px` never applied (`.field-input` is `flex: 1` in a column group), so the variant keeps the real 34px. A9: the meta ellipsizes first (`flex: 0 1000 auto`, 64px floor, right-aligned; text column `flex: 1 1 auto`, description `contain: inline-size`). `drawer({reserveHeaderEnd: true})` (`ui-drawer--reserve-end`, overlay only, 56px). `modal({footerEnd})`. `textInput({minWidth})`. Base rules: `.ui-list-pane { flex: 1 1 0 }`, `.badge { flex-shrink: 0 }`, `.tabpanel { padding: 16px 20px 80px }`, and the section default flip (`.editor-section { padding: 12px 20px }`, `--flush` zeroes the sides with no `!important`; every section outside `.area-studio` already passed `inset: 'none'`). Contracts in `test_ui_search_list_contract.py`, `test_ui_drawer_contract.py`, `test_ui_modal_contract.py`, `test_ui_code_facts_contract.py`, `test_ui_editor_contract.py`, `test_ui_library_contract.py`, `test_ui_tabs_contract.py`, `test_ui_feedback_contract.py`.
